# TalentNext — DESIGN.md

**The baseline for all four portals.** Read this in full before any UI change on any of them.
Every value below is copied from the layer that states it, with the `file:line`, so it can be
re-derived and never retyped from memory. If a rule here and a reference screenshot disagree,
the rule wins unless Maryam says otherwise. **A new rule she gives is written HERE first, as one
line, in the same session — then implemented.** The argument behind each line is in
`docs/HISTORY.md` under the same § number; the primary source is the comment above the rule in
its `hifi/build/` layer.

## 1. The four portals

| Portal | File | On the design system | Rebuild |
|---|---|---|---|
| Candidate | `hifi/` (compiled from `hifi/build/`) | source of it | `cd hifi/build && python3 build.py` |
| Cohort Leader | `hifi/` — same renderer, `S.portal='leader'`, `lead*.js` | source of it | same |
| Talent Agent | `tn-agent-portal.html`, hand-written | yes — links `design-system/talentnext-ds.css` + `.js` | `cd design-system && python3 build-ds.py` |
| Super Admin | `tn-admin-portal.html`, hand-written | yes — links `design-system/talentnext-ds.css` + `.js` | `cd design-system && python3 build-ds.py` |

Collisions: "candidate portal" = `hifi/` unless "wireframe"/"early prototype" is said; "agent"
in `hifi/` is somebody the candidate books, in `tn-agent-portal.html` it is the signed-in user.

**Type** — `63-typography.css:140-236`; face `11-type.css:294` (**Plus Jakarta Sans**)

| Role | size / lh | Role | size / lh |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--t-display` | 34 / 40 — hero NUMERALS only | `--t-body` / `--t-compact` | 13.5 / 22 · 13.5 / 19 |
| `--t-h1` (≥900: 28) | 24 / 30 | `--t-label` · `--t-desc` | 12.5 / 17 · **13.5 / auto** |
| `--t-h2` | 20 / 26 | `--t-eyebrow` / `--t-caption` | 11.5 / 16 |
| `--t-h3` | 17 / 23 | `--t-sec` | **16 / 22 — every section heading, every width, every ground** |
| `--t-h4` | 14 / 19 | `--t-sec-lg` | 18 / 24 — one reader, `.dc-t` |

Weights: `--t-w-book` **400**, `--t-w-strong` **500**. Nothing else is loaded for a role.
§63 is the last layer: no layer after it may set a size, a weight, a `text-transform` or a text
colour. Take a role; if none fits, state the exception inside §63 §7.

**Space** — `01-foundation.css:389-390, 414, 435, 438`; `10-lattice.css:46`

`--s01..--s12` = 2 / 4 / 8 / 12 / 16 / 24 / 32 / 40 / 48 / 64 / 80 / 96. Section padding `--s06`
top and bottom (**32 between sections** as of 28 Sep 2026 — base `.sec` padding is `--s05`, §10/§138; a **20** gap ONLY where the next section is a bare blocks/cards row with no heading or text, §101). `--sec-h-gap` 12 (heading → its content). `--sec-desc-gap` 8
(heading → its own description). `--radius` **0px**. `--label-col` 184px (the desktop label column).
`--pad-x` is THE page side gutter (`10-lattice.css:383`, §15): every section, page header, list row and cell resolves to it, so the content column has one spine. The ladder is **24 below 1200, 32 at ≥1200** — the mobile/tablet value was raised **16 → 24** (Maryam 4 Oct 2026: "set the page left and right margins on mobile app to 24px ... the whole mobile design"), so a phone page sits 24 off each wall, not 16. One token, all four portals via the DS; the NIL microsite's own mobile wrap was moved to 24 to match (`30-nil.css`).

## 3. Standing rules — the checklist

Type and case
- Two weights, 400 and 500. Never 600+. "No bold" means Medium. The ONE stated exception is the head band label "Summary by Tal" at 600 (§63 §10, off Figma 871:6425). (§63, memory 4 Sep)
- Nothing is set in capitals except the card wordmarks. Words go in the markup in sentence case. (§63 §2)
- Buttons are Title Case, every word, by `text-transform:capitalize` on `.btn` — not by editing strings. (§63 §4b)
- Section heading is `--t-sec` 16px everywhere; `aiHead`'s title shares the token. (§63 §8b)
- A FIGURE-CARD TITLE IS BLACK, not the grey eyebrow: `.stats .l` / `.facts .l` / `.pf-facts .pf-l` take `--text-primary` (keeping the eyebrow size/weight); the sub-line `.d` under it stays `--text-secondary`. On a dark card the §6a ink rule still wins (`--on-dark`). All portals via the DS. (§63, Maryam 9 Sep 2026: "the four card titles should be black always ... revise the 4 cards structure everywhere")
- Display size is hero numerals only; a level NAME is h2. (§63)
- A DESCRIPTION (`--t-desc`, every `.t-desc` / `.u-desc` and the assignments in §63 §3) is **13.5px with an AUTO line height** (`line-height:normal`, the face's own leading), in `--text-secondary`. It no longer shares the label's 12.5 / 17; a description is read, a label is scanned. The one role in the scale without a stated leading — do not give it a px value. (§63 §1, Maryam 8 Sep 2026, was 12.5 / 17)
- A DESCRIPTION IS `--text-secondary` **#828282 EVERYWHERE, and it reads the token — never a hardcoded grey** (Maryam 9 Sep 2026: "i want the desc color to be #828282, i want it everywhere"). This is a global rule, not a per-screen fix: when it comes up, SWEEP every layer for description/supporting greys set as a hex and route them to `var(--text-secondary)`. Stragglers cleaned so far: `.qa-b > span` (#717171, quick-action descriptions), `.jrn-i` upcoming journey steps (#515151), its onboarding icon `.ob-jr-ic`, `.rec-f` (#414141). The floor tier `--text-helper` #666563 (timestamps, legal, chart axes, captions) and state colours (the journey `.done` green / `.on` accent, the star) are NOT descriptions and stay. (§63, all portals via the DS)

Colour and line
- One hairline for every stroke/border/outline, `--rule` **#efefef** (Maryam 7 Sep 2026). Panels `--surface-2`, page margin `--surface-3`. Control edges (input/select/checkbox) keep the darker `--border-subtle-02`/`--border-strong-01` — a field must stay findable. (§10, §12, §18)
- Never hardcode a hex; read the token. State colours are a SET and do not follow the accent: the journey's green/orange/grey, the rating star. (§63 §14, re-hue notes)
- The accent as ink is `--accent-text`; `--accent` on words is a stated exception (the selected rail label, `.jrn-pill`). (§63 §41)
- An UNSELECTED side-nav module (icon AND name) is `#828282` grey — the rail de-emphasises everything you are not on; the SELECTED module is `--accent` (icon, label and the 3px bar). Set on `.sn-item` in §01/§10 and `.sn-item.sn-sub` in §63; the `.on` state keeps `--accent`. All portals via the DS. (Maryam 7 Sep 2026, was #dfdfdf → #b1b1b1 → #828282)
- A field's line is `--rule` at rest and `#FF3733` at 2px on focus. (§12)
- The side-nav divider is ONE full-height line, drawn once by `.app::after` (≥900), never by the app-bar cell AND the drawer both — two stacked 1px borders read as a double rule on fractional-DPR screens. `.nav-t` and `.sidenav` carry no `border-right`; the line sits at `--rail-w-nav` collapsed / `--drawer-w` open and slides with the rail. It is SUPPRESSED (`content:none`) on the full-bleed scenes that have no rail — account creation (`.app:has(> .auth-card)`, which also covers the onboarding split step), the Tal onboarding intro (`.ob-open`) and the Next-in-Leadership run-up (`.nil`) — else it draws a stray line down an empty page. (§14 §1, §34, Maryam 7 Sep 2026; scene exception 9 Sep 2026)
- All cards and containers have a white fill; grey only for intentional tracks and hover. (memory, admin)
- The cohort leader dashboard's "Your upcoming calls" cards (`.lcal`) other than the black one (`.lcal-next`): a `--surface-2` **#fcfcfc** grey fill inside a `--rule` **#efefef** 1px border (Maryam 9 Sep 2026, iterated the same day: no-border+#efefef, then #fcfcfc fill, then #efefef border). The black cell keeps `border-color:transparent` so all three share the grid track's 1px height (§113.3). Both read the token, never the hex — a deliberate exception to the "cards are a white fill" rule, by instruction. The card MARKUP: the "Cohort N call" title leads the header row top-left (the cohort-number `.gcard-art` badge is gone), the date is top-right, and the sub-line is the track + week only (the "N candidates" count is removed). The section has no description. (§113/lead.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- THE BLACK "Your upcoming calls" CARD (`.lcal-next`, leader dashboard) IS A ROW, NOT THE LIGHT CELLS' COLUMN (Maryam 28 Sep 2026, four asks on one card): (1) the scheduled time sits in a SQUARE BLOCK on the LEFT — `.lcal-slot`, a WHITE-5% wash (`color-mix(--on-dark 5%, transparent)`, Maryam 28 Sep 2026, was `--accent`), day over time centred both axes, `align-self:stretch` + `aspect-ratio:1` so it is square off the card's own height (no re-derived number, trap 20); it BLEEDS `margin:-1px 0 -1px -1px` over §113.3's transparent card border so it sits flush to the card's top/left/bottom (else the dark ground shows through that 1px as a stroke); the two lines are solid `--on-dark` white (the day via §63 §20c). (2) The header's right slot is a LIVE COUNTDOWN to the call (`.lcal-timer`, `data-calltill`=`joinClock(k.when)` epoch, ticked by `callTimerArm`'s 1s clock, "Live now" once reached) where the date used to be. (3) The member count (`${k.seats} candidates`) sits under the title (`.lcal-seats`, `--on-dark-2` via §63 §6a). (4) The Join is the ACCENT primary, DISABLED (Maryam 28 Sep 2026: "the button is in accent color state but disabled since it is not clickable" — reversing the same day's white-fill and §81's grey wash): no `.lcal`-specific button rule, it follows the global gated Join (see the §81-reversed rule below). `joinLive`/`joinArm` still gate the button (disabled while the call is >5 min out). The LIGHT cells (`.lcal` non-next) are unchanged. hifi leader only. Refinements (Maryam 28 Sep 2026): the RED BLOCK BLEEDS to the card's top/left/bottom edges — the card drops its `--s05` padding + gap (`padding:0;gap:0`) and `.lcal-body` carries it, so the block is flush on three sides and the body's left padding is the one gap to the text; the block's TEXT IS SOLID WHITE (the "Today" `.t-desc` lifted from `--on-dark-2` to `--on-dark` in §63 §20c, 0,4,0) with a CALENDAR mark above it (`.lcal-slot-ic`, `I.calendar`, a stroke glyph on the slot's white ink); the COUNT ROW leads with TWO overlapped 22px roster photos (`.lcal-faces`, `avatar({i,img},22)` off the cohort's first two `members`, each disc a card-ground ring, the second `-8px`); and the TIMER carries a leading clock mark (`.lcal-timerbox` + `I.time`) and reads HOURS:MINUTES only (`callTimerFmt` → HH:MM, still ticked 1s). (§113/§63/lead.js/views.js, Maryam 28 Sep 2026) — TWO REFINEMENTS (Maryam 30 Sep 2026): the DATE/TIME BLOCK (`.lcal-slot`) is HIDDEN (`display:none` at the foot of its §113 rule, styling + markup kept so restoring is one line); with it gone the body's left inset lifts from `--s05` to `--s07` so the text sits an even 32 from all four card walls. And the "+N" overflow disc (`.lcal-face-more`) drops its card-ground WHITE fill for a SOLID DARK GREY (`color-mix(--on-dark 14%, --gray-100)` — opaque, 14% white into the card's #111, NOT a low-opacity wash, §113) with WHITE ink (`--on-dark`, §63 §20d, was `--accent-text`) so it reads on the grey — "the white +8 circle should be grey ... +8 text in white", then "solid dark grey not low opacity grey". (§113/§63/lead.js, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
- The cohort leader dashboard scrolls plainly — the sticky figure-band "position indicator" is removed (Maryam 9 Sep 2026: "hide the fix tabs interaction, go with the generic scroll just like other pages"). The four figures are plain static `.stat` cells (not `data-jump` buttons), the `.lead-bar`/`.lead-tabs` sticky tab strip is gone, and `leadStick` + its scroll/resize/click listeners are removed. The four sections keep their ids for the counts. (§113/lead.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- `gcard(kind,tag,title,sub,go,art,at)` draws NO left icon unless a real cover (`art.src`) is passed — the old `.cardrow-ic` `GC_IC[kind]` fallback mark is gone (Maryam 9 Sep 2026: "remove the left side cohort icons"). Its one live caller, the leader dashboard's cohort rows, passes `null` art, so those rows are text + arrow. (views.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- The leader's black COHORT CALL CARD (`leadCallCard`, on the Sessions and Cohort pages): header reads "Upcoming Cohort Session" (not "Cohort N call"); `crow` draws NO left mark (`leadCall`'s `who` has no `i`, and `crow` omits `.crow-ph` when a record has neither initials nor photo); the action is `crow`'s gated primary Join — the disabled ACCENT "Join call" (accent-disabled since 28 Sep 2026, was grey) while the call is >1 min away — not "Generate the brief". (§113/lead.js/views.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- A CANDIDATE-TURNED-COHORT-LEADER LEADS ONE COHORT AT A TIME; a MODERATOR (a cohort leader an admin added, `u.addedBy` set) may lead multiple (Maryam 14 Sep 2026). On the hifi cohort leader portal (the persona Priya is a candidate-leader) this is "one running cohort, keep previous led cohorts": `LEAD_COHORTS` holds only the ONE active cohort (Cohort 41, `status:'active'`), so every current-work surface (identity line, dashboard figures, attention queue, calls diary, "Your cohorts", chat) reads as one cohort automatically; completed/past led cohorts live in `LEAD_PAST` (Cohort 33, `status:'completed'`), reachable via `lcoOf`/`LEAD_ALL()` for the Evaluations demo, the Course Reports selector and a "Past cohorts" section on the Cohorts page. Multi-cohort concurrency (a second live cohort, cross-cohort aggregation, "which cohort needs me most") is hidden, not deleted — the machinery survives for a moderator build. (lead.js/lead2.js/lead5.js, Maryam 14 Sep 2026)
- On the SUPER ADMIN portal `u.addedBy` is the moderator marker (Maryam 14 Sep 2026, "addedBy = moderator; cap the rest"): a leader WITH `addedBy` is a moderator (admin-added, may lead multiple); a candidate+leader WITHOUT it is a candidate-turned-leader capped at ONE running cohort. The Users dashboard Cohort-Leader/Moderator split keys on `addedBy` (not the older `u.volunteer`); the Users → Cohort Leader role tab carries a "Leads" column showing how many cohorts each leads (candidate-leader shows 1, moderator shows its count). Seed: `u4` Maryam is the candidate-leader (no `addedBy`, `volunteer:true`, leads one — Cohort 2); `u40` Bruno is the moderator (`addedBy` set, `volunteer:false`, leads two — Cohort 1 + Cohort 4); `COHORTS.leader` strings aligned to match. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 14 Sep 2026)
- The leader COHORT CARD (`cohortCard`, Cohorts page): no image placeholder / cohort-number disc (`.cco-art` removed); the detail line is "Level E3 · Week N of 13" (the level via `c.level`, not the intent); the candidate count is an inline pill (`.cco-pill-in`) under that line, not a corner badge. A COMPLETED past cohort (`LEAD_PAST`, `status:'completed'`) draws a quieter `pastCohortCard` — level, "Completed", candidate count, no pace ring / flags / next-call. (§92/lead2.js, Maryam 9–14 Sep 2026)
- The leader COHORT page (`V.leadCohort`) has FOUR tabs: Candidate Progress (default), Discussion, Ranking, Members (`S.ldrCTab` default `progress`). The Candidate Progress table is the first tab's content, not a standalone section above the strip. (lead2.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- The CANDIDATE PROGRESS table (leader Cohort page + Course Reports) carries a "Course" column (2nd, `lcourse(c)` = `courseOf(c.level)`) and shows Attempts as a WHOLE number (`Math.round(m.att)`, not `.toFixed(1)` — "attempts are 1 or 2, not 1.1"). (lead2.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- The leader 90-DAY SUMMARY / evaluation screen (`V.leadSum`): the header action reads "View Candidates Progress" (not "Their full record"); the "What the 90 days produced" figures have no "read-only" caption and no foot divider (`sec-noline`), flowing into a "Your notes on {first}" section — the leader's own `S.ldrNotes` for the candidate, drawn READ-ONLY (no edit/delete chips) — then "Your recommendation" (no "next agent reads" caption); "Send Recommendation" carries no tick icon. (lead3.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- CANDIDATES take DIFFERENT courses: `mcourse(m)` / `mlevel(m)` spread the four catalog courses (`COURSE_NAME`) across members by a stable name-hash (authored variety), so the Candidates Progress "Course" column varies down the table. The leader cohort card's third row is "Total Courses" = distinct courses in the cohort (was "Assessment"). The section/tab is "Candidates Progress" (plural). (lead.js/lead2.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- The leader MEMBER page (`V.leadMember`): figure sub-lines are plain descriptors, NOT insights ("of the course", "course average", "per chapter" — no "-N against pace" / "going round twice"); Attempts is a whole number; below the figures a "Course" card shows the candidate's course NAME + DESC (`mcourse`/`courseDesc`, ENROL_COURSE for E3/E4, authored for E1/E2 — no course photo asset in this build), left-aligned. (lead2.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- The cohort leader PROFILE has a Specialities `.skl` chip cloud on BOTH the General and Public tabs (`LEADER.specs`). (lead4.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- The Messages DM list row (`.ldr-dm-t`) is the SAME height as the thread header (`.ldr-dm-h`) — both `--s04 --s05` padding (~61px). (§36, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- THE NOTIFICATION PANEL (`.notif`, all four portals via the DS §15): its close (`.x`) sits at the RIGHT of "Mark all read" on one header row (`.notif-all` + `.x` in the flex `.notif-h`, h2 `margin-right:auto`) — never a `.notif-h-a` wrapper or a `.btn`. Each row's mark is a BARE glyph, no chip: `.nrow-ic` has `border:0; background:transparent; background-image:none` (kills §24's `--tal-chip` radial) — an UNREAD mark reads `--accent`, a READ mark reads `--text-secondary` #828282 (the side-nav icon grey). The old 6px unread square (`.nrow.un::after` and the admin's `.dot`) is GONE — state is carried by the mark's ink + the title weight. The notification TIME (`.nrow-w`) drops BELOW the message a size smaller — see the reference-refinement rule below (this SUPERSEDES the 15 Sep "right end, sibling of `.nrow-b`" placement). EVERY divider pays the gutter — the header rule, the rules between rows and the footer rule are inset `::after`/`::before` lines at `--pad-x` both sides, never a wall-to-wall border. OPENING the panel dims the WHOLE frame incl. the app bar (`.notif-scrim`, z 49, only ≥900 — on a phone the panel is full-bleed §28 so there is nothing to dim): the panel + scrim are a child of `.app` (positioned), so on the admin they moved OUT of `.shell-body`. The panel width is `max-width:450px` (§27, Maryam 15 Sep 2026). NO "View all notifications" footer — removed on the admin (the only portal that had one) 15 Sep 2026; the full-record page `home/notifs` stays reachable by deep link. (§15/§27/§24/§19, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- THE NOTIFICATION ROW IS A CALM, SPACIOUS, MESSAGE-FIRST BLOCK (`.notif .nrow`, all four portals via the DS §15; Maryam 21 Sep 2026, off a reference used only for spacing/hierarchy quality, NOT its tabs/footer/buttons). The row is a two-column GRID — mark, then message — with the mark TOP-ALIGNED to the title (`.nrow-ic` sheds its 40px box: `width/height:auto`, `align-self:start`, `margin-top:1px`, glyph 20px) rather than centred on the whole block, `column-gap:--s04` mark→message, roomier `padding:--s05 --pad-x` (the row now pays the same `--pad-x` as its `::after` divider, so mark/message/divider share one left edge). The TIME (`.nrow-w`) is grid-row 2 in the message column (`row-gap:--s02`), indented past the mark, at `--t-caption` 11.5px in `--text-helper` — noticeably smaller than the 13.5 message and always the floor grey (read + unread), so the message is the focus and the time is secondary (§63 sizes/inks it; the layout is §15). Message hierarchy unchanged: title h4 (unread 500/primary, read 400/secondary), desc 13.5 secondary. Scoped to `.notif` so the admin full-page list (`home/notifs`, `.mem` rows) and any other `.nrow` reuse keep the flat row. NO in-notification action buttons — the notification informs and the whole row navigates to the owning module, which is the documented model ("acting on any item happens in the module that owns it"); this is deliberate, not the reference's Resend/Copy pattern. (§15/§63, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- A NOTIFICATION NEVER CARRIES A FILLED ICON — a filled glyph in any notification is swapped to its STROKED pair (FILL 0), or, where the mark has no stroked pair, to another stroked glyph that fits (Maryam 21 Sep 2026). Applied: candidate `NOTIF` `checkFilled` → `checkOutline` (Account created / Interview complete / Course complete, ×4); agent `AGENT_NOTIF` `star` → `starOutline` (a rated interview). Leader/admin notif data was already all FILL 0. This is the notification DATA only — the Earnings/payout ledgers and the permissions matrix keep their own state glyphs. (data.js/tn-agent-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)

- THE SUPER ADMIN CANDIDATE DETAIL (`candidatesDetail`, `candidates/detail/<id>`): NO Tal summary (`detailHead` with no `sum`, back arrow only). The identity row is the candidate portal's `.idhead` — a ROUND 64px portrait (`.idhead-ph`) on the LEFT of the name, the actions a KEBAB menu (`rowActs`, floated right by `.idhead-act`) holding Edit / **Mark As Cohort Leader** (only for an eligible NON-leader; there is NO "Remove Cohort Leader" here — Maryam 20 Sep 2026: "do not give remove a cohort leader option in candidate detail page menu", removal lives on the Cohort Leaders table `cohortsAssign`, so an already-leading candidate shows no toggle) / **Deactivate** (was "Suspend" — the word is "Deactivate"/"Deactivated" now on the candidate confirm, status tag and Status filter too) / Erase — never inline buttons. The record is the candidate portal's eight-fact GRID (`.facts.pf-facts` via `admFact`, `candProfile` data — authored §74, Maryam's u4 matches the candidate portal exactly), third-person: "My track"→"Track", "My level"→"Current Level". NO black level card, NO level-history section, NO separate scenes carousel. Interviews (heading only, NO description, NO "All Bookings") are the candidate portal's COLLAPSED `.found` record (`admIvRow`, `S.ivOpen`, `data-ivopen`): chevron on the LEFT, NO status tag, agent name the title with the kind·fee·when as a sub-line, and a trailing **"View Transcript"** accent-ink link (`.iv-tx-link`, `--accent-text`, `data-go="market/recording/<bookingId>"`) at the row's right end that opens the transcript page — a sibling of `.found-t`, so it navigates rather than toggling the accordion (Maryam 15 Sep 2026). Each opens to the three scenes kept from it in a STATIC grid (`.scv-static`, no carousel/scroll for three), the scene titles in BLACK, NO per-scene "Talent Agent" attribution row and NO void-the-score action. Below Interviews come the candidate's CV — Experience / Education / Licenses & certifications / Skills — the candidate portal's own sections shown READ-ONLY (`candCV`, `cvRow`/`cvArt`, `CAND_CV`; authored placeholder §74). The issuer logos ride in `PF_ART`, now embedded into the design system (`build-ds.py`, off `hifi/build/logos/`, in `DS_HAVE`). Quiz results (`admQuiz`/`.adm-quiz`) is TEXT ONLY — the lede + the two bands lists, heading aligned with the page; the ROSE PIE CHART is REMOVED (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "remove this quiz pie chart"), and the "Where this puts them on the ladder" rows stay removed. Generous gaps between the parts (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "add some gaps"): `.adm-quiz-txt` s07 between lede and the two lists, `.adm-quiz .qzp-g` s05 title→rows, `.adm-quiz .qzp-l` s03 between rows — scoped to `.adm-quiz` so the shared DS quiz-peek keeps its own spacing. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN CANDIDATE-DETAIL COURSES (`candCourses`/`candCourseRow`, `.crs-*`): the accordion body runs FULL WIDTH against the course row — `.crs-list .acc-b` opts out of the DS `--measure` reading cap (`max-width:none`) so the completed course's black card and the four-cell `.facts.pf-facts` insight band fill the width, not a 68ch column (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "full width against the course"). The certificate card is STRIPPED: NO "Course completion certificate" header (`.crs-cert-h` gone), NO Download/Share buttons (`.crs-cert-a` gone) — just the badge + course name + "TALENTnext"; the badge (`.crs-cert-mk`) shrinks 56×60 → 40×44 (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "do not give share or download buttons and remove the 'Course completion certificate' ... reduce the badge icon"). A NOT-STARTED course is HIDDEN — `candCourses` filters `status !== 'Not started'`, so only completed/in-progress courses list (Maryam 15 Sep 2026). Admin-local. The COLLAPSED course ROWS are FULL WIDTH too (Maryam 17 Sep 2026: "the course section rows should have full width"): the old `.acc.crs-list{width:100%}` PINNED the accordion to the section content box while the DS `.sec > .acc` bleed shifted it left `--pad-x`, so every row sat 32px short on the RIGHT under the facts grid; dropping the pin lets the bleed work — row content aligns to the content column, the hairlines bleed full. An IN-PROGRESS course OPENS to the candidate portal's Course Progress content (Maryam 17 Sep 2026: "show the course details just like ... the course progress page ... the performance graph, the progress by chapter") — `candCourseProgress(c)`: `coursePerf(c)` (the DS `.perf-*` line chart — one green line, red low point, dashed cohort rule, the average-score ring callout, "Your score / Cohort average N%" legend, a "Key insight" foot) over a "Progress by chapter" `.tile-stack` of `candChRow` rows (`chRow`'s markup READ-ONLY — the green tick + `min · %` meta on assessed chapters, growth-area sparkle, the not-started circle; NO Restart/Resume and NO `.ch.open` "Continue →", the admin is viewing not taking). Data on the record (`series`/`assessed`/`avg`/`cohort`/`growth`/`insight`), authored §74; chapters from `COURSE_CH`. Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- EVERY PERSON PHOTO ON THE SUPER ADMIN PORTAL IS A REAL PHOTOGRAPH, NEVER AN ILLUSTRATED AVATAR (Maryam 20 Sep 2026: "get rid of avatars and use real images — the other images we are using as candidate or talent agent profile images overall on the platform"). `agentFace(name)` — the one helper behind every admin face (`personCell`, the detail portraits, the scenes carousel, the leader/cohort/linked-account discs) — draws from the REAL photo pool the design system ships and the marketplace + call surfaces already use: `AV.priya/owen/lena/samuel/hana` (five agent portraits) + `CALL_ART.faceW/faceM` (two interview-call faces), seven photos, all reachable as DS globals in this linked portal. A person whose first name IS one of the five agents gets their own portrait; everyone else is assigned one of the seven deterministically by a name hash (stable per person). This REVERSES the same-day choice of `hifi/build/avatars/` (av1 + b1..b4 + g1..g5) — those are the candidate portal's ILLUSTRATED placeholder discs, used there only for the ANONYMISED same-level Ranking, never a real profile (build.py's note). Admin-local (`ADM_FACES`/`ADM_FACE_POOL`/`agentFace`). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 20 Sep 2026) — the APP-BAR account avatar (`.shell-avatar`, top-right) follows the same rule: it shows `agentFace(ME.name)` as an `<img>`, not the "DS" initials (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "use a profile image of the super admin on the top right of the portal header").
- THE TAL FLOATING DOCK IS HIDDEN ON THE SUPER ADMIN PORTAL (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "hide tal floating bottom bar from the super admin portal"). The frame's dock slot renders nothing where it used to call `askDock()`; the bulk-action bar still rides that slot when table rows are selected (it is a selection tool, not Tal). `askDock` stays defined — restore it by putting it back in the `bulkCtx() ? bulkBar() : …` ternary. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- EVERY CANDIDATE- OR AGENT-NAME CELL IN A SUPER ADMIN TABLE carries the person's PHOTO — a small round 28px disc beside the name (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "add agent and candidate images with their names in all tables, do this for whole portal"). `personCell(name, sub)` (a real photo via `agentFace` — see the real-photograph rule above, so one face per person everywhere) replaces `idCell` at every person column: Users, Candidates (name + Agent), Talent Agents, Agent Applications, Cohort Leaders (assign), Bookings (candidate + agent), Recordings (candidate + agent). A non-person cell — a missing agent (—/Unassigned/blank), and every `idCell` that is NOT a person (service health, audit action, case ref, transaction/invoice/fee, employer company, lead) — falls back to the plain two-line `idCell`, so no face is invented. The name's weight/ink still come from the cell (`.pcell-n` inherits), so the DS first-column strong-ink and the row-hover/selected accent reach it unchanged. Admin-local (`.pcell*`). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN RECORDINGS REGISTER (`marketRecordings`, `market/recordings`) is a LEAN list — Candidate / Agent / Interview / Length / Transcript / Actions — and a ROW OPENS THE INTERVIEW TRANSCRIPT (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "these rows should be clickable ... show the interview scenes and transcript"). The SCENES-consent, Employer-visibility, Retention and Status columns are REMOVED from the table (their state still drives the row's kebab actions — play / download / withdraw consent / delete — and lives on in the data); the "Expiring soon" toggle and the consent/status filter dropdowns are removed too — search only. Candidate and agent cells are `personCell` (photos). The row's `go` is `market/recording/<id>`. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 15 Sep 2026) — THE ROW ACTIONS ARE **View / Download / Delete**, three only (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "the action items should be View / Download / Delete"): **View** → `market/recording/<id>` (the detail page, which now carries the recording AND the transcript — see the transcript rule below); **Download** → `dltranscript:<id>` (the transcript .txt, shown only when `transcript==='Ready'`); **Delete** → `recdelete:<id>` (avail rows). The old "Open transcript", the "Play recording" modal (`recplay`/`SHEETS.recording`, kept defined but no longer reached from the kebab) and "Withdraw SCENES consent" are dropped from the kebab. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026) — THE PAGE HEADING READS **"Recordings & Transcripts"** (Maryam 21 Sep 2026), matching the nav sub-item / breadcrumb (was "Recordings"). The table gains a **"Scenes"** column between Length and Transcript, reading **"N scenes"** — the same count the recording's Scenes tab lists (`ADM_SCENE_SET.length` per interview with an agent, so table and tab agree). The **Transcript** cell is now COLOURED via `tag()`/`.statw` (not plain text): **Ready** green, **Generating** amber, **Failed** red — `TAGV` gains `ready:'green'` + `generating:'warn'` (`failed` was already red). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN AGENT INTERVIEW DETAIL (`agentsInterview` PAST record, `agents/interview/<id>`) carries TWO TABS — **About Interview** and **Transcript** (Maryam 17 Sep 2026: "i need two tabs on this page, About Interview and Transcript, the transcript from this page will be shown there"). The identity header (`aiHead('Interview with <cand>', '<agent> · <kind> · <when>')`) stays above the strip; the strip is `filterStrip('ivtab', 'about', …)` in a `.sec.sec-cs` (reusing the `data-filter`/`getFilter` tab mechanism, no new handler); About holds the facts + Tal's/agent's evaluations + the candidate's rating & review, Transcript holds `ivTranscript(iv)` (moved off the page foot). The facts are a HORIZONTAL `.facts pf-facts` block of FOUR — Candidate / Interview fee / Candidate rating / When — the **Kind row is removed** (Maryam 17 Sep 2026: "remove the kind row and show the other 4 in the horizontal blocks view"); the rating cell is hand-written (`ic('starOutline')` mark + `ratingCell` HTML) because `admFact` escapes its value. The upcoming (non-past) interview view shows the four facts as a `.facts pf-facts` block, and — for a FIRST interview only (`!agIvIsRe`) — ALSO the candidate's levelling-quiz read (`admQuiz({name})`, the shared §74 read personalised by first name) and a **"What {first} wants to talk about"** section (heading personalised with the candidate's first name, NO description line): the note the candidate wrote the agent at booking, AUTHORED PLACEHOLDER `ivTopicOf(b)` (§74 — the admin seed has no per-booking note; a stable leadership-situation answer per candidate off a name-hash), shown NOT in a box but as the answer wrapped in `--accent-text` curly quotation marks on both sides (Maryam 21 Sep 2026). The quiz + talk-about show for EVERY upcoming interview (first and re); a "What {first} wants to talk about" heading is personalised (no desc line) and the note reads as the answer wrapped in `--accent-text` curly quotation marks (Maryam 21 Sep 2026). A **RE-interview** (a cohort finisher — the seed carries two upcoming re-interviews, `bk14` Grace Kim / `bk15` Diego Alvarez, with an explicit `stage:'booked'`) ALSO shows, via `reInterviewExtras(iv)`: the candidate's whole profile (the candidate-detail 8-fact `candProfile` band + `candCV()` Experience/Education/Licenses/Skills/Linked), a **Cohort journey** four-figure `stats()` band (Chapters done / Assessment average / Time invested / Tasks on time, authored §74 matching the candidate-portal course band), and a **Cohort leader evaluation** (`cohortLeaderEval` — the leader resolved from the candidate's cohort, photo via `agentFace`, a read paragraph and two `qzList` columns; authored §74). There is NO divider line above this section (Maryam 22 Sep 2026: "remove the line above") — the journey `.sec` carries `.sec-noline` (its §14 foot `::after` off); the eval keeps its normal top padding so it sits a proper gap below the band (no line); the aiHead carries NO desc line (Maryam 22 Sep 2026); the leader-row ★ is the rating gold `--star`; the read paragraph runs FULL WIDTH (`max-width:none`, off the reading measure); and "Recommends re-interview" is a GREEN CHIP (`.tag sm green`, the `verifyTag` pill), not `tag()`'s plain `.statw` text (Maryam 22 Sep 2026: "Recommends re-interview should be a chip"). ALL the content sections of this interview-detail page are wrapped in `.iv-detail-body` so a page-scoped rule gives every consecutive `.sec` an extra `--s06` gap (48→72, Maryam 22 Sep 2026: "give more gaps between each section") — the wrapper reaches the sections from shared helpers (`admQuiz`/`candCV`/`reInterviewExtras`) a per-section class could not, and it restates the top-block no-divider `::after` the first section had as `.modhead + .sec`. Admin-local. The candidate is resolved by `candByName`, synthesised (§74) when the booking's name is not in `CANDS`. A first interview shows only facts + quiz + talk-about (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "show some re-interviews as well … for their detail page … quiz details / what {name} wants to talk about / candidate whole profile / cohort journey in 4 blocks / cohort leader evaluation"). The tab strip section carries `.iv-tabs` and an admin-local rule zeroes its bottom padding + `.cs` margin and pulls the content up to a single `--s05` gap (Maryam 17 Sep 2026: "remove the unnecessary gap between tabs and the content"; the DS `:has()` strip-only zeroing did not resolve at compute time, so a plain class handles it). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN "TAL'S READ" ON THE INTERVIEW DETAIL MIRRORS THE TALENT AGENT PORTAL exactly (Maryam 17 Sep 2026: "i need the tal evaluation just like it is on the talent agent portal"). The old `aiHead('Tal's evaluation') + .iv-card` plain paragraph is replaced by the agent portal's `.ai-aura.tal-eval` component: the blob mark + `.ai-label.bare` "Tal's read", the read as highlighted `<b>` accent phrases (`.tal-eval .ai-body p b` — `--ai-hl-bg` background + `--accent-text`, both ship in the DS this portal links, so `iv.tal` is rich HTML and is NOT escaped), and a `.help mt5` "Proposed only. TalentNext sets the level after analysis, so this is not a decision you make here." line. Each `PAST_IVS[].tal` was rewritten as a four-paragraph read in the agent-portal structure (Tal suggests <level> · what earns it · what holds it below the next level · ask in the room), derived from the existing tal/strengths/growth so no new claims (§74); level phrasing is "Explorer – E<n>" (en dash, allowed), no em dashes (§5). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN INTERVIEW TRANSCRIPT (`marketRecordingDetail`, `market/recording/<id>`, a hidden sub opened from a recordings row) MIRRORS the Talent Agent portal's transcript (Maryam 15 Sep 2026, with a reference): `detailHead` back arrow, `aiHead('Interview Transcript', …)` with the candidate·agent·kind·when·length sub and a Download Transcript action, then the DS `.tx-*` component — a "Find a word in the transcript" search (`data-srch="txq"`), round topic-filter chips (Everything / Marked scenes / Delegation / Hard conversations / Decisions, `data-filter="txf:<k>"`) and the line list (timestamp · speaker · text). The MARKED-SCENE lines (the `scene` tag, "📹 Marked scene" chip) ARE the interview scenes, so this one page is the "scenes and transcript" the row promised. The interviewer (agent) reads accent (`.tx-line.me`), the candidate grey, as the reference shows. `TX_LINES_ADM` is authored placeholder (§74); `recTranscript` (the .txt download) is derived from the same lines so the download matches the page. The `.tx-*` CSS ships in the design system this portal links — only markup + data are portal-local; filter/search reuse the existing `data-filter`/`data-srch` handlers (no new listeners). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 15 Sep 2026) — THE VIEW PAGE NOW CARRIES THE RECORDING TOO, not the transcript alone (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "on view we should not only see the transcript on the next page but the recording as well"): the `aiHead` reads "Interview recording", then `recPlayer(b)` — a 16:9 prototype player (streaming not wired) whose POSTER is a real captured call frame, `assets/agent/iv-recording-still.webp` served relative (the same still the Talent Agent portal embeds as `IV_REC_STILL`), with the `--s...` play disc + scrubber over it — then a `secHead('Transcript')` and the existing `.tx-*` search/filters/lines. Admin-local (`recPlayer`). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026) — THE VIEW PAGE IS THEN SPLIT INTO **THREE TABS — Recording · Scenes · Transcript** (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "there should be 3 tabs ... show scenes of the interview as well"): a `.sec.sec-cs.iv-tabs` strip (reusing `filterStrip('rectab', 'recording', …)` + `data-filter`, the interview-detail tab mechanism, no new handler) under the `aiHead`, then ONE pane — **Recording** = `recPlayer(b)`, **Scenes** = `admScenes([b])` (the shared `dsScenes` carousel of this interview's clips, `ADM_SCENE_SET` × the agent, authored placeholder §74), **Transcript** = the `.tx-*` search/filters/lines (rendered only inside its pane). The three panes are `.sec` siblings of the strip, so `.iv-tabs + .sec` gives the tight `--s05` gap. This SUPERSEDES the single-scroll "player then transcript" layout above. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026) — SUPERSEDED 30 Sep 2026 by TWO tabs (below).
- THE RECORDING DETAIL IS "INTERVIEW ASSETS" WITH TWO TABS — **Recording & Scenes · Transcript** (Maryam 30 Sep 2026, this SUPERSEDES the 21 Sep three-tab layout). (1) The **submodule** (rail leaf under Interviews) is renamed **"Recording, Scenes & Transcript"** (`MODULES` market `recordings` `t`, was "Recordings & Transcripts"; the register list-page `searchHead` heading is left as "Recordings & Transcripts"). (2) The detail **page heading** is **"Interview Assets"** (`aiHead`, was "Interview recording"; the deleted-recording branch too). (3) The Recording and Scenes tabs are MERGED into **"Recording & Scenes"**: the recording shows as the compact **`.iv-rec` attachment card** — the same small card the interview/approval detail pages use (`.iv-rec-sec` + `aiHead('Recording', …)` with the card in the action slot, the 64×44 `assets/agent/iv-recording-still.webp` still, file name + "Video recording · <len>" + a play mark) — NOT the big 16:9 `recPlayer` (kept defined, no longer reached); `admScenes([b])` (the "Interview scenes" carousel) sits below it in the same pane. `filterStrip('rectab', 'recording', [['recording','Recording & Scenes'],['transcript','Transcript']])`; render is `tab === 'transcript' ? transcriptPane : recordingPane`. (4) The header→tabs gap matches the tabs→content gap — see the ONE tab-strip standard below. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
- EVERY DETAIL-PAGE TAB STRIP HAS ONE STANDARD GAP, ABOVE == BELOW == 32, MATCHING THE MODULE LIST PAGE (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "set a standard … match the first table page tabs above and below gaps, and correct this on all detail pages who has tabs"). A module LIST page's filter strip (`.cs.lst-filt`) renders 32 above / 32 below; every DETAIL page that carries a tab strip (`.sec.sec-cs.iv-tabs`, and the Settings inner tabs `.set-tabs`) now renders the SAME. This REPLACES the whole run of per-page tab-gap patches (the 17/18/24/25/28 Sep interview / approval / recording / lc-body / settings rules, and the 30 Sep `.ivrec-*` one) with a single block at the top of the admin `<style>`. The recipe: the header section pays **16** below its content **and its `.sec-h` heading-to-content margin is zeroed** (that 24px gap is wasted trailing space when the next section is the strip — it was the real cause of the 57-vs-32 imbalance); the strip `.sec` wrapper pays **0** vertical padding/margin; its `.cs` pays **16** above AND **16** below the tab row; the content section pays **16** above — 16+16 = 32 on each side. All carry `!important` (the sanctioned section-rhythm override — the ~30-class `:not()`/`:has()` padding chain plus `.iv-detail-body > .sec + .sec`'s 24px margin, the authtight-card precedent). The `.iv-tabs-tight` / `.lc-body` tab overrides and the `.ivrec-head`/`.ivrec-tabs` classes are removed. **Do NOT add another per-page tab-gap rule — change the one standard block.** Verified 32/32 on recording, interview, level-approval, settlement-batch and Settings. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN COURSE DETAIL (`cohortsCourseDetail`, `cohorts/coursedetail/<id>`) shows a COURSE OUTLINE — the candidate portal's chapter accordion (`courseOutline`, `.acc.ol` / `.ol-t` / `.ol-m` / `.ol-cd` "Chapter details" hover label / `.lrn` / `.skl`), added after the Price/Length/Status facts (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "show course outline ... like the one we are showing on the candidate portal side"). Rows are title + "Chapter N · X min"; each opens to "What you'll learn" (`.lrn` ticks) + "Skills you'll gain" (`.skl` chips). The `.acc.ol` component ships in the DS this portal links, so only markup + data are local; the accordion reuses the admin's `data-crsopen`/`S.crsOpen` open-index handler — compare with `String(S.crsOpen) === String(i)` (the dataset value is a string, like `candCourseRow`), not `===` on a number. `COURSE_CH`/`COURSE_SYL`/`COURSE_LEARN` are the reference E-track curriculum, AUTHORED PLACEHOLDER (§74) — every course shows this until real per-course chapters land. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN COHORT DETAIL head (`cohortsDetail`): the "Led by <leader>" line in the `aiHead` description carries the leader's PHOTO as a small round 24px disc (`.cl-lead` inline-flex + `.cl-lead-ph` round `agentFace(leader)`), Maryam 15 Sep 2026 ("show the cohort leader image"). The date field's label is "Cohort starts on" (was "Cycle start") on the create/edit sheet, the extend sheet and the detail fact card; its trigger (`.fdate-t`) insets the calendar icon at `--s05` like the form dropdown's chevron (not `.inp`'s wide `--s08`) so the date reads like the other sheet fields (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "why this field is not looking like other fields"). Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)

Shape and rhythm
- Radius 0. Round things: marks/discs, `.agt-tag`, the `.tbl-flag` chip, the Tal dock (all a literal `999px`), a chat bubble's `16 16 4 16`, a Tal reply's stat card (`.tw-lines`, `16px`, Figma 882:6701) and its follow-up chips (`.tw-chips .chip-tal`, `999px`), the time-grid slot block `.calw-ev` (`8px`), the calendar now-line pill `.calw-now-t` (`999px`) (Maryam 6 Sep), and the auth-card consent boxes `.auth-card .cbx .box` (`4px`, Maryam 17 Sep 2026). (§56, §31.6, §114, §117, §115, §41, §39, §17)
- Sections are **32 apart**; heading to content 12; heading to description 8. The gap drops to **20** ONLY when the NEXT section is a bare blocks/cards row — a block as its first child, **no heading and no text** (§101's `:has(> .stats:first-child)` test). A section that leads with a heading takes the full **32** even when it holds cards (Quick Actions, "Choose your scenes", a headed facts band are all 32, not 20). This SUPERSEDES the 1 Sep "48px section rhythm" and §101's `--pad-x` block gap, platform-wide (Maryam 28 Sep 2026). Mechanism: base `.sec` top/bottom padding is `--s05` 16 (16 + 16 = 32 between two sections, §10); `.dark-card`'s own margin is `--s05` to match (§75); §101's bare-block-pair gap is `20px` (was `--pad-x`); a dark card → a headed section is restated to 32 in §138. (§10, §75, §101, §138)
- EVERY module/content section closes with a **1px `--rule` divider** — EXCEPT a section that holds a card or a block, which closes with 32px (or 20px next to another block) of air and no line (Maryam 28 Sep 2026, platform-wide, all 4 portals). A "block/card" section is one whose content is `.stats`, `.facts`, `.tile-stack`/`.tile`, `.cardrow`, `.gcard`, `.kv`, `.qa`, `.stat`, `.certban`, `.note`, `.tbl-wrap`, `.plate`, `.lvl-hero`, `.lcal-row` (the leader call card), or a `.dark-card`. A **run of consecutive bare block/card rows** (each leading with a block, no heading of its own) stacks at **20px with no dividers** (§101), and the run's LAST block row pays **32px** to the next headed section (§138 restores its bottom to `--s05` — the leader dashboard's call-card → badge → figures → "Attention Required" is the worked case: 20, 20, then 32). No divider after the last section, after a full-bleed ground (`.tint`), a tab strip (`.sec-cs`/`:has(> .cs)`), a `.sec-noline`, or the components §14's join list already excludes (`.found`, `.signed`, `.pf-tgs`, `.pf-facts`, `.lead-bar`). The divider itself is §10's full-bleed `.sec::after` hairline, inset to `--pad-x` at desktop; §138 is an OPT-IN re-enabler (a `:not()` chain scoped away from scene pages by the §14 scene guard) so no special component ever grows a stray line. This REVERSES §14's "divider only before a heading" model — a divider now falls after every prose/heading section regardless of what follows, unless the section is a block. Deliberate per-page rhythms are kept as exceptions (their sections nest under `.iv-detail-body` / `.lc-body`, one level below `.page > .sec`, so this rule can't reach them; authtight auth cards keep their `!important` 32 rhythm). (§138 / §10 / §14 / §101)
  - The candidate dashboard's pulse black card → Quick Actions is **32px, no line**, on every journey stage: Quick Actions leads with a heading, so it is an ordinary section (32), not a bare block row (20). §75.194 zeroed the section's top after a card (the 1 Sep reduction); §138 restores `.dark-card + .sec:has(> .sec-h)` to `--s05` (16 + the card's 16 margin = 32). No line — a dark card draws none and §75.169 already dropped qa's own hairline after a card. The 20px case is only a **bare** blocks row after the card (a `.facts`/`.stats` band with no heading of its own), which §101 collapses. (§138 / §101 / §75, Maryam 28 Sep 2026; this corrects a first pass that wrongly set the pair to 20.)
- A mark is a bare 18px glyph, no tinted chip — every page-body content/figure mark (`.stat-ic`, `.qa-ic`, `.prog-ic`, `.pulse-ic`, `.pf-facts` label mark, `.tg-mk`, `.pf-sr-ic`, `.cl-ic`, `.qzp-r-ic`, `.sump-fi`, `.cco-ic`, `.aw-ph`, `.sumdrop .sd-ic`, `.sig-ic`, `.note-mk`, `.ob-jr-ic`) is 18px, one uniform page-icon size, all four portals via the DS. This SUPERSEDES the earlier 20px (and the 16/17px per-mark variants). The 40px-chip list-row LEADING icons (`.ch-ic`/`.nrow-ic`/`.cardrow-ic`/`.ach-ic`, 22px) are a separate component and are NOT page marks; the Tal floating-field marks (22px, §117) and the side-nav rail icons (20px) are their own sizes. (§29, §72, Maryam 16 Sep 2026)
- A DECORATIVE category-hue mark is INK, not a hue: every bare glyph that only categorises reads `--icon-primary` **#111** (on a dark card, `--on-dark`), never a `--mk`/category colour. This blackens `.stat-ic`, `.qa-ic` (+ all `ic-*` variants), `.prog-ic`, `.pulse-ic`, `.pf-facts` mark, `.tg-mk`, `.pf-sr-ic`, `.cl-ic`, `.qzp-r-ic`, `.sump-fi`, `.cco-ic`, `.aw-ph`, `.sumdrop .sd-ic`. STATE/subject marks are NOT decorative and keep their colour: the green done/verified tick `#00a43c` (`.rec-v`/`.crow-v`/`.pnc-v`/`.pulse-tick`/`.sump-rm.on`/`.note.succ`), the rating star, the journey green/amber/red, the cert-banner brand mark, and `.row-lead > .btn`'s tinted icon-button. All four portals via the DS. (§29/§70 etc, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- A CLICKABLE block's decorative mark turns `--accent` **#bd2a2a** on hover — and NOTHING ELSE moves (Maryam 9 Sep 2026: "do not make the border black on hover"). The icon lights on the container's `:hover`, on the quick-action card (`.qa-c`), the cohort card (`.cco`) and the quiz-peek row (`.qzp-r`); armed in `HOVER_KEEP` (`qa-c`/`cco`/`qzp-r`). Each card's old border/background hover was DELETED at source so arming the class revives only the mark. Static figure cells (stat/pulse/facts/prog/sump/cl) get no hover. (§70/§92/§80, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- `.stat` and `.qa-c` are one shape: title `--t-h4`, content hangs from the top, the figure/arrow centred on the block's right. (§29, §70)
- The cohort leader's "Progress by Chapter" status mark sits on the RIGHT for EVERY row (`ldrChRow`): a green `checkFilled` for a finished chapter (no longer an inline `.ch-tick` beside the name), a **very light** `--gray-20` **#e1e0dc** ring for the rest (Maryam 9 Sep 2026, "they should be very light in grey" — was `--text-secondary` #828282, a mid grey that competed with the tick). The not-started `--icon-primary` and the started `--in-progress` orange both go to `--gray-20`, so the column reads as one green tick against very light rings; state is in the meta ("Started" / "Not started"). `.ch:not(.done):not(.locked) .ch-ic svg`, so the finished tick keeps its green and the candidate's padlock its ink. (§15, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- Every photograph of a person is a disc; artwork (covers, badges, `.crt-art`) stays square. (§106)
- The interview-detail scene cards' play mark (`.scene-play`, on `V.report`'s kept-scenes row AND the chooser) is the SAME disc as the profile-settings scenes' `.scv-play`: a round translucent-black wash (`border-radius:50%`, `rgba(0,0,0,.44)`) with a WHITE glyph — not the old white square with a dark glyph. Sizes stay context-tuned (44px / 32px desktop chooser); only the treatment matches. The `.scene-full` unavailable state dims the disc to a fainter dark wash. (§38, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- EVERY INTERVIEW-SCENE THUMBNAIL CARRIES A SHARE CONTROL at its TOP-RIGHT — a round translucent-black disc (matching the play mark, `rgba(0,0,0,.44)`) with a WHITE `I.share` glyph, on both the candidate Profile's "Interview scenes" carousel (`.scv`) AND the interview Report's "Scenes" row (`V.report`, `.scene-cell`) (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "a share icon on the top … of interview scenes thumbnail … open a dropdown … share on social platforms options … just the share icon"; the disc was moved from top-left to top-right the same day). Clicking it opens a dropdown of the platforms — **Share on LinkedIn / Instagram / X** (`SHARE_NETS`, the three brand marks off `SOCIAL`, NOT filtered by `S.linked` — the reference shows all three), reusing the certificate-hero share menu look (`.scv-share-menu`: white `--layer-01` ground, `--rule` 1px border, a brand-tinted mark + `.t-body` "Share on {n}" per row). The trigger is the ICON ONLY (no "Share" word). The menu is RIGHT-anchored (it extends left from the top-right disc, which also keeps a 220px menu inside a card narrower than it); only the report row's `:first-child` (the leftmost narrow card on a phone) flips to left-anchored. State is `S.shareOpen` (the open card's id, one at a time — trap 9); `data-shareopen` toggles it, the platform row (`data-shareto`) closes it (a prototype — picking a network is the whole flow, like the cert share), and an outside click closes it. The share wrapper is a SIBLING of the overflow-hidden thumbnail (`.scv-art` / `.scene-still`) so the menu escapes the clip; on the Report row each `.scene` (a `<button>`) is wrapped in a `.scene-cell` (a flex column, so its `.scene` — which has no width of its own — fills the grid track again instead of shrinking to its title) so the share `<button>` is not nested in the card button. The menu fits inside the tall carousel card, so `.scv-row`'s scroll clip does not reach it. Candidate/leader only — the behaviour needs `S.shareOpen`, so §139 is in `build-ds.py`'s `NOT_IN_DS`; `scenesCarousel`/`dsScenes` takes an optional `opts.shareFor(index)` injection point that is inert without the argument. (§139, views.js, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
- A person's active status is `.av-on` (§09): a green (`--support-success`) circle at the avatar's lower-right, ABOVE the photo (`z-index:2` — the `.av-ph img` is absolutely positioned, so a plain sibling paints behind it). Sized in per-cent so one class fits every avatar; its host is `position:relative` and non-clipping — a SIBLING of `.av-ph` (`.idphoto`, `.mhead-av`), never a child (`.av-ph` is `overflow:hidden`). On the agent + cohort-leader profile photos and the messages header. (§09, Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- Icons are Material Symbols **Rounded, FILL 0**, pasted on the `0 -960 960 960` box; only four glyphs flip to FILL 1 to say a state (`star`, `checkFilled`, `stopFilled`, and their pairs). (trap 7)
- Cohort covers are one 9:5 rectangle, `object-fit:contain`. (§86)

Components
- THE FRONT DOOR'S LEFT PANEL IS A PHOTO SLIDER (hifi candidate portal, ≥900). §57's single `__AUTHART__` gradient stays as the pre-decode ground; three photographs cross-fade over it (`.auth-slide-1/2/3`, `__SLIDE1..3__`, Maryam's onboarding images resized to 1080w WebP), on a 15s pure-CSS `authSlide` cycle — NO JS/timer (a repaint would blow away the password being typed), so the slide is animation PHASE not `S`. POSITION BARS sit at the RIGHT END of the copyright row (`.auth-foot-row` is `space-between`: `©…` left, `.auth-bars` right): three 32×5 `999px` white-20% tracks, each with an `--accent` `::before` fill that WIPES — it moves in from the left, holds while its slide shows, then goes out to the right, and the exit of one bar overlaps the entry of the next (`authBarFill` clip-path keyframe + the slides' delays) so the accent reads as one fill travelling bar to bar. Motion-off leaves bar 1 filled. The welcome `<h2>` is SEMIBOLD (600, §63's second stated exception, scoped to the slider); the lede (`.auth-lede`) is a half-width 320px measure at 23px line-height. The panel wears the wordmark as a crisp VECTOR (`AUTH_ART` prints `LOGO_SVG` — the wordmark traced from `LOGO_K` with vtracer, `fill:currentColor`, filled white here — not the upscaled-blurry `LOGO_W` raster), at a reduced 150px width and a 64px top inset (§57's 291/133 stay for the DS's raster). `LOGO_SVG` lives in data.js and only `AUTH_ART` uses it, so `build-ds.py` does not extract it. Two BACKDROPS anchor the text on the photo: a SOFT, gradual dark gradient rising from the FOOT (`.auth-brand::after`, multi-stop from ~.78 up to transparent by mid-panel so the photo stays visible) under the copy + bars, and a gentle dark wash at the TOP (`.auth-brand::before`) seating the white logo on the brighter slides. §57 flips the welcome copy to `--on-dark` and the "Let's begin." line to `--accent`, scoped `:has(> .auth-slide)` so the design-system front door (no slides) keeps its dark-on-gradient copy + black logo. hifi-only — §132 is in `build-ds.py`'s `NOT_IN_DS` (only hifi's `AUTH_ART` emits the markup; the base panel still crosses via §57). (§132/§57, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- THE SIGN-UP EMAIL BANNER (`.auth-id`, the panel naming the address) is a NEUTRAL GREY CHIP WITH BLACK TEXT: ground `rgba(0,0,0,.03)` (black at 3%, a stated overlay value), ink `--text-primary` #111 for the label, the address value AND the "Not you?" link (the value inherits the chip's `color`) (Maryam 29 Sep 2026: "black text inside and the bg #000000 3% opacity"). This SUPERSEDES the 17 Sep tinted-accent chip (ground `color-mix(--accent 12%, #fff)`, ink `--accent-text`), which itself reversed the original solid `--accent` + white. §57.4, crosses to the DS. (§57, Maryam 29 Sep 2026)
- THE AUTH-CARD FIELD FOCUS IS A COLOUR CHANGE, NOT A THICKENING — an auth field must not change height when clicked (Maryam 17 Sep 2026, "the fields input behavior is broke, it changes the height everytime I click on the field"). §12's generic `.app .inp.fill:focus` swaps the 1px underline to 2px and yanks `padding-bottom` to 0 to hold ITS box height; the card pads 8/16 and carries no such compensation, so that rule shrank every auth field 7px on focus. §17's focus rule now matches `.app` + `.fill` (→ 0,5,0, beating the fill rule) and keeps `border-bottom:1px solid var(--text-primary)` + `padding-bottom:8px`, so the box holds 41px. Crosses to the DS. (§17, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- THE AUTH FLOW HAS NO ROSE BACK-WASH (Maryam 4 Oct 2026: "we do not need the gradient drop at the back on account creation prototype flow" — account creation, Set New Password, Log In, Reset Password). The two decorative rose ellipses behind the card are OFF: Ellipse 42 (`.app:has(> .auth-card)::before`, the blurred `--auth-wash-1` #ffcdcc band 30px above the top edge — the "gradient drop" read on a phone and the warm top glow on desktop) and the desktop brand-corner Ellipse 43 (`.auth-card::before`, `--auth-wash-2`). Suppressed with `content:none` at the foot of §17 — all widths (a container query adds no specificity, trap 3), crossing to the DS so the talent-agent and super-admin front doors drop it too. The account/sign-in screens stand on a plain ground: white on a phone, `--surface-2` on desktop (the shell/page `background:transparent` that let the wash show is now harmless). The washes' own geometry + blur rules are KEPT above, so deleting the two `content:none` lines restores the door's warmth. The LEFT panel is untouched — the hifi photo slider (§132) and the DS brand panel (§57) keep their own backdrops; only the wash BEHIND the card is gone. This REVERSES the §17 "the wash is on both frames" treatment for the account flow. (§17/§57, Maryam 4 Oct 2026)
- THE AUTH-CARD FORM PANEL (`.auth-card > .auth-col .page`, the bordered card the form sits in ≥900) is `max-width:588px` (was 608, "reduce the width a little(20px)") and inset **24 / 28 / 28** (was 33 / 41 / 41); the auth consent boxes (`.auth-card .cbx .box`) carry a soft **4px** corner — a stated radius-0 exception like the admin select boxes. All cross to the DS. (§57/§17, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- AUTH-CARD FIELDS PAY NO LEFT PADDING — the input text/dots align to the label's left edge, not 16px in (Maryam 17 Sep 2026, "remove the left side padding"); the label sits **6px** above its field (`.auth-card .f > label` margin-bottom); the password↔confirm rows are **32px** apart (`.f-row` gap). (§17, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- THE SET-PASSWORD AND LOG-IN CARDS SIT ON ONE EQUAL **32px** RHYTHM ≥900 (Maryam 17 Sep 2026, "give 32px space between all rows … each section of right side card", and "i need 32px equal gaps in this card as well" for log-in): every section's own vertical padding is zeroed and a single 32px top margin carries every join (desc→…→CTA), matching the password↔confirm 32; on log-in the two bare `.sec-rule > .f` fields (email/password) also take 32 so every gap is one value. Both cards opt in with **`.authtight`** on their `.page` (the marker only these two screens write); no dividers inside an authtight card (`.sec` `border-top:0`, killing §17.6's `.sec-rule` line that read as a divider under the log-in sub-text). The padding-zero carries `!important` **on purpose**: the platform section rhythm (1 Sep "48px" rule + §101 block gaps) sets `.app .page > .sec` padding through a ~30-class `:not()`/`:has()` chain that no plain selector can outweigh; reproducing it to add an exception is worse. Desktop card only. (§57, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- THE hifi `create` (SET-NEW-PASSWORD) SCREEN reads heading "Set New Password" / desc "Create a strong password for your account." (with the full stop), a black "Set Password" CTA that goes to the **Log In screen** (`data-go="login"` — this SUPERSEDES the same-day `stage:onboard` route to the Tal onboarding), and has NO "Already have an account? Log in" closing row (Maryam 17 Sep 2026). The three consent boxes START UNCHECKED; the CTA is DISABLED (`.btn[disabled]` look + `pointer-events:none`) until the TWO required consents (`.cbx-req`, Terms + interview-recording) are both ticked — a pure CSS `:has(.cbx-req input:not(:checked))` gate that re-reads live on each native toggle, no render (trap 9). The third (marketing) is optional. hifi-only markup (`AUTH.create` in views.js) + §17. (Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- THE hifi `login` (LOG IN) SCREEN, reached from the Set Password CTA (Maryam 17 Sep 2026, supplied content): heading "Log In" (supplied casing, kept), sub "Enter your account details to successfully signin." (Maryam 7 Oct 2026, supplied copy kept exactly; was "Enter your details to sign in to your account."), an **Email Address** field (placeholder "Enter your email address") and a **Password** field (placeholder "Enter your password") with a **"Forgot Password?"** link under it (`data-go="forgot"`, ink `--accent-text` — Maryam 18 Sep 2026), a **"Remember me"** consent box, and a black **"Log In →"** button. The Log In button goes to the **Tal onboarding prototype THROUGH THE TN LOADER** (`data-loadgo="onboard"` — Maryam 4 Oct 2026: "before going to onboarding screen i need a loader screen first" — was a plain `data-go="stage:onboard"`, 18 Sep 2026, itself from `data-loginas="candidate"`). `data-loadgo` is the load convention: it sets `S.obLoading` then `setStage('onboard')` so the 2s three-blade `.tn-loader` paints before the onboarding welcome (the same beat "Let's Get Started"/"Find an Agent" play); handler in views.js, guarded against a double press. It DROPS the older login's role picker (`loginRoles()`, now unused). hifi-only markup (`AUTH.login` in views.js). (Maryam 17–18 Sep 2026) — A **SIGN-UP ROW** sits below the Log In button (Maryam 22 Sep 2026, reversing the 18 Sep "drop the Sign up row"): a LEFT-ALIGNED `.auth-alt` line "New to TALENTnext? Sign up", the lead-in `--text-secondary` grey and **"Sign up"** the accent as ink (`--accent-text`), semibold (the platform's strong weight `--t-w-strong` 500) and UNDERLINED; 24px above it. Inline styles follow the "Forgot Password?" precedent (hifi-only markup). NAVIGATION (Maryam 22 Sep 2026): "Sign up" opens the external get-started page `https://web-puce-six-27.vercel.app/new/get-started` in a NEW TAB — a real `href` + `target="_blank"` (`rel="noopener"`), NOT a `data-go` route, because it leaves the prototype for another origin. (Maryam 22 Sep 2026)
- THE hifi FORGOT-PASSWORD FLOW is `forgot` → (Send Code, "Sending..." beat) → `fcode` (Enter Code) → `reset` (Maryam 18 Sep 2026). The `forgot` screen reads heading **"Reset Password"** / desc **"Enter the email for the verification code."**, the email field, and one CTA **"Send Code →"** — NO "Remembered it? Back to log in" line. The CTA carries `data-send="fcode"`: on click it swaps to **"Sending..."** and disables via inline `pointer-events:none` (NOT the `disabled` attribute — §02's `.btn[disabled]` greys it and the loading button stays dark), then advances to `fcode` after ~900ms; the button is mutated in place with no render() in the interval so the loading label survives (trap 9). `fcode` (Enter Code, content off Maryam's reference, built in our auth-card components) reads heading **"Enter Code"** / desc **"We sent a 6-digit code to your email."**, a **"Verification Code"** label with an `--s06` gap below it to the code row, a SIX-digit `.otp` row (matching `.otp`'s 6-cell grid), a full-width **"Verify Code →"** button (`data-go="reset"`) and a **"Didn't receive code? Resend"** line BELOW the button — plain block flow, not `.foot-row` (which sits the action and the way-out side by side on desktop). (Maryam 18 Sep 2026) It is separate from `verify` (the create-flow six-digit email confirm that lands on onboarding). The `reset` (SET-NEW-PASSWORD) landing reads heading **"Set New Password"** / desc **"Set a strong password for your account."**, the New password + Confirm new password fields with NO `.pw-rules` checklist, and one full-width **"Update Password →"** CTA that returns to the **Log In screen** (`data-go="login"` — Maryam 18 Sep 2026, was `stage:new` into the dashboard) — NO "Changed your mind? Back to log in" row (the create screen keeps the pre-filled/pre-ticked composition; `reset` is stripped to the two fields and the button). The old `sent` "Check your email" screen is off this path (kept defined, reachable by nothing). hifi-only markup (`AUTH.forgot`/`AUTH.fcode`/`AUTH.reset` in views.js) + the `data-send` handler. (Maryam 18 Sep 2026)
- A centred button with a LEADING icon (`<svg> Label`, i.e. `.btn.noic` with the mark before the words) gets +--s02 (4px) of right padding, so the icon's own left air does not make the group read right-tight. Universal, all portals, one rule in §02. Only the leading case: the GO arrow (`I.arrowRight`, tagged `.i-go`) TRAILS a button and is excluded — it is the one CSS-visible signal that separates leading from trailing (both are a lone `<svg>` child); `.ic-l` (box ends at its words) and `.ghost` opt out too. (§02, Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- "Make this a black card" means the WHOLE §75 recipe — inset by `--pad-x`, `--s07` inside, haze as `background-image`, the section's hairlines off, `.dc-hd` heading rule, ink flipped by token. Never `.plate` / `.sec.on-dark` in the page body (they get hoisted into the head band). (§75)
- Every heading with a sentence under it is `aiHead({title, desc, act})`; the actions centre on the pair. One Tal star per page. (§73)
- The enrolment card's figure row (`.facts.eo-facts`): the fourth cell's heading is "Members" (not "Total Members"), with the "N seats left" chip (`.eo-chip`) on its RIGHT on the same line — `.eo-fl` is `space-between`, and the chip is `--t-caption` 11.5px at 1px vertical padding (smaller than the old `--t-label`) so it fits there. The "View Course" CTA joins the row as a trailing `.eo-cta` cell and draws NO divider before it (`> .eo-cta::before{content:none}` — it is the card's action, not a fifth figure), at the FULL `.btn` height (40px), never `btn-sm` (32px), so it matches the card's other buttons. Between 900 and 1300 the row with a CTA goes 2×2 with the button on its own full-width line below (`.eo-facts:has(> .eo-cta)`), because five columns crush the cells there; `V.enrol` (no button) keeps four-across. (§63/§108, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- The enrolment black card carries **"Tal recommends"** with the sparkle (`.ai-label bare rec-lab`) above the course name (`.eo-cname`), the same label the agent recommendation card wears (Maryam 9 Sep 2026). Its "View Course" CTA (`.eo-cta`) is DASHBOARD-ONLY — gated on `act === undefined`, not `!act`: `V.payment` passes `act:false` (a card with no head action), and `false` is falsy, so `!act` was still drawing "View Course" on the checkout page where the course is already chosen (Maryam 9 Sep 2026: "remove the view course button from black card on this page"). `undefined` = the dashboard's "no act"; `false` = payment's "no action at all". (views.js `enrolOffer`, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- Head band: Tal's summary left, the one dark card or the journey right; full-width summary when there is no second column. (§56, §70)
- The head band WASH (`--ai-wash`, on `.modhead::before`) is the Figma node 871:6425 gradient, whole: `linear-gradient(183.76deg, rgba(189,42,42,.05) 11.02%, rgba(255,255,255,.05) 52.27%)` — the new accent #bd2a2a at 5% along the TOP edge fading to white by the panel's middle, no green. Replaces the old green-through-warm horizontal ramp. All four portals via the DS. (§70, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- The head band label "Summary by Tal" is FLAT BLACK SEMIBOLD, no text gradient: `--text-primary` ink at `font-weight:600`, the `--ai-grad` clip removed. 600 is the ONE stated exception to the two-weight rule (§11's SemiBold face is loaded); the Tal sparkle mark beside it keeps its ramp. All four portals via the DS. (§70 §2 + §63 §10, Maryam 9 Sep 2026, off 871:6425)
- The Tal summary DESCRIPTION (the body paragraph under the label, `.ai-aura.talsum > .ai-body` and its `.tsum` p) is `--tal-sum-desc` **#414141** at `--t-w-book` (400) — a dark grey well ahead of a plain description, platform-wide (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "the tal summary desc needs to be a little darker", then set to #414141). This SUPERSEDES the 9 Sep `--text-secondary` **#828282** (off Figma 871:6425), which in turn reversed an earlier "TRUE BLACK" (`#000`); the `<b>` accent phrases still carry the emphasis and keep `--accent-text`. It reads a dedicated token (`--tal-sum-desc`, §01), so ONLY the summary body moves — every other description stays #828282. All four portals via the DS. (§33/§01, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- The Tal summary MARK (the "star" beside "Summary by Tal", `.ai-aura.talsum .ai-label::before`) is the BLOB artwork `--tal-mark`, not the sparkle/sphere it was: **28px**, `background-size:contain`, radius 0, no gradient/glow/wordmark, no `mask`, and the `::after` spin off. The WINNING layer is **§70.2** (which had reduced §33.6's sphere to a 12px `--ai-star` sparkle shared with `.rec-lab`); that shared rule is SPLIT — the "Tal recommends" `.rec-lab` mark KEEPS the 12px sparkle, only the talsum star becomes the blob. §33 §7 is also repointed to the blob for consistency but §70 wins. Every head-summary Tal mark is now the one blob; the black card's "Tal recommends" sparkle is untouched. All four portals via the DS. (§70/§33, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- THE ORB'S STATE IS PER-SLOT, AND ONLY THE ORB YOU ARE TALKING TO REACTS (Maryam 17 Sep 2026: "use isolated states of orb where ever they are meant to be used ... in the header there should always be idle state"). An orb is either **live** — it reports what Tal is doing — or an **identity mark** that says "Tal" and holds `idle` for ever. Live: the chat hero (`.tal-hero`, candidate + leader + onboarding) and the byline on the reply that is STREAMING. Idle always: the chat header (`.ask-top-id`), the floating dock and ask bar (`.askline-mark`), the Tal panel header, the composer (`.composer-mk`), the head-band summary mark, and every settled byline. `borbMark(cls, live)` (icons.js) stamps `data-borb-live` for the first kind; `orbSync` (orb-wire.js) drives `.borb[data-borb-live]` and PINS every other `.borb` to idle on each render, so "chrome is idle" is an invariant restored by the renderer rather than a convention nine call sites have to keep. This REVERSES orb-wire.js's first model, one `querySelectorAll('.borb')` taking every orb at once — asking Tal a question set six orbs thinking across the frame, which read as the page glitching. (§132/§133, icons.js + orb-wire.js, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- The head band's Tal MARK is the ORB COMPONENT in its idle state, not a picture of it (Maryam 17 Sep 2026: "in summary section you should use the idle state of the orb"). `placePageSummary` (ai6.js) emits `borbMark('tal-mk')` into `.ai-label` — without `live`, so it never thinks or speaks — and §133 switches off §70.632's `--tal-mark` `background-image` on `.ai-label::before`, gated on `:has(> .borb)` so a portal that has not emitted one keeps the artwork. Same **28px** as the artwork it replaces, so the band's geometry does not move. This SUPERSEDES the 9 Sep "the summary mark is the BLOB artwork `--tal-mark`" rule, which stands only for the hand-written portals until they emit an orb. (§133/§70/§33, ai6.js, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- THE SMALL / CHROME TAL MARK IS THE FLAT BLOB, NOT THE ANIMATED ORB (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "use this blob in all the short blobs places like the ones attached", supplied as `tal-blob.html`, with the Summary-by-Tal band mark, the Ask-Tal dock mark and the chat-header mark as the three examples). Every small Tal mark that is CHROME — the head-band summary mark, the ask bar / field / dock (`.askline-mark`), the chat header (`.ask-top-id`) and panel header, the composer, the standalone mark and every SETTLED thread byline — renders as the clean STILL blob: a flat **145° `#ff6e60` → near-white** body (`--tblob-rose`, a stated exception to §132's "the orb is shades of --accent") with the three brand chevrons in flat white at **half the disc**, no glass, no moving pools, no cast shadow, no motion. The LIVE orb is untouched and keeps the full §132 animated component: the chat hero and the byline of the reply that is STREAMING (a byline flattens the moment its reply settles). Implemented CSS-only in §135 on `.borb:not([data-borb-live])` — the exact seam `borbMark` already stamps (see [[the orb's state is per-slot]] rule above) — so no call site, no JS and no §133 box-sizing changes and every slot flattens at once. The three blade clip-paths are byte-for-byte §132's / `tal-blob.html`'s (same brand mark). Crosses to the DS: the agent portal's borb marks flatten with the candidate portal's; the admin portal emits no borb (Tal summary hidden) so it is untouched. Reversible: drop `135-tblob.css` and its two LAYERS entries. This SUPERSEDES, for the chrome marks, the 17 Sep "the head band mark is the orb component in its idle state" — the chrome orb no longer draws the glass/motion, only the live orb does. (§135/§132/§133, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- THE ORB'S TINT DEPTH HAS A HARD CEILING AT `--borb-int: 1.08` and passing it costs detail rather than buying colour. Each of the four tint clouds ramps from `rgba(t, 0.92 * int)` to transparent; CSS clamps alpha at 1, so a peak above 1 paints a FLAT OPAQUE disc in the middle of that pool — at 1.86 the inner 42% of every cloud was flat, four overlapped into a solid red ball, and a pool with no gradation cannot show that it is MOVING, so the background motion disappeared as a side-effect of a colour setting. Depth comes from the palette, from the four pools overlapping, and from `--borb-core` (the density of the middle, its own control) — never from clipping. Ships at 0.52 with the core carrying the colour. (§132, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- THE ORB'S DENSE MIDDLE IS DRAWN UNDER THE MOVING POOLS, never over them. `.borb__field::after` is the core at `z-index:0`; the four `.borb__cloud` tints sit at `z-index:1` above it and the grain, sheen and cast shadow above those. Anything painted OVER the clouds hides the motion — the layer shipped first as a white lid and then as a red one, both of which read as a flat disc with no movement in it. The core's ramp runs DOWN the palette as it travels out (t1 at dead centre through to t4 at the rim), landing the middle near `rgb(226,116,111)` against a near-white rim, off Maryam's reference of 17 Sep 2026. (§132, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- The highlighted-phrase POPOVER (`.sumdrop`, opened from a `data-sum` accent phrase) takes the platform's own edge and corner: a `--rule` **#efefef** 1px border (was the `--ai-1` accent edge) and radius **0** / `--radius` (was 8px). Its prose (`.sd-lead` / `.sd-read` / `.sd-nb`) is `--text-secondary` **#828282** at 400 (was `--text-primary`); the blue lead-in `.sd-read b` (#0488c5) and the black "Your Next Step" label `.sd-nt` keep their colours. All four portals via the DS. (§70 + §63, Maryam 9 Sep 2026, off 875:6570)
- A `.crow-a` action button is `min-width:185px` (a floor, equal pair) + `white-space:nowrap`, so a long label ("View candidate profile") grows to fit on ONE line rather than wrapping; below 900 it goes full-width. A button label on two lines is a bug. (§71, 6 Sep)
- The candidate General edit form (`pfFormGeneral`) holds Name, Nickname (a handle), Email (read-only), **Current role, Industry, Years of experience, Intent aspiration** and About — Name / Nickname / Email on row 1, Current role / Industry / Years of experience on row 2 (`.pfe-g3`, three columns ≥700, stacking below), Intent aspiration a 3-option select (`INTENTS` = Develop in my current role / for another role / for ownership), then About full width (`w:1`). Intent aspiration is the SINGLE intent field cohort formation reads (it is the "intent aspiration" of the five onboarding fields — not duplicated). The **student flag is derived from Current role = "student"**; the degree program lives in Education (its `degree`/`field`). `pfMiss('general')` counts name/nickname/role/industry/years/intent/email/about; the read view (`pfSecView.general`) appends Current role / Industry / Years / Aspiration to the `.facts pf-facts` band (icons `user`/`skill`/`time`/`flag`). This SUPERSEDES the 7 Sep four-field freeze — the client's five-field onboarding requirement reopens it. (§111, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- The Intent aspiration field is OUR dropdown (`dd()`), never a native `<select>` whose open list is the OS's (Maryam, 9 Sep 2026: "this dropdown should not be system dropdown but of our ui"). §123's `.dd*` CSS already ships in this build; the candidate portal now has the `dd()` builder + `S.dd`/`S.ddVal` state + `data-ddtoggle`/`data-ddset`/click-away handlers, ported from the agent portal — `pfField`'s `t:'dd'` emits it. The form is a prototype that does not persist on Save (`data-pfedit` only closes), so the pick lives in `S.ddVal[fid]` for the session. (§123, views.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- The profile fact band (`.facts.pf-facts`, read view) is a FIXED FOUR COLUMNS at ≥900 (`§105.1a`, inside the container query), so the candidate's eight facts read as two attached rows of four rather than one row of eight (Maryam, 9 Sep 2026: "divide these 8 cards in 2 rows ... 4 cards in each row, all attached, just like a grid"). The band's own 1px grid gap paints the row hairline too, so it reads as one grid. A four-fact band (leader/agent public profiles) stays one row of four; below 900 the base `.facts` auto-fit stands (two columns on a phone). (§105, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- The COHORT LEADER profile module mirrors the candidate's: FOUR tabs — General Profile / Public Profile / Notifications / Privacy Settings (`S.ldrPfTab`). General is the candidate's `pfSecView.general` shape — a 72px `.idhead` (name, "Volunteer cohort leader · leading since …", the "Assesses E1–E3" `.tag`, the `.av-on` dot, Edit details), a four-fact `.facts pf-facts` band (Candidate rating 4.9 · Cohorts led 8 · Completion rate 84% · Level movement +0.8 — the leader's standing figures, moved off the old Public "Your standing"), then an About paragraph (`LEADER.bio`). Notifications is its own tab (the four `.tg` toggles). Privacy Settings is the candidate's `pfPrivacy` composition — Sign in and security (two `.pf-sr` rows, `LEADER.email` + password with Reset via `S.pfPw`/`data-pfpw`, the inline `pfFields('pw', …)` form) over Closing your account (`.close-b`, `data-del`). `LEADER` gained `email` and `bio` in lead.js for this. (Maryam 7 Sep 2026, `V.leadProfile` in lead4.js)
