- The cohort leader's PUBLIC Profile is built on the Talent Agent's Public Profile (Maryam 9 Sep 2026, "improve the cohort leader public profile just like talent agent public profile"): a 72px `.idhead` with rating + assess range on the `.idmeta` line (`★ 4.9 · Assesses E1–E3`), a four-cell `.facts pf-facts` band, an About paragraph and a Specialities `.skl` chip cloud — NO "public profile" heading/description/edit button (editing is on General's "Edit details"), first two sections `.sec-noline` so it reads as one band, no `.av-on` dot (§09). The four cells are the leader's, not the agent's — Cohorts led / Call length / Cadence / Leading since (a leader is unpaid, so no fee cell; rating and range stay in the idmeta, not repeated). The old "Your listing" kv folds in: range → idmeta, call length → a cell, Specialism → the chip cloud (`LEADER.specs`, authored placeholder). This SUPERSEDES the 7 Sep identity (stars line + "8 cohorts led" helper + bio quote). (`V.leadProfile` in lead4.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- EPIC 12 — THE COHORT LEADER WORKSPACE (stories 12.1–12.9, hifi leader portal `lead*.js`, Maryam 30 Sep 2026). FOUR gating decisions locked up front: **(1) the 5-item story rail (My Cohort · Sessions · Discussion · Messages · Evaluations) PLUS Course Reports + Certifications kept** — the standalone Cohorts LIST and the Ranking tab drop (redundant with one live cohort; Past cohorts folds into My Cohort, Profile stays in the account menu). `leadCohorts`/`leadCohort`/`leadCalls` stay DEFINED, off the rail, deep-link reachable; **(2) leader-side only** — the candidate-facing mirrors (the candidate's read-only session schedule + Join, the shared Discussion tab, the candidate end of Messages, the notifications) are a follow-up; **(3) the handle / "show real name" model (22.3)** is introduced; **(4) the full Sessions spec** (repeat series, overlap refusal, edit/cancel variants, the auto-attendance register). Screens-only prototype: LightspeedVT sync (19.1), Twilio rooms, notification/email sends, real file uploads and exports are STUBBED; a held session's register and the candidate figures are authored/derived (§74). Per story:
  - **12.1 shell** — `NAVSETS.leader` is the 7-item rail above; the app-bar carries a persistent cohort-name+week chip (`leadHeaderChip`, guarded `isLead() && V.leadDash` for the cross-file TDZ on a `#leader/...` deep link); the account-menu switch ("Switch to {other portal}") is the existing `data-swap`. `leadLive()` is the one active cohort or null; a null-cohort workspace reads "You are not leading a cohort at the moment." and keeps past cohorts readable.
  - **12.2 My Cohort** (`V.leadDash`, moved to lead2.js so lead.js's foot render falls back cleanly) — a cohort header (name, course, Day n/90 + Week n/13 = `leadWeek(day)=ceil(day/7)`, start–end dates, count + levels present, average progress, next session + `sesCountdown`), a Summary-by-Tal card, three aggregate counters (Average progress / Below pass mark <75% / Never signed in — NO attendance counter), the candidate table (`leadRosterTable`: Candidate/Level/Chapters/Assessment/Attempts/Time/Attendance/Last active; every column sortable via `data-ldrsort`+`S.ldrSort`; level `dd('ldrlvl')` filter; search filters the DOM in place via `ldrRosterSearch`/`leadApplyRosterSearch`, re-applied in the render wrapper), the LightspeedVT last-read stamp (`LEAD_SYNC`), and a Past cohorts band.
  - **THE HANDLE MODEL (22.3)** — `handleOf(m)` (accepts a member or a name), `SHOW_REAL` set (who opted in, authored §74), `leadName(m)` (HTML: handle always, real name beside it where shown) and `leadPlain(m)` (what search matches). Applied across roster, member detail, messages, discussion, attendance.
  - **12.3 progress detail** (`V.leadMember`) — handle name, level + day/week + joined-week, Contact; figures (Assessment red under 75%); Course card + **Open in LightspeedVT** (`launch` glyph, prototype external link); Progress by Chapter (`ldrChRow`); an **Attendance** list (held sessions since join, `memAttended`, "Attendance unavailable" for a no-data session) with the running total; editable notes. The interview-scenes row was **removed** — 12.3 forbids the leader seeing recordings/scenes/transcripts/Level Reports/AI score.
  - **12.4 notes** (`ldrNotesSec`/`ldrNotesRead`) — Type is two radios (Strength / Area to develop, neither pre-selected, `data-ldrnotek`+`S.ldrNoteK`), one Note field (1–2000, no title), tagged with the cycle week (`leadWeek`) + date; a note is editable/deletable for 24h after writing AND while the cohort is active (`noteEditable`, `leadEditable(c)=c.status==='active'`); the eval screen shows them read-only, grouped under Strengths / Areas to develop (`ldrNotesRead`).
  - **12.5 sessions** (`V.leadSessions`, `leadSessionSheet`) — a weekly series generated from the cohort's `callBaseISO` (`leadBuildSeries`, chapter = that week's `CH`), one-offs/cancellations pushed onto `LEAD_SESSIONS[coId]` at runtime. The next session is a highlighted card with a 5-minute-gated Join (`sesJoinable`); upcoming + past lists; a past held session opens its attendance record, an unavailable one reads "Attendance unavailable", a cancelled one shows its reason. The schedule/edit form (native controls, read on Save so typing never re-renders; overlap-error re-render keeps the typed values on `S.sesForm`): Title, Chapter (`select`), Date (today..cohort end), Start time (+tz label), Duration (15–240), What it covers, Repeat (Does not repeat / Weekly / Fortnightly → occurrences to day 90). **Overlap refusal** (`leadOverlap`) names the clash. Edit and Cancel (reason mandatory, shown to candidates) carry a this-vs-all-later scope for a series. **The session handlers check specific actions BEFORE the backdrop `data-sesclose`** — the Save button sits inside the `.modal` that carries `data-sesclose`, so `closest()` would swallow the click if the close branch ran first.
  - **12.6 attendance** (`V.leadSession`) — the register is DERIVED at read time from each member's engagement (`memAttended`, stable per member+session, `ATTEND_MIN_MIN=5`); attended-of-held since a member joined leaves out cancelled + no-data sessions (`leadAttn` → 4 of 5, not 4 of 6). A read-only register (handle + mark + minutes) + a session note (`SESSION_NOTES`, editable while the cohort is active).
  - **12.7 discussion** (`V.leadDiscussion`, `LEAD_DISC`) — threaded posts newest-first: pin (one at a time, leader only, `discPinSheet` confirm when replacing), reply (one level), react (single thumbs, toggled), edit for 1h after posting (`discEditable`), a "Cohort Leader" badge on leader posts + "You" on your own; NO delete, NO moderation. `LEAD_ME` is Priya.
  - **12.8 messages** (`V.leadMessages`) — a message carries text, a voice note (its duration) or an attachment (name/type/size, 25 MB cap); read receipts ("Read <time>" from `thread.readAt`); a "Past cohorts" section (a thread outlives its cohort); a read-only state once the cohort closes (the composer is replaced by "This cohort has closed. You can still read your messages."). Voice recording is a real timer (`ldrRecTick`, elapsed-time not a counter — trap 17, auto-stops at 5 min) with no captured audio; the file input carries the name/type/size, nothing uploads.
  - **12.9 evaluation** (`V.leadEvals`/`V.leadSum`) — the recommendation window closes when the cohort is Completed (`end + completeDelay`); the Evaluations list shows the outstanding count + "Recommendations can be written until <date>." Awaiting (black card) + Evaluated lists. The eval screen adds an **Attendance** kv and the grouped read-only notes to the existing figures + `LDR_RECS` radios + "Where they grew" / "Still to develop" (both now mandatory); **Save draft** (`s.draft`, keeps the candidate in Awaiting, prefilled on reopen) and **Send Recommendation** which opens a confirmation (`leadEvalConfirmSheet`, "…cannot be changed afterwards") before publishing. (lead.js/lead2.js/lead3.js/lead4.js + 31-lead.css/36-lead2.css/99-notes.css/100-msgui.css, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
- EPIC 12 — LATE REFINEMENTS (hifi leader, Maryam 30 Sep 2026, `lead*.js` + `31-lead.css`/`36-lead2.css`/`113-leadcalls.css`/`63-typography.css`):
  - **The Evaluations page (`V.leadEvals`) is TWO TABS — "Awaiting Evaluations" / "Evaluated Candidates"** ("this page should have 2 tabs …"), `data-ldrevtab` → `S.ldrEvTab` (default `awaiting`, the leader's own `.sec.sec-cs > .cs` strip, count in `.lf-n`). The BLACK CARD is GONE ("in awaiting evaluations do not show the black card just show the candidate row like … evaluated candidates"): a waiting candidate is the SAME `sumRow` `.tile` as a published one, differing only by cta verb (**Evaluate Candidate** vs **View Evaluation**) and the absence of the recommendation chip; both subtitles read "Scored N%". `evCol` and its `.ev-*` column drawing (**§90 is now dead CSS — flag to drop the layer**) are retired. The **"2 recommendations outstanding … until <date>" window line is REMOVED** (the count/date live only in Tal's summary now); with it gone the strip is the first section after the Tal band so §15 drops its top border and it reads attached, and `.lead-evtabs`/`.lead-evbody` (§31) tighten the strip→content gap to `--s05`.
  - **The Session detail (`V.leadSession`) top facts are FOUR BLOCKS, not a kv** ("make the top 4 rows into 4 blocks in a row"): a `.facts pf-facts` band — **Session** = "Week N Cohort Call" (new lead fact), **Chapter** = "Chapter N" (the week number, not the chapter title), **Date and time** = date · time with NO "(London (UTC+01:00))" zone, **Attendance**. The Register section's **"Worked out from the room · 5 min minimum" caption is REMOVED**, its rows (`.atd-row`) pay `--pad-x` each side so the content lines up with the heading + facts, and the **"Note on the session" section is REMOVED** from the page foot (`SESSION_NOTES`/`data-sesnotesave` stay defined).
  - **The Sessions page (`V.leadSessions`) next card is the MY-COHORT BLACK CALL CARD** ("use the same black call card on my cohorts screen in place of the black card on the session screen") — the `.lcal-next` `lcalCard(lcall(c), true, {countdown:'In 02:40'})` in its `.lcal-row`, replacing the older `leadCallCard`; `lcalCard` gained an `opts.countdown` override and the Sessions copy reads **"In 02:40"** (HH:MM) where the dashboard reads "In 2hrs 40mins".
  - **The `.lcal-next` black call card**: the date/time block (`.lcal-slot`) is HIDDEN and the body pays an even `--s07` on all four walls; the "N minutes" foot reads **"N minutes session"**; the count→foot gap is tightened (body gap `--s03`, foot `padding-top:0`); the "+N" overflow disc is a solid dark grey (`color-mix(--on-dark 14%, --gray-100)`) with white ink. The **cohort header** (`.lead-cohd`) title→day-line gap is `--s02` (4, was 8).
  - **The leader Messages page** (`.msg-mod`) pays a `--s05` bottom padding under both panes ("give bottom padding to message field"), reversing the same-day flush-to-foot pass so the composer is not jammed against the frame. (Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
- The Talent Agent's PUBLIC Profile identity uses the SAME large 72px `.idhead` as My Profile (not the old 40px `.cardrow`); the sub-line is the rating + assess range, no `.av-on` dot (that green marker is the owner's own view, not the listing). (Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- Both idhead sub-lines under the candidate's name — the read view (`pfSecView.general`) and the edit form (`pfFormGeneral`) — show the NICKNAME, not the email or the headline. (§111, Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- A read-only field is `[readonly]` + `.pfe-ro`: §12 draws `[readonly]` with a DASHED underline (the lock), and `.pfe-ro:focus` holds that dash instead of arming the 2px accent — never `disabled` (the value stays selectable), and no "Needed" flag. `pfField`'s `{ro:true}`. (§111/§12, Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- A profile photo's edit control in EDIT view is the `.idphoto-edit` pencil badge on the disc's bottom-right — a ROUND (`999px`) chip in `--link` blue (deepening to `--link-hover` on hover), `--background` ring, NOT a "Change photo" button beside it. This REVERSES §105, whose reason (two controls 40px apart) the button's removal undoes; the badge is the one place a mark is not radius-0. Read view keeps a plain disc, no badge. (§11/§111, Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- The PHOTO PICKER modal (`photoSheet`, opened by the `.idphoto-edit` badge) has TWO tabs — **Profile Image** and **Avatar** (`S.photoTab`, a `.cs` strip filling the width, the sheet header divider dropped so the strip is the one line under the title). PROFILE IMAGE: the current photo large and centred (160px disc — enlarged from 120 to use the modal's space, Maryam 14 Sep 2026), with a centred Upload / Remove row below — each mark LEADS its label (`.btn.noic`, `I.upload` = the upload arrow NOT `I.add`, `I.close` for Remove). Upload is a `<label for>` over a hidden `<input type=file accept=image/*>` so the click opens the OS file dialog; `pfPhotoUpload` reads it into `S.photoPreview` and re-renders. Remove sets `S.photoPreview='removed'`, swapping the disc for the `image` placeholder glyph in a light `--surface-2` disc. AVATAR: the ten supplied discs (`AVATARS` — av1 + b1..b4 + g1..g5) at FIVE per row (two rows), the current one carrying `.photopick.on`; selection is `S.avatarPick` (default `av1`). The black footer button reads **"Update Photo"** on the Profile Image tab and **"Update Avatar"** on Avatar; a prototype, so it and Cancel just close (opening resets to the current photo). All state in `S` (trap 9). BOTH tabs are the SAME height — `.photo-sheet .sheet-b` holds a `min-height` and vertically centres each pane — so the modal does not resize when you switch (Maryam 14 Sep 2026). (§11, `photoSheet`/`pfPhotoUpload` in views.js, Maryam 14 Sep 2026)
- The candidate General-details EDIT form (`pfFormGeneral`) has NO "General details" heading — it opens straight on the photo row; the `.sec-h` stays only as the carrier for the Save / Discard pair, which `.pfe-acts{margin-left:auto}` floats to the far right of the label-column-free profile page. The READ view never had that heading (it opens on the `.idhead`). (§111, views.js, Maryam 14 Sep 2026)
- Module pages have no `<h1>` and no fact row; the page name is in the top bar; back is "← Back". (§78)
- A sub-page's back arrow returns to where you came FROM (`S.hist`'s last entry), never a hardcoded view — same target the breadcrumb's penultimate crumb links; fall back to the module root only for a deep link with an empty stack. (§78)
- A STRIP-ONLY tab section draws NO closing hairline under it: `.cs`'s own `border-bottom` is the boundary, so a `.sec-cs` holding only the strip has its `::after` off. A `.sec-cs` that ALSO holds its content (leader Cohort/Reports/Profile) keeps `::after` — that is a real section-foot separator, not the after-tabs line. Gated `:not(:has(> .cs + *))`. (§98.3)
- A strip-only tab section sits ONE `--s06` (24) above its content, not two: its own `padding-bottom` and the `.cs` `margin-bottom` are zeroed so only the content section's `padding-top` remains — else the strip pays the gap twice (~64px). Same `:not(:has(> .cs + *))` gate. (§98.4, agent portal, 6 Sep)
- Quick action cards may take a short one-line description, but it is a STATIC descriptor of the destination — never the queue's data (a name, time or count), which lives on the page the card opens. (§98.3, agent portal)
- A dashboard's quick actions are a ROW of `.qa-c` cards laid out by `.qa` — 2/3 up on the candidate and agent dashboards (the base grid, `max-width:784px`), and 4 up on the admin dashboard via `.qa.qa-4` (§70), which lifts the cap and steps 4 → 2 → 1 across 900/600 like the `.stats` rows beside it. At the narrower 4-up width the title and description must each hold ONE line, so the copy is kept short (§98.3 makes the descriptor static, which keeps it short too). `.qa` is a §10.15 label-column opt-out (its heading sits above the card row). (§70/§10.15/§98.3, Maryam 8 Sep 2026)
- A dashboard puts related SHORT key/value blocks SIDE BY SIDE, not each in its own full-width band — the frozen file's `g-fin`/`g-3`/`g-2` rows. `.dgrid` (`.dgrid-2` / `.dgrid-3`) is the grid, each `.dcol` a heading + its `.kv`, no card chrome (the linear-band aesthetic holds), stacking to one column below 900. Inside a `.dcol` the `.kv` drops the 184px label spine §10.5 gives it (a narrow cell has no room) and reads label-left / value-right; the per-block description and action are dropped (the rail is the navigator). Wide stat rows and tables stay full-width. (§120.1e, Maryam 8 Sep 2026)
- The Interviews view keeps its FOUR tabs (Requests / Upcoming / Evaluations / Past Interviews) even with nothing booked; each tab draws its OWN empty section, never one page-wide empty state that hides the strip. The lists are gated on `v.busy` (IVS is a fixed array, so `reqOpen()`/`diary()` are non-empty whatever the stage). (`V.interviews`, agent portal, 6 Sep)
- No timezone is shown anywhere in the agent portal — no "ET"/"Eastern time" label, and times are bare ("3:00 PM"). (Maryam 6 Sep)
- The time-grid day column head is Google-style: day abbreviation ABOVE the date, centred; today's date is a filled accent disc (same marker as the month grid). (§42, 6 Sep)
- The calendar has three views behind a Daily/Weekly/Monthly switch (`data-calview`), Weekly the default: Weekly/Daily are a time-grid (`.calw` — hour axis, a column per day, bookings placed and sized by time, a now-line at `CAL_NOW_MIN` on today), Monthly is the month grid (`.cal-grid`). One header (`calHead`), one popover, one set of block states across all three; nav means ±1 month / ±7 / ±1 days by `S.calView`. (§41/§42, agent portal, 6 Sep)
- Calendar slots are FILLED by type in both views — first interview `--cal-iv`, re-interview `--cal-reiv`, open `--cal-open`, no border: the weekly/daily time-grid is a block sized to its duration (`.calw-ev`, 8px, 3px off the hour line); the MONTHLY view is a compact one-line row (`.cal-slot`, 6px) carrying the same fill (no dot) + time + name. **The fill reads `--cal-color`, not the never-assigned `--cal-fill`** (Maryam 2 Oct 2026) — `--cal-fill` was referenced by both rules but set nowhere, so the blocks shipped transparent and their 3px top/bottom air was invisible, making a half-hour-starting block's text look like it sat ON the hour line it crosses ("the 5:30 slot touches at the bottom"). Painting the card floats it over the gridline with equal visible air top and bottom. Today's date is a filled accent disc, white text, no border. A cell lists every slot on its day up to 3, then `+N more` (`.cal-more`) opens the day view. No Join or any button on the slot; Join is in the card it opens. (§41/§42, agent portal, 6 Sep)
- A DISABLED JOIN KEEPS THE ACCENT PRIMARY LOOK, IT DOES NOT GREY OR GO WHITE (Maryam 28 Sep 2026: "the button is in accent color state but disabled since it is not clickable ... implement this change everywhere"). This REVERSES §81's 3 Sep grey-disabled-Join rule platform-wide: a `.dark-card`/`.plate` primary that is `disabled` keeps §75.3/§19/§15's accent gradient + `--on-accent` white ink (§81 restates the accent on the `[disabled]` selector, §63 §20 keeps the white ink); `[disabled]` still carries §02.108's `pointer-events:none`, so it looks live and cannot be pressed. `joinLive`/`joinArm` add/remove `disabled`; only the LOOK changed. Crosses to the DS, so the leader dashboard/sessions/cohort cards, the candidate cohort next-call, the consult plate and the agent portal's gated Joins all follow. (§81/§63, all four portals, Maryam 28 Sep 2026)
- ~~A Join is shut (`disabled`, grey) until the call opens~~ — SUPERSEDED 28 Sep 2026 (accent-disabled, above); the gate is still on by default. (§81, `joinLive`)
- THE JOIN BUTTON IS DISABLED WHENEVER THE CALL IS MORE THAN 1 MINUTE AWAY — it enables only inside the final minute before the start time. A countdown/`crow-due` timer reading more than `1:00` always means a disabled Join; the button goes live when the timer reaches ≤ 1 minute (and stays live once the call is open). (§81/§119, `joinLive`, 6 Sep)
- The "Add a slot" control leads with a plus (`I.add`), never a trailing arrow — adding is not going somewhere. (§64, agent portal, 6 Sep)
- A dialog footer action (a `.sheet-f` / `.conf-f` button) grows NO trailing arrow — it is one of a pair of answers next to Save, not a link. §64's arrow is for a quiet button that takes you to a page. (§64, 6 Sep)
- A MODAL SIZES TO ITS CONTENT and never reserves blank space: `.sheet-b{flex:0 1 auto}` (was `flex:1` = `1 1 0%`, whose flex-basis:0 + `overflow:auto` let some engines make the body taller than its content inside the auto-height sheet — a reserved gap, e.g. below the Origin dropdown on Add User). The sheet is header + content + footer, capped at `max-height`; the body scrolls only when capped; a dropdown opens downward over the body, no space kept for it. All portals via the DS §02. (Maryam 13 Sep 2026)
- A MODAL OVERLAY COVERS THE WHOLE FRAME, app bar included: on the Super Admin portal the `.modal` overlays (`confirmSheet`/`sheetView`/`dsPayForm`) are direct children of `.app` (positioned), not `.shell-body` (which begins below the 68px bar) — so `inset:0` dims the header too; `.modal` z-index (70) beats the bar's (30). The notif panel stays in `.shell-body` (a bar dropdown, not a full overlay). (tn-admin-portal.html render, Maryam 13 Sep 2026)
- MODAL FRAME (all `.sheet` modals, all portals): one uniform `--s06` (24) inset on the header, body and footer — the title, fields and buttons all sit the same distance from the walls (header keeps `--s05` right for the `.x`). The header divider is INSET by `--s06` (a `.sheet-h::after` rule, not a wall-to-wall border); the footer has NO top divider. The header's BOTTOM inset is the one exception — **`--s04` (12), tighter than the other three walls** (Maryam 2 Oct 2026: "reduce the gap between the modal heading row and the divider below it"), so the divider sits close under the title; the body still pays its own `--s06` under the rule. (§02, §10, 6 Sep / 2 Oct 2026)
- SECONDARY BUTTONS ARE A FILLED GREY BUTTON (`.btn-s`): a `--rule` **#efefef** ground, no border, hover one grey step darker (`--gray-20`) — the Cancel half of every Cancel/Save pair, "Keep the interview", "Discard", the quiet pair-member in a sheet or confirm footer. This SEPARATES secondary from GHOST/TEXT (`.btn-g`/`.btn-t`), which keep the transparent, bordered treatment (an admin heading ghost is untouched). All portals via the DS §02. (Maryam 2 Oct 2026)
- The add-slot sheet is OUR dropdown, never a native `<select>` whose open list is the OS's: `dd(key, opts, val, {ic})` draws an `.inp.dd-btn` trigger (rotating `chevDown` flush right, a leading `time` clock on the time variant) and a `.dd-menu` popup on `.acct-menu`'s recipe; the open one is `S.dd`, and its `.dd-opt.on` row is the accent — tick fill `--accent-text` (§123) and label `--accent-text` (§63 §52) — over a `--overlay-selected` ground. `dd-opt` is the one armed hover. (§123 + §63 §52, agent portal, 6 Sep)
- An open dropdown OVERLAYS everything and is not clipped by the sheet's scroll: `editSheet` puts `.dd-open` on `.sheet-b` while `S.dd` is set, which makes the body `overflow:visible` and lifts it above the footer (§123.4). Time fields open upward, the day field downward. (§123, agent portal, 6 Sep)
- The add-slot Day is a WEEKDAY name only ("Monday"), not a date, with NO "Day"/"Time" field labels (the controls say what they are); the Time is two side-by-side dropdowns in a `.tm-row` grid — start "to" end, each 12-hour with AM/PM (`SLOT_TIMES`), after the reference; no "45 minutes…" helper; the save button is "Save Slot" (a third `SHEETS` tuple slot). (§123, agent portal, 6 Sep)
- ADDING A PAYMENT METHOD OPENS ONE SHARED MODAL ON EVERY PORTAL (Maryam, 6 Sep): Stripe's own "Add Payment Method" form, EMBEDDED as a picture (the Calendly third-party rule), never redrawn. `payForm(tab, open)` in views.js draws it — a `.pay-modal` over the `PAY_ART` picture (Card / US-bank), three transparent buttons over the form's own painted tabs and Save/Cancel row (`data-paytab` swaps the state, `data-payclose` + backdrop close). It crosses to the design system as `dsPayForm` (DS_RENAME/DS_BUILDERS) with `PAY_ART` re-embedded by `build-ds.py` and named in DS_HAVE. `S.payTab` is the state; each portal has its own open flag (`S.addCard` candidate, `S.payModal` agent/admin) toggled by an "Add card"/"Add payout method" button. No "How a fee splits" on the agent Earnings page.
- A candidate PAYS from a saved card, never a re-typed one: `cardPicker()` lists `S.cards` (`BMK` brand marks, the `.cardrow` the Payments page draws minus Remove), the default selected (`payWithIdx()` → the `def` card), `data-paypick` chooses (`S.payWith`). "Add a card" (`addCardAct`) rides the RIGHT of the "Pay with" heading row (`.sec-h-act`), not the card list, and opens the shared modal. Used on the course payment (`V.payment`) and the booking checkout (`V.checkout`). Both screens are the picker + the pay button and nothing else: no Tal summary band (no `PAGESUM` entry), no processor note, no fee/total or order-breakdown tile, no refund line — the price is on the pay button. (candidate, 6–7 Sep)
- An `ENROL_COURSE` (course card) `desc` is a DEFINITION of the course — what it teaches and what it is for, in `E3`'s catalog register — NOT the journey/format ("take the same chapters further, close the 90 days"). The format lives in the stat cells, the crumb and `ENROL_DESC`. (candidate, 7 Sep)
- The ALL-COURSES list (`allCourses`) FOLLOWS THE ALL-AGENTS LIST exactly (Maryam, 9 Sep 2026: "follow the organized ui we have on the all agents screen"): the `.agt` table is a BARE sibling of the heading `.sec`, like `agentsTable()` — never nested inside the `.sec` (that pays a 32px inset each side, narrowing the table 64px and squeezing "What it covers" to ~173px so every topic pill wraps to its own line). And EVERY course carries a rating, so the identity cell is the same three lines on every row (name+`ag-rec` / stars+rating / "N reviews"), as the agent list always is — a 2-line cell beside a 3-line one is the ragged, uneven-row "broken structure" this fixed. E3/E4 read their figures off `ENROL_COURSE`; E1/E2 are `COURSE_RATE_PH`, an authored placeholder flagged like `COURSE_TOPICS`. (candidate, §114, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- BOOKING OPENS WITH PAYMENT, THEN THE CALENDAR (Maryam, 19 Sep 2026, two scenarios): a candidate books an open slot only after a payment method is on file, so the order is **Book → payment → payment-success confirmation → calendar → pick slot → booking confirmed**, REVERSING the old "calendar first, pay second" order and the 18 Sep "complimentary skips checkout" shortcut. The `agent:<k>` route (the marketplace card, the list "Book" button, the dashboard recommendation — all still emit `agent:<k>`, the DS builder markup is untouched) now lands on `V.checkout` (the PAYMENT step) FIRST; a NEW `cal:<k>` route reaches the calendar directly and is used by "Continue" after payment and by Reschedule (no re-charge). FIRST interview (complimentary): NO card is on file, so the payment screen shows an EMPTY-STATE placeholder (`.empty pm-empty`, the `creditCard` glyph + "No payment method added yet") — never a saved card; "Add a card" opens the Stripe modal (it does NOT auto-open, so the empty state is what the reader lands on), the page states "$0 charged today, card saved for next time". **While NO card is on file there is NO CTA** (Maryam 19 Sep 2026: "remove button when no payment method is added") — closing the add-card modal on the checkout view flips `S.pmAdded`, which replaces the empty state with the saved card (`cardPicker`) AND reveals the **"Continue to Pay"** CTA (was "Confirm ($0 today)"); `S.pmAdded` resets on each booking start. The button is gated `charged || S.pmAdded`. RE-interview (charged): the saved-cards picker (`cardPicker`), the button reads "Pay $X". Both carry `data-payok` → `S.paySuccess` → `paySuccessModal()`: the `.conf conf-ok` shell with a dedicated **Interview Fee row** (`.bkc-fee`/`.bkc-fl`/`.bkc-fv` — the amount is its OWN row, not buried in the copy; complimentary shows the standard fee STRUCK with "Free" via `ivFeeLabel(false, a.price)`, a re-interview shows the charged amount), the copy explaining without repeating the figure ("complimentary, no payment charged, method saved" / "payment successfully processed, continue to select a time"), and one **"Pick a slot"** `data-paygo` control → `cal:<k>` (Maryam 19 Sep 2026, was "Continue to scheduling"). `data-payok` does NOT book. `V.agent` (the calendar) is now the LAST step: no fee row, its foot button is "Confirm booking" (`data-book`) → booked dashboard + `ivBookedModal` ("Interview booked"). `data-book` clears `S.paySuccess`. (candidate, views.js `go`/`V.checkout`/`paySuccessModal`/`V.agent`/handlers, Maryam 19 Sep 2026)
- Booking's "Proceed to pay" no longer books; it goes to `V.checkout` — the card-picker + a "Pay $X and book" button that carries `stage:booked` (the old Proceed target). A card choice is inserted between the click and the commit; the destination is unchanged. (candidate, 6 Sep) — SUPERSEDED 19 Sep 2026 by the payment-first order above (`V.checkout` is now the first step, not reached from the calendar).
- A COMPLIMENTARY INTERVIEW BOOKING SKIPS THE CHECKOUT SCREEN and shows a SUCCESS MODAL on the booked dashboard (Maryam, 18 Sep 2026: "no need to show this screen, just show a modal of successful interview booking ... the screen the modal will open on should be the booked interview prototype screen"). `V.agent`'s Proceed button carries `data-book` when the interview is complimentary (`!ivCharged`) — it sets `S.ivBooked` and `setStage('booked')` (skipping `V.checkout`), and `ivBookedModal()` (the `enrolSheet` `.conf conf-ok` shell — success mark, "Interview booked", one dismiss control) renders on the dashboard, gated `S.ivBooked && (S.stage==='booked'||resched)`; the button/backdrop clear the flag onto the dashboard behind. A CHARGED re-interview still routes through `V.checkout` for the card picker, whose confirm now also carries `data-book` (same modal). (candidate, `data-book`/`ivBookedModal`, Maryam 18 Sep 2026) — SUPERSEDED 19 Sep 2026: payment now comes FIRST for every booking (complimentary too — the card is captured at $0), so nothing "skips checkout". `ivBookedModal` stays as the final "Interview booked" dialog raised from the calendar's `data-book`; `data-payok` on the payment step raises `paySuccessModal` before it.
- The RESCHEDULE stage (`resched`) draws the booked dashboard but the interview is TWO DAYS OUT and the action MOVES it (Maryam, 18 Sep 2026): `CALL_ROW.iv` returns `when:'in 2 days'` and a `resched` flag + agent `key` on `S.stage===RESCHED`; the black card's `.dc-when` reads **"In 2 days"**, and `crow` swaps the accent Join for a WHITE **"Reschedule"** button (`.crow-resched` — white ground §77, dark ink §63 at (0,5,0) to beat §63's dark-card `.btn:not(.btn-p)` white) that reopens the agent's booking calendar (`data-go="agent:<key>"` → `V.agent`). (candidate/`crow`/`CALL_ROW.iv`, Maryam 18 Sep 2026)
- The COMPLIMENTARY interview fee shows the agent's price STRUCK THROUGH with "Free" beside it, not "Complimentary" (Maryam, 18 Sep 2026: "show the price but cut it and write Free next to it"). `ivFeeLabel` returns `<s class="fee-was">$price</s> <span class="fee-free">Free</span>`; §63 mutes/lines-through `.fee-was` while "Free" keeps `.bkc-fv`'s accent. Booking-page fee value (`.bkc-fv`) AND the payment + calendar agent head (`.bkp` `.rec-f`, both `V.checkout` and `V.agent` — Maryam 1 Oct 2026: "instead of 'Complimentary' with payment icon, use '$95(cut price) Free' text"; the head's complimentary case drops the wallet mark and reads `ivFeeLabel(false, a.price)`, the charged case keeps the wallet + "$X Interview Fee"). The receipt still reads "Complimentary". (candidate/`ivFeeLabel`/§63, Maryam 18 Sep / 1 Oct 2026)
- THE COURSE IS CHARGED AT ITS FULL $690 — NO INTERVIEW CREDIT (Maryam, 8 Oct 2026: "course price is 690 and payment button says 595? correct this ... check that this is correct on all types of payments"). The old −$95 "interview already paid" credit (course $690 → $595 due) dates from when the first interview was paid; it is complimentary now (`IV_FIRST_FREE`), so nothing is credited. One set of figures everywhere: course fee $690 = "Pay $690 and start" = the "Successfully enrolled" Course Fee = the enrolled note = the Payments row + its receipt = the enrolPre notification; the first interview reads "$95 Free" / "Complimentary" / $0 (its notification is "Card saved … nothing was charged", not "Payment received $95"); the re-interview is charged at the agent's own fee ("Pay $95" with Priya) and is NOT included in the course fee (Tal's cost and re-interview answers, `wCost`/`wReCost`, say so). `checkoutPlate` drops its credit line. (views.js/data.js/ai8.js, Maryam 8 Oct 2026)
- THE "OPEN QUIZ RESULTS" QUICK ACTION OPENS A MODAL, NOT THE RIGHT SLIDER (Maryam, 1 Oct 2026). `quizModal` (ai6.js) is a centred `.modal`/`.sheet` showing the exact `SUMDROP.quiz` read `sumDropCard` draws in the Summary-by-Tal highlight popover, wrapped in `.sumdrop.sd-flat` (§70 flattens the floating-card chrome), with a secondary **Close** button. The card carries `modal:'quiz'` → `data-quizmodal`; `S.quizModal` gates it; `data-quizclose` + backdrop close it (`placeSumDrop` spares `.sumdrop:not(.sd-flat)` so the modal body is not swept). The highlight popover's own "Open your quiz results" action button is REMOVED (`SUMDROP.quiz` has no `act`; `sumDropCard` renders `.sd-act` only when `act` exists). `quizPeek`/`peekPanel` stay defined, reachable by nothing on the dashboard. (candidate/`quizModal`/§70, Maryam 1 Oct 2026)
- THE DASHBOARD REC BLACK CARD SHOWS THE STRUCK PRICE, NOT "Next slot" (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "instead of the next slot show the price that is $120 Free for the first interview, cut the $120 so the user knows it is free"). The `.rec-f` meta for a complimentary first interview drops both the "Complimentary" word and the "Next slot: …" chip and shows `ivFeeLabel(false, a.price)` — the agent's price STRUCK with "Free" — beside the length (`[💰 $95̶ Free][🎥 45 mins call]`). The price is `a.price` (the agent record — $95 for Priya, one price across every surface; it reads "$120" only if the record does). A charged re-interview is unchanged (fee · length · next slot). This UPDATES the 18 Sep "the rec cards still read Complimentary". (candidate/`talRec`/`ivFeeLabel`, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- The full-bleed CALL screen (`.call`) is in the rail-divider suppression list (§14, `.app:has(> .call)::after{content:none}`) with the other full-bleed scenes — else the stray left line draws down the video. (§14, Maryam 18 Sep 2026)
- THE MOBILE REPORT FLOW — a batch of PHONE-ONLY (`@container app (max-width:599.98px)`) corrections to the report page (`#day34/report`), Maryam 24 Sep 2026. Desktop and tablet are UNCHANGED (the two-column band, 32px black card and quiet ladder all stand). Six: (1) NO PAGE HEADING ON A PHONE, AND THE SUMMARY ATTACHES TO THE HEADER — a module page with a Tal summary shows NO title on a phone (not in the bar, not above the band), and the summary wash sits straight under the header (Maryam 24 Sep 2026: "remove the heading and attach the summary section with the header" — this SUPERSEDES the same day's "move the name above the summary as a `.mob-ph` title" and its balanced-spacing/reduced-size follow-ups). `markMobHead` (ai11.js) stamps `.mobph-on` on such a page (test: `.modhead .ai-aura.talsum` present, dashboard `.ph-you` absent), and §137 drops the header trail (`.crumb-bar`) below 600 via `.app:has(.view-col .page.mobph-on)`. Removing the title is all it takes to attach the summary — page content already begins at the header's bottom edge (the frame's 16px is its own padding, not a content gap). No `.mob-ph` element is created any more. A dashboard keeps its own head and its bar name. (2) THE TAL DOCK IS SHORTER on a phone — `min-height` 64→56, side padding 24→16 (`.askdock > .askline`), the comet's corner `.ai-run rect{rx}` 31→27 (§117's note: move it with the height), and the review capsule that floats above it re-pins to 56 (§130's 66→56). (3) THE "Scenes" HEADING CARRIES NO HELPER CAPTION ("The three you kept" / "The full interview, all scenes" span removed — views.js, all widths). (4) THE KEPT SCENES ARE ONE ROW at every width — `.scene-row` is `repeat(3,1fr)` throughout (was a single column below 672), gap `--s03` on a phone, `--s05` above (§38, reversing the "stack below 672" seam). (5) THE BLACK LEVEL CARD (`.lvl-hero`) PAYS 16px on a phone, not §97's 32 (`--s07`→`--s05`). (6) THE LADDER'S AHEAD LEVELS TAKE COLOUR on a phone — `.lvl-hero .ladder i:not(.done):not(.on)` is `--accent` at opacity .22 (reversing §15's "the levels behind you are quiet" for the phone; on 100% > done ~42% > ahead 22%). hifi-only (§137 in `build-ds.py` `NOT_IN_DS`); the shared dock/ladder on the other portals are left at their desktop values. (§137/§38/§97/§117/§130/§15, ai11.js, Maryam 24 Sep 2026)
- The booking CHECKOUT (`V.checkout`) MIRRORS the booking page (`V.agent`) so moving from the Calendly card to payment only swaps the card, not the width: the SAME `.bkp` agent profile head on top (full width, its hairline below), then "Pay with" + `cardPicker()` + the pay button in the SAME 830px centred column the Calendly card sat in (`.bks-w`, §76.1b) — not rail-to-rail. The pay button sits inside `.bks-w` under the picker (`.bkpay-go`, left-aligned), not in `.bkc` (whose `margin-left:auto` right-aligns it). (candidate, §76, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- A disclosure's state is `S.disc` AND a DOM class; opening one must not reset the scroller. (§65)
- A row's arrow may carry a verb label (`.row-cta`) only when it is the one thing the page behind it is for. (§112)
- A tab row is `.sec.sec-cs > .cs`, never `.tabs` inside a padded `.sec`; a queue count rides the tab in `.lf-n`. (§15/§16, admin 4 Sep)
- A list page is: heading row (title + description, its buttons on the RIGHT end — NO search here), then the `.cs.lst-filt` tab strip, then a toolbar (`.lst-tools`) with the search on the LEFT and the table's own filters on the RIGHT, then the table. The heading row gets equal air above and below (`--s07` both). (§128, Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- The tab strip keeps its TOP border on admin list pages: `.modhead + .sec > .cs` (§15) drops it under the Tal band, but the heading sits between them here, so it is restored. (§128, Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- On the SUPER ADMIN portal a PAGE-HEADING DESCRIPTION (`aiHead`'s `.aih-d` lede) is the description grey `--text-secondary` **#828282** — the colour the Tal summary body carried before it was darkened one step platform-wide the same day (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "the desc with the page heading needs to be in the font colour what tal summary desc colour is in right now"). The DS only colours `.aih-d` through `.sec > .sec-h.aih` (a DIRECT child, §63 §13), but admin list heads wrap it in `searchHead` (`.sec > .hd-srch > .hd-srch-t > .sec-h.aih`), so that rule never matched and the desc fell back to the inherited primary #111 (black). An admin-local rule (`.app .page .sec .sec-h.aih > .aih-b > .aih-d`, descendant so it covers wrapped and direct heads, (0,7,0) later than the DS base) pins every admin heading desc to #828282; the dark-card override (0,8,0, §63) still wins on a black-card heading. Admin-only, in `tn-admin-portal.html`. (§63, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- Admin tables follow the cohort leader's table whole (§31): header divider the light `--rule` (not `--rule-ink`); NO hairlines between rows (rows separated by air, `td border-bottom:0`); the first data row stands off the header by `--s06` (`tr:first-child + tr td`); cells `vertical-align:middle`; the first column is `--t-w-strong` (500) so the name is the anchor, its `idCell` caption sub-line staying book. (§128 + §63 §56, Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- EVERY `.tbl` header row (all portals) is the SAME SIZE AS ITS CELLS, in BLACK STRONG: `.tbl th` moves off the eyebrow role (11.5px) to `--t-compact` (13.5px, `.tbl td`'s own size/lh/ls) at `--text-primary` and `--t-w-strong` (500 — "semibold" is 500, never 600; two weights only). `.chart-table th` stays the eyebrow (a chart's data readout, not a listing). (§63 §56b, Maryam 13 Sep 2026)
- EVERY `.tbl` data cell (all portals) is the DESCRIPTION GREY `--text-secondary` #828282 by default, EXCEPT the first column (the name anchor, pinned back to `--text-primary`) and any cell whose colour lives on its own element — `.statw` status green/amber/red, the `.t-caption` identity sub-line's floor grey, a `.tag` chip's own ink — which beat `.tbl td` (0,2,1) and are children, so none is named in the rule. (§63 §56b, Maryam 13 Sep 2026)
- A table's filters are OUR dropdowns (`.fdd`, never a native select), one per sensible categorical column NOT already covered by the tabs (e.g. Users/Candidates: Status); they write the page's filter state via `data-filter` and open downward. `S.fdOpen` is the open one; a choice or an outside-click closes it. Where the tabs already are the table's filter, the toolbar is search-only. A filter's option labels MIRROR the words the table's own column shows (Users say "Inactive", so the filter says "Inactive", not "Deactivated"). (§128, Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- EVERY DROPDOWN ON THE SUPER ADMIN PORTAL OPENS DOWNWARD, never above (Maryam 12 Sep 2026: "dropdowns should always open below not above"). The filter (`.fdd`), form (`.fdd-form`) and rows-per-page (`.pgn-dd`) menus all use `top:calc(100% + --s02)` — the pagination menu was the one exception (upward, because it sits at the table foot) and is now downward too. The row-action kebab (`placeRowMenu`) is the one adaptive case that may still flip up as a last resort, only when opening down would hide options behind the floating Tal dock (§125). (127-pagination.css, Maryam 12 Sep 2026)
- **UPDATED 8 Oct 2026 (Maryam: "the dropdown should be opened above the content, not pushing the content down"): the slider's dropdown / date popup now OVERLAYS the fields below it (absolute, `z-index:40`, full field width) instead of laying in flow; the body keeps `overflow-y:auto`, so the scroll-jump fix below still holds (measured: no field moves, scrollTop unchanged).** ~~A FORMDD / DATE POPUP IN THE RIGHT-SLIDER SHEET (`.peek-form`) OPENS IN FLOW~~, and must NOT collapse the body scroll (fix, Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "on clicking this field it jerks me to the top of the form and stuck the form scroll … correct this whole experience"). The shared `.dd-open` mechanism flips the sheet body to `overflow:visible` so a floating absolute popup is not clipped by the sheet — but in the tall, scrollable right-slider that EXPANDS the body to its full content (clientHeight jumps) and forces `scrollTop` to 0, jerking the form to the top and freezing its scroll; picking or leaving the field jumps it back down. Fix (admin-local `<style>`, scoped to `.peek-form` so centered modals keep the floating popup): the body STAYS `overflow-y:auto` on `.dd-open`, and the OPEN popup lays IN FLOW — `.peek-form .sheet-b .fdd.fdd-form.on .fdd-menu` and `.fdate.on .fdate-pop` are set `position:relative` so they push the fields below down and the body simply scrolls to reveal them (no jump, no clip, scroll preserved). Verified: opening the LightSpeedVT select with the body scrolled to the bottom keeps `scrollTop`, the field stays put, the menu opens directly beneath it, and picking closes without a jump. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
- THE COURSE FORM'S LEARNING-PLATFORM FIELD IS LABELLED **"LightSpeedVT Courses"** (was "Learning platform course"; placeholder "Choose a LightSpeedVT course"). `KINDS.course` `lsvt` field only; the detail fact keeps its own "Learning platform" label. And the course create/edit form drops its trailing `help` info block (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "remove the info block at the end of the form"). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
- A LIST TABLE's SEARCH FIELD carries a clear (X) button at its right end while it holds text (`.srch-x`, shown on `.srch-has`): pressing it empties the query and returns the full table, caret back in the now-empty field (`data-srchclear` → `setFilter(key,'')` + `SRCH_FOCUS`). The input reserves right padding so text never runs under the X; the X is one of the armed hovers (`HOVER_KEEP`). (128-adminlist.css + lstTools, Maryam 12 Sep 2026)
- The toolbar (search) row's air ABOVE and BELOW is equal — `--s07` (32) both sides. On a list page the operative rule for the gap ABOVE is `.app .cs.lst-filt + *{margin-top:var(--s07)}` (§120.4): the tab strip sits between the heading and the search, and that `+ *` rule (0,3,0) beats `.lst-tools`'s own `margin-top` (0,2,0), so the toolbar's own margin alone was not enough — the gap above read 16 against 32 below. Both are `--s07` now. `.lst-tools{margin-top:var(--s07); margin-bottom:var(--s07)}` carries the below gap and covers the no-tab case (heading→toolbar collapses to 32, unchanged). Across every admin list table. (120-admin.css §120.4 + 128-adminlist.css, Maryam 12–13 Sep 2026)
- NO NATIVE `<select>` ANYWHERE ON THE SUPER ADMIN PORTAL (Maryam 11 Sep 2026: "i do not want any system dropdowns anywhere ... update it everywhere"). Every `t:'select'` form field — sheet forms via `fieldHtml`/`recSheet` AND settings pages via `settingsForm` — renders through `formDd(f, val)`, our dropdown (reuses `.fdd*`, like `impRoleCell`, so no new CSS). It is DOM-only: a hidden `[data-f]` input that `readForm`/`readPageForm` read unchanged, and `data-formddtoggle`/`data-formddset` handled in the NON-rendering click listener — a pick must never `render()`, or the form (read from the DOM on save) is rebuilt and every other in-progress field is lost. Opening one adds `.dd-open` to its `.sheet-b` (overflow:visible) so the menu is not clipped. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 11 Sep 2026)
- On the Super Admin portal a SUB-MODULE heading row has EQUAL air above and below (§128.1b/1c): the first content section after `.modhead` that LEADS WITH A HEADING (`> .sec-h`) takes `padding-top:var(--s07)` (32, same as a module's list page — overriding §16's `.sec:has(> .cs){padding-top:0}` that was pulling a heading tight against the band, e.g. Roles & Permissions), AND that heading's own bottom gap to its content is `--s07` too (`> .sec-h{margin-bottom:var(--s07)}`, Maryam 12 Sep 2026: "bottom space equal to the above space"), not the default `--sec-h-gap` (12). A strip-only section (no `.sec-h`) keeps hugging the band, and section headings deeper down keep the tight heading→content gap. (128-adminlist.css, Maryam 11–12 Sep 2026)
- ALL USERS IS THE ONE DIRECTORY; THE ROLE FILTER TELLS PEOPLE APART (Maryam, 7 Oct 2026). The **Candidates** module, the **Talent Agents & Marketplace** module, **Cohort Management › Cohort Leaders** and **Users & Roles › Staff Members** are HIDDEN (`hidden:true`, still defined and deep-link reachable; their detail pages stay the row targets). `usersAll` lists every account but Super Admins and employers, and the role TAB STRIP is now a **Role dropdown** (`filterDd('role')`, first in the toolbar): All · Candidate · Talent Agent · Cohort Leader · then each internal role by its own name (Reviewer · Supervisor · Manager of Agents · Admin, `staffRoleOpts(false)`) — there is NO umbrella word like "staff" for internal accounts. FILTERS AND COLUMNS FOLLOW THE ROLE (`roleGroup`): All = Status + Erasure; Candidate = Track / Level / Cohort; Talent Agent = Listing / Training / Assesses with Rank + Agent Score columns and the `rankBanner` (the old All Agents + Marketplace); Cohort Leader = Stage (Leading / Approved / Awaiting interview / Interview pending, `leaderStage` off `LEADER_REQS` + `leaderCohorts`) and Type (candidate leader / moderator), an **Invite A Leader** button and a "Remove as cohort leader" bulk action (the old Cohort Leaders pipeline); an internal role = Department + the staff columns. Picking a new role clears the previous role's sub-filters (`USERS_SUBFILTERS`). A row's menu follows the PERSON (`userRowActs`): internal → `staffRowActs` guards, agent → marketplace + account actions, a leader applicant → Book interview / Allot a cohort / Remove. The heading CTA reads **Invite User** (Maryam 7 Oct 2026) and always opens the one Add User slider, whose ROLE IS A SINGLE DROPDOWN of every role (Maryam 8 Oct 2026, replacing the radios and the red "add a reviewer… instead" link): Talent Agent · Cohort Leader · Reviewer · Supervisor · Manager of Agents · Admin (`ADDUSER_ROLES`), preselected from the Role filter when it is addable (`addUserDefaultRole`). The picked role reveals its fields by CSS off the hidden input's value attribute (agent: commission + initial rating; leader: existing-candidate picker; internal: phone, job title, department) so typed fields survive; an internal role submits through the internal invite (`sendinvite`). Every link that opened a hidden list now opens All Users pre-filtered through a ROUTE PRESET (`users/users?role=candidate`, `splitPreset`/`applyPreset` in `go`/`goBack`/`K.after`); a person's detail page lights Users & Roles › All Users in the rail and trail (`foldedDetail`). Six `LEADER_REQS` applicants with no account got authored-placeholder USERS + CANDS rows (u53–u58, §74). This SUPERSEDES the 13 Sep role-tab rule below, the Cohort Leaders table rule, the Epic 6 two-submodule surface and the 22 Sep "Staff Members is a visible rail leaf" rule, and Epic 1's "All Users is external only". (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 7 Oct 2026)
- THE CREATE ROLE FORM FOLLOWS THE CREATE ROLE STORY (Maryam, 12 Oct 2026). The `KINDS.role` slider on create is: **Role name** (required, 1–50, letters/numbers/spaces, UNIQUE across every role built-in and custom, case-insensitive — an inline error under the field via `K.validate`, which `saveRecord` now honours) · **Description** (required, 1–255) · **Permissions**, this role's column of the matrix with NOTHING granted on open: one collapsed `<details>` group per module in NAVIGATION ORDER (Dashboard, Staff, Users, Roles, Levelling, Marketplace, Cohorts, Courses, Interviews, Support Requests, Finance, Audit Log, Onboarding Questions, Settings), each with a live "n of m" count and only its own actions; then the **eight sensitive** permissions in the story's order (Approve candidate level, Approve agent score, Release payout, Change fee, Data: Export, View AI raw score, Set assessment range, Play a recording), each grantable; then the **two reserved to Super Admin** shown LOCKED (disabled box + lock glyph, never read). Actions Cancel / Create Role; the old "starts with no permissions" info block is gone. `PERM_MODULES` is now the story's set — every module View/Create/Edit/Delete except Dashboard (View), Audit Log (View), Onboarding Questions + Settings + Support Requests (View, Edit), Courses (View, Create, Edit) — so the per-role grids below use the same 43-permission matrix; Approve refund and Accounts: Delete left `PERM_SENSITIVE`. Edit keeps name + description only. Grants are native checkboxes sharing `data-f="perms"` (no render on a tick), visually hidden behind the platform's own HugeIcons glyphs: `Square` unticked (`--border-strong-01`), `Checkmark Square 02` ticked (`--accent`), the reserved two in `--gray-20` — pasted verbatim from `@hugeicons/core-free-icons@4.3.5` (`HI_SQUARE`/`HI_SQUARE_CHECK`), never the DS `.cbx .box` filled square (Maryam 12 Oct 2026: "why you are not using the checkboxes icons from hugeicons?"). `crp-*` namespace (`.rp-*` ships in the DS). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 12 Oct 2026)
- EACH ROLE'S PERMISSIONS TABLE IS A COLLAPSED ROW (Maryam, 7 Oct 2026). On Roles & Permissions the Permissions section is an `.acc.perm-acc`: one row per role — the role name over "N of M permissions granted" (M = the grantable module actions; Super Admin reads "Every permission, always") and a chevron — opening to that role's grid, whose top-left cell now reads "Module". All rows start collapsed; several may be open at once (`S.permOpen`, `data-permopen`). EDIT IS PER ROW (Maryam, 8 Oct 2026): each row carries its own Edit button LEFT of the chevron (the section-level Edit Permissions button is gone); pressing it opens that row in edit mode for that role only (`S.editPerms` = the role key) and swaps Edit for Cancel + Update Permissions; while one role is being edited the other rows hide their Edit; Super Admin has none. The row header is a div holding three controls (title toggle · actions · chevron toggle), never nested buttons. The count updates live as you tick. STARTING SETS (Maryam 8 Oct 2026, authored placeholder §74, read off each role's description): Reviewer 8/27 (views across Dashboard/Users/Levelling/Marketplace/Cohorts/Onboarding + Interviews edit, plus Approve candidate level); Supervisor 9/27 (Users + Marketplace edit, views incl. Finance, plus Approve agent score); Manager of Agents 9/27 (Users + Marketplace + Interviews edit, views of Levelling/Cohorts). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 7–8 Oct 2026)
- BULK IMPORT (`users/import`): FIRST AND LAST NAME ARE SEPARATE COLUMNS and SUPER ADMIN CAN NEVER BE IMPORTED (Maryam, 8 Oct 2026). The CSV is `first_name,last_name,email,role` (template, paste placeholder, upload copy and sample all say so; an older three-column `name,email,role` row still parses by splitting the name). The review table has First name + Last name cells, each validated ("Missing first name" / "Missing last name"). The role dropdown and the accepted-roles list offer exactly what Add User offers — Talent Agent, Cohort Leader and the internal roles (`IMP_ROLES`, `IMP_NOT`): NO Super Admin (refused "Super Admin can’t be imported", granted only through the grant flow), NO Candidate (refused "Candidates join on their own" — Maryam 8 Oct 2026, they sign up themselves) and NO Employer (refused "Employers can’t be imported" — out of the system since 19 Sep). The Done screen carries a grey "View all users" beside the black "Import more users". An imported internal role (Reviewer / Supervisor / Manager of Agents / Admin) lands as an internal, invited account. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 8 Oct 2026)
- ONE MENU PER PERSON — THE TABLE ROW AND THE DETAIL PAGE CARRY THE SAME OPTIONS (Maryam, 7 Oct 2026). `userMenuActs(u)` is the single source: the All Users row draws it whole, and every person detail page (candidate, talent agent, cohort leader, internal user, generic user) draws it minus View through `detailMenu(u)` — a detail kebab is never built by hand again. One fixed order: View · Edit · the role's own steps (agent: Pause/Restore Listing, Set Assessment Range, Override Training Gate; leader pipeline: Book Interview / Allot a Cohort; the Mark as / Remove as Cohort Leader toggle; internal: Change Role, Mark as Cohort Leader, Mark as Talent Agent) · Deactivate or Reactivate. An invited account offers only Resend Invite · Cancel Invite. A candidate with no candidate record (e.g. a moderator) opens the cohort-leader detail (`userDetailRoute`). The original Super Admin keeps its protected badge in place of a menu. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 7 Oct 2026)
- THE ACCOUNT STATUS SET IS INVITED · ACTIVE · DEACTIVATED, NOTHING ELSE (Maryam, 7 Oct 2026). On All Users the Status filter reads All · Invitation pending · Invitation expired · Active · Deactivated — Invited splits because the column prints two words for it ("Expires in N days" while the 7-day link is live, then "Invitation Expired"), and a filter mirrors the column (`userStatusKey`, `USER_STATUS_OPTS`). Listed / Paused / Not listed are NOT account statuses — they are a talent agent's marketplace LISTING state (the Listing filter + State column, Talent Agent role only, which replaces Status there). Leading / Approved / Awaiting interview / Interview pending are the cohort-leader STAGE (Cohort Leader role only). Three separate dimensions, never mixed into one Status list. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 7 Oct 2026)
- NO USER IS EVER DELETED ON THE SUPER ADMIN PORTAL — DEACTIVATE IS THE ONLY WAY OUT (Maryam, 7 Oct 2026). Every user menu (All Users rows for every role, the bulk bar, the candidate / agent / internal / generic user detail kebabs, and the hidden Candidates / Agents lists) offers Deactivate (and Reactivate), never Delete or Erase. With nothing deletable, All Users drops the Erasure column and filter and the Deletion-in-progress / Deleted status options (§60, a control that can never act is not drawn). The delete sheets (`delext` / `delstaff` / `askdel:user|cand` / `bulkuserdel`) and the erasure helpers stay defined, unreached. Deactivation therefore schedules NO erasure: `dodeactext` records only when and why (the Reactivate sheet shows those two, no "Erasure begins" countdown), the toasts drop the erasure wording, the detail pages draw no erasure panel, and Platform Settings hides the Account closure grace-period group (`numGroup('closure')` restores it). This SUPERSEDES the 24 Sep external-delete lifecycle (3.4–3.6) and the Epic 1 staff delete (1.11) for the surface. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 7 Oct 2026)
- The Users & Roles list filters by ROLE through a TAB STRIP (`filterStrip('role', …, ROLE_TABS())` with live counts), NOT a dropdown (Maryam 13 Sep 2026, reversing the 11 Sep dropdown — "take the role tabs as we had before ... remove the roles filter"). The tabs are the same role set (`ROLE_OPTS` = All / Candidate / Talent Agent / Cohort Leader / Admin / Employer — Admin is the `u.internal` bucket, Deactivated is the Status filter's job not a role); the toolbar keeps only the Status `.fdd`. When the Role tab is Cohort Leader, the table gains an "Added by" column: a cohort leader an admin added is a MODERATOR, `u.addedBy` is the adding admin's id, and the cell reads "You" for the signed-in Super Admin (ME) or the admin's name for any other (the "Added by" lives in the header, not repeated per cell; authored placeholder on the two leader accounts). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 11 Sep 2026)
- EMPLOYERS ARE NOT PART OF THE SYSTEM FOR NOW, so the All Users screen has NO Employer tab AND NO employer rows (Maryam 19 Sep 2026: "remove the Employers tab entirely, as employers are not currently part of our system"): `usersAll` filters `ext` to `!u.internal && !u.roles.includes('employer')`, drops `'employer'` from `extTabs` (so the strip is All / Candidate / Talent Agent / Cohort Leader), and the head copy loses "and employer". So the "All" count and every tab exclude employers, not just the tab. The Employer role, its seed accounts and the (hidden) Employers/Finance modules are untouched — this is scoped to the All Users list. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 19 Sep 2026)
- THE ROLES SUBMODULE IS A SINGLE INTERNAL-ROLES VIEW — no External category, no All/Internal kind tabs (Maryam 19 Sep 2026: "remove the External Roles category ... no need to maintain separate All and Internal tabs ... a single Roles view containing the roles added by the Super Admin ... treated as the system's internal roles"). `usersRoles` shows `ROLES.filter(r => r.kind === 'internal')` (Reviewer / Manager / Supervisor / Admin / Super Admin), drops the `.cs.lst-filt` kind strip and the conditional "Type" column, and the Permissions matrix columns follow the same internal set. External roles (candidate / agent / cohort leader) are self-serve product memberships, not admin-managed roles, so they are not listed. Every role added here is INTERNAL: `KINDS.role` drops its "Kind" field and create/update fix `kind:'internal'`, so a new role appears in the view. The Roles table carries NO row-select checkboxes (`table(…, {select:false})` — Maryam 20 Sep 2026: "remove the table checks from the roles table") even though it has a kebab column that would otherwise auto-draw them. The Permissions matrix edit button reads **"Update Permissions"** in edit mode (was "Done") and is DISABLED until a capability is toggled (Maryam 20 Sep 2026): `S.permsDirty` starts false on entering edit mode (`editperms`), is set true by `toggleperm`/`doperm`, and gates the button's `disabled`; leaving saves and clears it. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 19–20 Sep 2026)
- THE INTERVIEW/APPROVAL DETAIL CONTENT TAKES THE `--pad-x` INSET (Maryam 22 Sep 2026: "give the padding to the content ... a normal page padding just like any other page"). On `.iv-detail-body` pages (`marketApproval`, `agentsInterview`) the back-arrow header (`.ph`) and any headless action-button section already sit at the DS `--pad-x` inset, but the HEADED content sections were flush against the content box (the 15 Sep "align heading with content" fix zeroed them), so the headings/facts/ladder/notes read as un-padded beside the arrow and buttons. Fix: `.app .iv-detail-body > .sec{padding-inline:var(--pad-x)}` — the whole content column moves out to the arrow/button edge, arrow and buttons untouched (the back arrow keeps its normal ~12px optical hang). NOTE: this is the REVERSE of an earlier same-day attempt (pulling the arrow + button IN to the flush content edge) — Maryam wanted the content padded OUT, not the controls stripped. Scoped to `.iv-detail-body` (approval + interview detail), admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 22 Sep 2026)
- NO LABEL COLUMN ANYWHERE ON THE SUPER ADMIN PORTAL — every section stacks its heading ABOVE its content, never §10.15's 184px spine beside it (Maryam 14 Sep 2026: "none of our sections are side by side heading and content, everything is vertically aligned"). Every existing admin section already opts out of the label column by its content (kv / tbl-wrap / tile-stack / stats / …); this is now UNIVERSAL so a section built on any other wrapper stays vertical too — an admin-local rule in `tn-admin-portal.html` restates §10.15's opt-out for `.app .main .sec:has(> .sec-h)` (display:block + the `> .sec-h` reset) at (0,4,0), INSIDE the `@container app (min-width:900px)` tier (trap 3). It caught the candidate-detail Interview scenes (`.scv-row`) and Quiz results (`.adm-quiz`), the only two sections whose wrapper was not on §10.15's list. When reusing a candidate-portal component here, do NOT re-derive its label-column opt-out — the admin has none. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 14 Sep 2026)
- A NO-BACKGROUND heading-action button carries NO side padding, so the group's `--s05` gap alone sets the space between it and its neighbour (Maryam 13 Sep 2026: "remove the paddings of the buttons with no bg and then give them space the way it looks like ... between export and bulk correction"). A quiet `.btn-g`/`.btn-s`/`.btn-t` has the same 16px side padding as the filled `.btn-p` beside it but an invisible box, so the whitespace between two ghost labels (Export | Bulk Correction) was gap + both paddings (40px), reading wider than the ghost-label-to-filled-box-edge gap (24px). Zeroing the ghost padding equalises every visual gap in the row at the one 24px value. Scoped to the admin heading-action `.noic` ghosts only (`.aih-a`/`.sec-h-act`, §120) — §64's directional `:not(.noic)` go-button keeps its shaped padding. (§120, Maryam 13 Sep 2026)
- EVERY admin table (`table()`) grows a LEADING SELECT COLUMN — a checkbox on each row and a select-all in the header (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "on all super admin portal tables I need a checkbox on each row and ... the header row for select all"). It is a `role="checkbox"` BUTTON (`selBtn` → `.tbl-cb` > `.tbl-cb-b`), never a native `<input>`: one click target that the router matches (`data-sel`/`data-selall`) BEFORE the row's `data-go`, so ticking a box never opens the record, and a pure function of `S.sel[key]` redrawn each render (no stale DOM checked-state). Selection is keyed like `S.pg` (`pageKey()#TBL_SEQ`); a row's key is its id / `go` / first-cell text (content-stable across paginate + re-sort); `TBL_ROWKEYS[key]` holds the full ordered key list the SELECT-ALL handler reads (it toggles the WHOLE dataset, every page). The box is a QUIET LIGHT-GREY control square with SOFTLY ROUNDED corners (`border-radius:5px` — a stated radius-0 exception, like the admin `.tag` chips; Maryam 15 Sep 2026 "give corner radius ... it does not look this edgy"): a light `--gray-30` edge on white AT REST (Maryam 15 Sep 2026 "the color of the checkboxes should be very light grey"), and `--accent` fill + white check when ACTIVE (Maryam 15 Sep 2026 "the active state of these icons will be in accent color like before"). Box and tick are a step small: 16px / 10px (Maryam "reduce the icon size"). A SELECTED ROW gets NO background — its first-column NAME turns `--accent-text`, exactly the hover affordance (Maryam 15 Sep 2026 "on selecting a row, do not give bg to the row just make the first column text accent colored just like ... the hover state"): `.tbl tr.sel td.tbl-sel + td` (0,4,3) beats the name re-pin. The select cell hugs the LEFT (no inset, 34px wide) so the box lines up flush UNDER THE SECTION HEADING, not centred ~19px in (Maryam 15 Sep 2026 "reduce the padding of the rows from the left ... off the alignment"). Because the NAME anchor moved to the second cell, the DS's `td:first-child` ink/weight and the admin row-hover accent are re-pinned to `td.tbl-sel + td`. `opts.select === false` opts a table out (none does). Admin-local CSS (`#admin-tbl-sel`) + `table()`/`selBtn`/router in `tn-admin-portal.html`. (Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- WHILE ROWS ARE SELECTED, the floating Tal dock is REPLACED by a BULK ACTION BAR (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "change the tal field into the action bar ... the actions that could be done with those selected rows"). It shows the actions that apply, icon + text, DYNAMIC BY COUNT: ONE row shows the per-record set (a cohort → Edit / Archive / Delete); TWO OR MORE show only what applies to many at once (Archive All / Delete All — no Edit, you cannot edit two at once). A table opts in with `opts.selectKind` (a `KINDS` key) and rows carrying `id`; `SEL_TABLES` registers each such table per render, `bulkCtx()` finds the first with a live selection, and `bulkActions(kind, ids)` DERIVES the buttons from the KIND — always Edit (`rec:`) and Delete (`askdel:`), plus a status toggle for a KIND listed in `BULK_STATUS` (cohort → Archive, agent → Pause, cand → Deactivate). Single Edit/status/Delete reuse the row handlers; the multi actions are `bulkstatus`/`bulkdel` → one `.conf` confirm → apply to every selected record via the KIND, then clear `S.sel[key]`. The bar is a content-width pill centred in the `.askdock`, with the Tal field's own three-layer shadow and a height that fits its content (not the dock's tall pill); a trailing × (`data-selclearall`) clears the selection. Wired on the **cohorts, agents and candidates** tables (`selectKind`); add `selectKind` + row `id` (and a `BULK_STATUS` entry if the kind has a status) to extend — Users/Staff/Roles are not wired because their delete/deactivate use custom (non-KIND) flows. Admin-local (`.actbar`, `#admin-tbl-sel`), `bulkBar`/`bulkActions`/`BULK_STATUS`/`bulkCtx` in `tn-admin-portal.html`. (Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- A NON-KIND TABLE MAY DRIVE ITS OWN BULK BAR via `opts.bulk` — a `(ids) => [[icon, label, attr, cls?], …]` function returning its own buttons, for actions that act on something other than a record's KIND handlers (Maryam 15 Sep 2026). The **Transactions** table uses it for **"Download All"** (the `download` glyph) and **"Download as zip"** (a local `ADM_ZIP` = Material Symbols `folder_zip`, since the shared set ships no zip mark) — a prototype `bulktxdl` that toasts the count and clears the selection (no file leaves the browser, like the per-row "Download receipt"). `table()` registers a `bulk`-carrying table in `SEL_TABLES`; `bulkBar` renders a button's icon as a raw `<svg>` if the tuple's icon starts with `<`, else resolves it with `ic()` FIRST (the shared `I` proxy returns a truthy EMPTY svg for an unknown key, which blanks the mark — so `I[key]` can only be the fallback). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN TRANSACTIONS TABLE HAS NO EMPLOYER ROWS (Maryam 20 Sep 2026, extending the 19 Sep employer removal from All Users into Finance): `financeTransactions` builds a `base = TXNS.filter(t => t.party !== 'Employer')` and every tab count + the row list read `base`, so employer-party transactions are dropped from the table. That empties the **Unlocks** and **Placements** tabs (both were all-employer), so those two tabs are REMOVED — the strip is now All / Courses / Interviews / Scenes / Payouts / Refunds. The "Candidate" column therefore only ever holds a real person, and the cell is unconditionally `personCell(t.who, '')` (the earlier `t.party === 'Employer' ? idCell(...)` branch is dead and gone); `t.party` is also dropped from the search `hit`. This SUPERSEDES the 20 Sep "an EMPLOYER row stays the plain `idCell(t.who, t.party)`" handling. The employer seed rows stay in `TXNS` unread by this table (the hidden Employers module is untouched, per 19 Sep). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 20 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN TRANSACTIONS TABLE HAS A **"Scenes"** TAB (Maryam 20 Sep 2026: "add a dedicated Scenes tab ... whenever a candidate purchases additional interview scenes, record and display it here, separately from other transactions"): a `cat:'scene'` category between Interviews and Payouts — a candidate buying additional saved scenes from their own interview, beyond the free set. Same row structure and `methodMark` treatment as every other category (person cell + real photo, brand-icon method, `tag` status); the "Course" column shows the description as written ("Interview scenes ×N"). Priced by a new `FEES` line (`k:'scene'`, $15 per scene, set). Seed: three candidate scene charges (Sophia Ali ×2 / Liam Chen ×1 / Kai Andersen ×3) — authored placeholder (§74, the seed has no real scene-purchase telemetry). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 20 Sep 2026)
- A PAYOUT HAS NO "PENDING" STATUS — its in-flight state reads **"In progress"** (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "payouts will not have a pending status, it could be 'In Progress'"). The money is on its way to the agent's bank, so the payouts set is **In progress · Paid · Failed**. Scoped to the payout view by a `tag()` LABEL override, not a new status key — the money-in charges keep "Pending": the Transactions table passes `t.cat === 'payout' && t.status === 'pending' ? 'In progress' : null`, and the Payouts *module* passes `p.status === 'held' ? 'In progress' : null` (its pending bucket keeps the `held` key). The amber `warn` hue is unchanged (both `pending` and `in progress` map to it via `TAGV`). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN TRANSACTIONS STATUS SETS ARE DEFINED PER CATEGORY (Maryam 20 Sep 2026): money-in charges (**Courses / Interviews / Scenes**) are `Received` · `Pending` · `Failed`; **Payouts** are `Pending` · `Paid` · `Failed`; **Refunds** are `Pending` · `Refunded` · `Failed`. Colours via `TAGV`/`tag()`: `received`/`paid`/`refunded` green (good/done), `pending` amber (was `held`, which is retained only for the separate Payouts *module*), `failed` red. `refunded` now carries green (it used to fall through to plain uncoloured text). The seed TXNS rows were re-mapped to these keys (no more `settled`/`held`/`blocked` on the money-in/payout/refund rows shown in the table); the two figure helpers that read a TXN's escrow state (`inProgress`, and the deactivation "mid-flight payment" check) were switched from `'held'` to `'pending'` to match. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 20 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN TRANSACTIONS TABLE renames two columns (Maryam 20 Sep 2026): "Who" → **"Candidate"** (a person cell carries the real photo via `personCell(t.who, '')` — see the no-employer-rows rule above), and "Description" → **"Course"** with the leading "Course — " label stripped (`t.kind.replace(/^Course\s*[—–-]\s*/,'')`) so a course row reads as the course name alone. A non-course description (payout) is left as written. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 20 Sep 2026)
- A FAILED TRANSACTION CAN ONLY BE DELETED (Maryam 20 Sep 2026: "a failed payment means the payment didn't receive ... allow only delete action on such transactions"). A `status === 'failed'` row moved no money (a charge that never cleared, or a payout that never sent), so it has nothing to receipt or refund: its kebab shows a single **Delete** action (`asktxndel:<id>` → a `.conf` confirm "Delete <ref>?" → `dotxndel` splices it from `TXNS` + a Medium audit line; no step-up). Every non-failed row keeps Download receipt + (money-in, not already refunded) Refund. A failed charge is also removed from the **Issue-a-Refund** form's selectable list (`opts` filters `x.status !== 'failed'`), so it can't be refunded there either. Applies across all tabs (money-in charges and payouts). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 20 Sep 2026)
- AN INTERVIEW'S "KIND" IS CALLED **"Type"** EVERYWHERE ON THE SUPER ADMIN PORTAL (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "interview didn't have a kind but a 'Type', so replace the interview kind with type"). Every interview-facing LABEL that read "Kind" now reads "Type" — the Interviews register filter dropdown (`filterDd('bkkind', …, 'Type')`, "Any type"), the `KINDS.booking` create/edit field (`{k:'kind', l:'Type', …}`), the agent-detail Upcoming/Past interview tables' column header, the upcoming interview detail's `admFact` fact, and the recording-detail kv. The underlying data key stays `b.kind` and the value strings ("Interview" / "Re-interview" / "First (discounted)") are unchanged — only the visible label moved. NON-interview "Kind" labels are untouched: the Rulings Register decisions filter (blocked/open) and the grant-revoke revocation-kind field. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- EPIC 9 — THE FINANCE MODULE (Maryam, 29 Sep 2026, user-stories 9.1–9.4 + 9.8; `tn-admin-portal.html`). FOUR gating answers Maryam gave up front: (1) **transaction types = the story's four PLUS Scenes** — Course fee · Interview fee · Scenes · Payout · Rebooking credit; NO Refund type (the Refunds & Disputes module is retired, no Issue-a-Refund); (2) **filters = a TAB STRIP for the primary dimension + dropdowns for the rest** on every finance register (Type on Transactions, State on Payouts, Status on Reconciliation, Value type on Fee Settings; Status/Party/Funded-by/date-range as `filterDd`+`filterDate`); (3) **Fee Settings REPLACES Fee Configuration** — a read-only change-history log; commission stays PER-AGENT (Epic 6, set on the agent record); the platform-wide 20% split + placement + intent + sponsored fee lines are dropped from the surface; (4) **the visible Finance tabs are exactly the four the stories name** — Transactions, Payouts, Fee Settings, Reconciliation; Overview, Invoices & Billing and Refunds & Disputes are retired (off the rail, still defined + deep-link reachable). Screens-only (single Super Admin holds every permission; no `can()`). Dates in the platform convention (MM-DD-YYYY + bare 12-hour). Every export is TOAST-ONLY (`finexport`, the 25 Sep convention). The seeds carry the story fields but KEEP the legacy keys (`cat`/`dir`/`amt`/`status`/`net`/`gross`/`method`) so the dashboard revenue tiles, the payout gauge, `owedAgents`/`heldPayouts`/`inProgress` and the agent-detail earnings panel read on unchanged. Per story:
  - **9.1 THE FINANCIAL REGISTER** (`financeTransactions`, `finance/transactions`) — read-only; a row opens the detail. `TXNS` is the register (rich fields: `type`/`stripeRef`/`email`/`funder`/`sp` split/`cause`/`cr` credit + the legacy keys). Type TAB STRIP (All / Course fees / Interview fees / Scenes / Payouts / Rebooking credit); toolbar Status + Party-type `filterDd` + From/To `filterDate`. Columns: Reference (+Stripe ref) · Date · Type · Party (`txPartyCell` — person cell, or a plain cell for the **Platform** party, account type beneath) · Description · Amount (gross) · Split (`txSplitCell` — agent + platform, interview/payout only, adds back to gross) · Method (brand only, `methodMark`) · Status (`txStatusTag` — Succeeded/Pending/Failed money, Active/Used/Expired credit). Search on ref / Stripe ref / party name / email (never the card number). A **first interview** is the platform-funded 100% discount: party Platform, funder Platform, the candidate pays $0, the agent's full fee goes to escrow. A **Totals for this view** `kv` (`txTotals`) sits below: gross taken, held in escrow, released to agents, platform commission, active/applied/returned rebooking credit — all off the filtered rows.
  - **9.2 TRANSACTION DETAIL** (`financeTransaction`, hidden `transaction` sub) — read-only; `detailHead` + facts (Amount/Status/Created/Settled/Method/Stripe ref/Funder/Party/Email, provider reason when Failed) + Split (adds back to gross, "unchanged by who funded the interview") or, for a rebooking credit, its credit breakdown + source/replacement booking links + Cause.
  - **9.3 ESCROW & PAYOUTS** (`financePayouts`, `finance/payouts`) — ONE ROW PER INTERVIEW (`PAYOUTS` rebuilt from per-period). State TAB STRIP (All / In escrow / Paid / Returned); Funded-by `filterDd` + date range. Columns: Agent (+email) · Candidate · Interview (date + kind) · Amount (gross + agent share) · Funded by · State (`poStateTag`) · Releases (`poReleasesCell` — the due/paid date, blank = report outstanding = blocked; the **row markings** "Last run failed: <reason>" / "No payout destination" sit under it, NOT states). A **failure banner** (`note`) names any run that could not complete. **Release early** (row action → `SHEETS.releaseearly`: reason 1–500 → the story's confirmation → step-up → `releasePayout`, which writes a Payout transaction to the register + a High audit). **The weekly run is simulated** — `Run Weekly Run` (`askrunweekly`/`dorunweekly`) pays every `poEligible` payout (release date set, destination on file, incl. a run-failed retry; skips no-destination), no step-up (the platform paying on schedule). Totals `kv` (`poTotals`): held in escrow, blocked by an outstanding report, value of the next weekly run.
  - **9.4 FEE SETTINGS** (`financeFeeSettings`, `finance/feesettings`) — replaces Fee Configuration. Read-only history `FEE_LOG` of every fee value ever set: Value · Agent (commission rows, person cell) · From · To · Changed by (+role) · Changed on · Reason. Value-type TAB STRIP (All / Agent commission / Payout release delay / Payout run day) + date range + search; cannot be edited or cleared. **Set/Change commission lives on the agent record** — `SHEETS.commission` on the agent's Marketplace-standing panel (`agentMarketPanel` kebab + a commission `kv` line): value 0–100 + reason → confirmation ("Set/Change the commission for [agent] …") → step-up → `docommission` (sets `a.commission`/`a.commissionAt`, pushes a FEE_LOG row, High audit). The **two Money values (payout release delay, run day) live in the Platform Settings `SETTINGS` object** (Epic 11.1 — `SETTINGS.payoutDelay` / `.payoutRunDay`, read live by Payouts + Fee Settings); a Money-settings Save (`moneyCommit`) now also pushes a FEE_LOG row so the change shows here. A "Current Settings values" `kv` shows both.
  - **9.8 SETTLEMENT RECONCILIATION** (`financeReconciliation` + `financeBatch`, `finance/reconciliation` + hidden `batch`) — `SETTLEMENTS` is the settlement-batch model. Status TAB STRIP (All / Sent / Settled / Part settled / Discrepancy / Failed) + date range + search; an **ingestion-failure banner** surfaces `ingestErr`. Batch list: Batch (+provider ref) · Sent · Items · Platform total · Provider total (blank until the provider reports — an outage never shows a false difference) · Difference (red when nonzero) · Status (`batchStatusTag`); a row opens the batch detail.
