Batch detail: header facts + Re-check (`recheck`, simulated ingest — a Sent/Part batch resolves to Settled) + Mark reviewed (`SHEETS.markreviewed` — a note, changes no figure) + a reviewed note; an item list (All items / Unmatched-only filter) with a match indicator (`matchTag` — Matched / Amount differs / State differs / Missing at provider / Not in platform) linking each item to its transaction. It re-sends nothing and writes no adjusting entry — a failed payout is resolved in the payouts module. Stubbed (backend): the actual Stripe ingestion, the real weekly cron, the credit auto-issuance/expiry (Epic 8), the export files + 7-day notification links. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 29 Sep 2026)
- EPIC 9.5 / 9.6 — DISCOUNT CODES (Maryam, 30 Sep 2026, `tn-admin-portal.html`; a fifth VISIBLE Finance tab **Discount Codes** between Fee Settings and Reconciliation, `finance/discounts`, + a hidden `discount` drill-in). TWO gating decisions locked up front: **(1)** build the system-owned **automatic first-interview discount** as a special row (Enable/Disable), alongside user-created codes; **(2) screens-only** like the rest of the portal — every button (Create / Edit / Deactivate / Enable / Export) is always shown, none is permission-gated (the single Super Admin holds Finance: View, Change fee, Data: Export), and the step-up password on the fee-changing actions is still simulated (story 1.4). Two divergences from the story text, both toward the Finance module's own standing conventions (a Maryam ruling wins over a story, DESIGN.md §): **Status is the primary TAB STRIP** (not the story's dropdown), matching every other finance register; and **dates read MM-DD-YYYY** (the platform format), not the story's "12 Mar 2026". Column-click sorting is off portal-wide (13 Sep), so the story's "every column sortable" can't hold — the default Status(Live first)→end-date order is applied by pre-sorting the rows. Data model `DISCOUNTS` (`code`/`name`/`pct`/`applies` course|interview|both / `application` checkout|automatic / `start`/`end`/`limit` null=Unlimited / `perCand` / `deactivated` / `system` / `red` redemption ledger). **Status is DERIVED** (`discStatus`, precedence deactivated → expired end<today → exhausted used≥limit → scheduled start>today → live; `discStatusTag` — live green, scheduled amber, expired red, exhausted/deactivated plain). **Used count and value-given read off `red`** (`discUsed`/`discValue`), so the listing "Used" (`N / limit` or `N · Unlimited`) and the detail's Redemptions count always agree (the seed-drift trap). — **9.5 LISTING** (`financeDiscounts`): columns Code (upper-case, +"Automatic" bordered tag on the system code) · Name · Discount · Applies to · Application · Valid (`start – end`) · Used · Value given · Status · Actions; Status TAB STRIP + an **Applies-to** `filterDd`; search on code + name; `{select:false}`; a **"Totals for this view"** kv (value given away over the filtered set) below. Row actions: **View** always; a USER code Scheduled/Live gets **Edit** + **Deactivate**; the SYSTEM code gets **Enable** (when Deactivated) / **Disable** (when Live); Expired/Exhausted/Deactivated → View only. Screen actions: **Create Discount Code** (`SHEETS.disccreate`, a user code is always `application:'checkout'`) + toast-only **Export** (`finexport`). — **CREATE** is a plain form (code unique+upper-case+fixed-for-life / name / discount 1–100 / applies / start today-or-later / end ≥ start / usage limit optional / per-candidate optional), audit line on commit, no step-up. **EDIT** (`SHEETS.discedit`) is conditional: a **Scheduled** code opens every field but the code string; a **Live** code opens **only end date + usage limit** (the rest render disabled/readonly for reference), and the usage limit can't drop below redemptions already given — both need a mandatory **Reason** (1–500), then the confirmation "Save these changes to [code]? Discounts already given are unaffected." + **step-up** (two-step via `S._pending`, `dodiscedit`). **DEACTIVATE** (`SHEETS.discdeact`) is a reason sheet → confirmation "Deactivate [code]? It stops working immediately…" (no step-up). **ENABLE/DISABLE** the system code (`SHEETS.discenable`/`discdisable`) are reason sheets → the story's confirmation + step-up, toggling `deactivated`. Every commit writes a High audit line with before→after. — **9.6 DETAIL** (`financeDiscount`, `finance/discount/<id>`, read-only): `detailHead` + `aiHead`, a Summary kv (code / status / discount rule / application / valid / redemption limit / redemptions count / value given), and a **Redemptions** table (candidate `personCell` · reference · date · original · discounted · paid · funding source; paged at 5 so the shown count matches the summary; a row links to its transaction where a `txId` exists). The automatic code's redemptions read paid $0 / funded by Platform. All authored placeholder (§74 — no real redemption telemetry; the auto code's redemptions are the platform-funded first interviews). Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
- EPIC 8 — THE INTERVIEWS MODULE IS A FULL STAGED REGISTER + BOOKING DETAIL + REBOOKING-CREDIT RESOLVE + FULL RECORDINGS REGISTER (Maryam 29 Sep 2026, user-stories 8.1–8.4; `tn-admin-portal.html`). Four decisions locked at the start: **full register, keep the Levelling queue separate**; **adopt 8.4 fully**; **admin Resolve → rebooking-credit + stages + audit, candidate rebooking screen + auto issuance/expiry stubbed**; **platform date convention** (MM-DD-YYYY + bare 12-hour, no timezone). Screens-only (single Super Admin holds every permission, so 8.2's Finance-gated payment split and 8.4's Play-a-recording gate all show; no `can()` enforced).
  - **8.1 THE INTERVIEW REGISTER** (`marketBookings`, `market/bookings`) — replaces the narrowed "Upcoming Interviews" table with the seven-stage register (this SUPERSEDES the two-tab rule below AND the 20/21 Sep "Upcoming Interviews" / "no Stage column" clauses; the Awaiting-Approval tab stays gone — approval lives in the Levelling module). Stages `BK_STAGE` = Booked / Held / Awaiting approval (`await`) / Reported / Not held (`nothld`) / Awaiting candidate (`awaitcand`) / Resolved, in `BK_STAGE_ORDER`; `bkStageTag` hues via `BK_STAGE_HUE`. Columns (Maryam 29 Sep 2026): Candidate (name over its **email** via `candEmail`) · Agent · **Type** (the value is `agIvIsRe(kind) ? 'Re-interview' : 'First interview'` — the raw "Interview"/"First (discounted)" strings and the "100% discounted" caption are dropped) · Scheduled (**date over time in two rows** — `dpFmt(date)` then `slot12` 12-hour as a `.t-caption`, no timezone) · Stage · Fee (**the amount only** — `money(fee)` or "Free"; the candidate-paid + funding source live on the booking detail Payment panel) · Escrow (`bkEscrowTag` → In escrow / Paid / Returned, mapped from held/released/refunded via `BK_ESCROW`) · Report due (`bkReportDue`, only at Held) · Approval due (`bkApprovalDue`, only at Awaiting approval) · Actions. The Type FILTER label reads "Type" (the standing interview-Kind-is-Type rule). `table()`'s column-width estimate now reserves ~44px for a person cell's photo disc so a name never wraps to two lines (a general fix, all portals via the admin's own `table`). Overdue reports/approvals read the red `overdueSpan`. Filters (Maryam 29 Sep 2026): **Stage is a TAB STRIP** (`filterStrip('bkstage', …)`, the list-page pattern under the heading), each tab a live count off `base` (every filter but stage), All + the seven stages in `BK_STAGE_ORDER`; then the toolbar carries a **Kind/Type** `filterDd` and a scheduled **date range** (`filterDate('bkfrom'/'bkto', label, 100)` — each From/To trigger fixed at 100px via `filterDate`'s optional `w`). The **Needs-attention toggle was removed** (Maryam 29 Sep 2026: "remove need attention button"); `bkNeedsAttention` (Held-report-overdue OR Awaiting-approval-overdue OR Not held) stays for the dashboard attention band + page summary. Search on candidate/agent names + emails; default sort Scheduled most-recent-first; paged; `{select:false}` (read-only, no bulk). Row actions: **View** (→ booking detail) and **Resolve** (`renew` icon, ONLY on Not held). Toast-only **Export** (`bookingsexport`, the 25 Sep convention). Deadlines `REPORT_DEADLINE_HRS`/`APPROVAL_DEADLINE_HRS`/`REBOOK_WINDOW` are §74 placeholders. Page heading "Interview register".
  - **8.2 THE BOOKING DETAIL** (`marketBooking`, hidden sub `booking`, `market/booking/<id>`) — read-only, reached from a register row's View. `detailHead` back arrow + `aiHead('Interview booking', …)` with the stage tag + a kebab (Open recording where a recording exists, Resolve where Not held) in the act slot. Four panels: **Booking** (candidate·email / agent·email / scheduled / stage / kind / reschedule history / stated topic), **Recording** (none / deleted / Available + transcript + consent, 8.4 states), **Report** (by stage: not-taken / outstanding+deadline / awaiting-a-reviewer / settled / rebooking-credit / resolved), **Payment** in two parts — booking facts (total / candidate-paid / source / escrow) shown always, and the Finance part (transaction ref + agent-share / 20% platform-commission) shown because the SA holds Finance: View. Candidate emails read `candEmail` (placeholder for a non-USERS candidate).
  - **8.3 RESOLVE = A REBOOKING CREDIT** (`SHEETS.resolve`/`doresolve`/`doissuecredit`) — REPLACES the old chase / refund-and-close model. Offered ONLY on a Not-held booking (refused on Held even report-overdue, and on Awaiting approval). The resolution panel is read-only facts + Resolution "Issue rebooking credit" + a mandatory internal Reason; Apply opens the story's confirmation prompt ("Issue [name] a rebooking credit of [amount]? They have [n] days to use it." / "Give [name] a free rebooking? They have [n] days to book it." for a fully-funded first interview). Committing moves the booking to **Awaiting candidate**, sets `creditAmt`/`creditSource`/`creditDays`/`creditStart`, leaves the payment IN ESCROW while the credit is live, and writes a High audit line. The candidate's rebooking screen, applying the credit to a replacement, the automatic `REBOOK_WINDOW`-day expiry, and the automatic issuance on agent-cancel / account-deactivation (3.4/3.6/13.12) are candidate-portal + backend scope and are STUBBED (a comment + toast).
  - **8.4 THE RECORDINGS REGISTER** (`marketRecordings`, `market/recordings`) — REPLACES the lean list with the full register and DROPS the retention clock (this SUPERSEDES the 15 Sep lean-list rule and the 24-month retention / Expired / "expiring soon" model): a recording is kept until a Super Admin deletes it by hand, so `recStatus` is Available or Deleted only (no `recExpired`/`expiringSoon` seeding). Columns: Candidate (name+email) · Agent · Interview (date + kind caption) · Length · Transcript (`Generating`/`Ready`/`Failed`, a Failed row carries "No transcript, so this interview was not evaluated by Tal.") · SCENES consent (`scenesTag` Given/Withdrawn/Not asked) · Status (`recStatusTag`) · Actions. Filters: SCENES-consent + Status + Transcript `filterDd`s and an interview-date range; search on candidate name+email and agent name. Row actions: **Play** (`recplay` → logs + opens the detail Recording tab), **Transcript** (`rectx` → logs + opens the detail Transcript tab, only where transcript Ready), **Withdraw SCENES consent** (only where Given, `SHEETS.recwithdraw` — confirm "…Their SCENES stop being used immediately. The recording and their level are unaffected."), **Delete recording** (only where Available, `SHEETS.recdelete` — deletes at platform + Twilio, row stays Deleted, level/report stand). A Deleted row is not clickable and has no actions; the detail shows a "Recording deleted" state. `recplay`/`rectx` preset `S.filter['market/recording'].rectab` then `go`. Toast-only **Export** (`recordingsexport`). Candidate/agent notifications are stubbed.
- ~~THE INTERVIEWS MODULE (`market/bookings`, `marketBookings`) HAS TWO TABS~~ — SUPERSEDED 29 Sep 2026 by the Epic 8 register above (the register shows every stage; the approval queue stays in the Levelling module). Original (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "i need 2 tabs here, Upcoming and Awaiting Approval ... take back the upcoming interviews ui to the table ... the awaiting approval are the ones where the talent agent has sent the recording, level recommendation, and their notes, and super admin could see the tal's read as well, and super admin has to approve or override this decision"). A `filterStrip('bktab', 'upcoming', …)` sits under the heading; the search + the **Type** (`bkkind`) and **Talent agent** (`bkagent`) `filterDd`s serve both tabs. **UPCOMING** is the read-only register TABLE of booked interviews (`bkStage === 'booked'`): Candidate · Agent · Type · Scheduled · Fee · Escrow, `{select:false}`, no navigation — the same table that predated the 21 Sep diary list, which is REVERTED (the `.bk-row` diary, `ivChip`/`ivEscrowNote` are gone). **AWAITING APPROVAL** is the queue of submitted interviews (`pendingApprovals()` = `PAST_IVS` with `decision:null`): a table Candidate · Agent · Recommended level (`lvlChip(agentLevel)`) · Tal's suggestion (`lvlChip(talLevel)`) · Submitted · Actions, each row → `market/approval/<id>`. **THE APPROVAL DETAIL** (`marketApproval`, hidden sub `approval` in MODULES) MIRRORS THE TALENT AGENT EVALUATION SCREEN (Maryam 22 Sep 2026: "improve ... just like talent agent evaluation screen"), same flow top→bottom, wrapped in `.iv-detail-body` (the wider inter-section spacing): a light "Level approval" identity head (cand · agent · when), **Interview recording** (the agent's exact compact `.iv-rec` card from the DS — a 480px card with a 64×44 still `assets/agent/iv-recording-still.webp`, the file name, "Video recording · 48:00" and a play mark, non-clickable here; NOT the full-bleed 16:9 `recPlayer`, which blew the section width open — and NOT the agent portal's `recStill`/`IV_REC_STILL`, which are agent-local), **Tal's read** (heading only, no desc — `iv.tal` joined into one flowing read with its `<b>` highlights, then a "Tal reads {first} as a **{talType}**." line, `talType` an authored-placeholder T·A·L·E·N·T lens on `PAST_IVS`), a **Recommended level** band (agent's recommendation, Tal's suggestion, submitted/final, candidate rating over the 15-rung `admLadder`), the agent's notes (`ivEvalBlock` — strengths / growth / note), and a SINGLE primary action at the END (Maryam 22 Sep 2026: "a button at the end of the page 'Approve Evaluation'") — **Approve Evaluation** (`askapprovelvl` → `.conf` `mk:'check'` → `doapprovelvl`) beside **Override Level** (`SHEETS.overridelvl` → a `formDd` level picker + reason → `dooverridelvl`); the mid-page Approve/Override the recommended-level band used to carry is gone. A decision sets the candidate's level (`candByName(iv.cand).level`), writes a High audit line, and drops the interview from the queue; a decided interview shows the outcome text at the foot instead of the buttons. `agentLevel` / `talLevel` / `talType` / `submitted` / `decision` / `finalLevel` are authored placeholder on `PAST_IVS` (§74; piv4 is the agent-vs-Tal-differ case). The admin approves read-only (it does not write the recommendation, which is the agent's form). This aligns with the 15 Sep client ruling — the agent recommends, TalentNext confirms admin-side. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- EPIC 5 (LEVELLING) — ALIGNED, KEEP-SCATTERED, WITH 1–6 TALENT LETTERS (Maryam, 25 Sep 2026). Two gating decisions: **keep the levelling surfaces scattered** (no `Levelling` rail module — approval stays under Interviews, the ladder under Cohort Management, the per-candidate history + override on the candidate detail; the standing "only hidden modules are Levelling and Vetting" line still holds because Levelling is still not a rail module), and **adopt the 1–6 TALENT-letters model**: a candidate's standing is a TRACK + a LEVEL + a COMBINABLE set of 1–6 letters, shown `Builder — B3 (A, L, T)`, coexisting (the letters are not exclusive with the level). The six letters (`TALENT_LETTERS`, keys `T1/A/L/E/N/T2`, display chars T·A·L·E·N·T) print in FIXED TALENT order; the six MEANINGS are CLIENT CONTENT (authored placeholder §74), and the word TALENT repeats **T** (Top Performer / Team Player) — a display-ambiguity flag for the client. Helpers: `lettersLabel` (fixed-order display), `levelLetters(lvl, arr)` ("E3 (L, A)"), `standingLabel(c)`, `lettersPicker(sel)` (a `data-ltrtoggle` chip multi-select writing a hidden `[data-f="letters"]`, DOM-only per trap 9), `readLetters`, `recordLevelChange`, `levelDirection`. Candidate `letters`, interview `agentLetters`/`talLetters`/`transcript`/`filed`/`deadline`/`overdue` are authored placeholder (§74). This SUPERSEDES the single `talType` persona for a candidate's HELD standing (`talType` stays only as the read Tal names). Per story:
  - **5.1 (keep-scattered)** — no consolidated log; `candLevellingPanel` is RE-SURFACED on the candidate detail (reversing the 24 Sep "hide leveling section") as the home for the per-candidate history + override. It shows the standing with letters, a `TALENT letters` row, and each change as `levelLetters(from) → levelLetters(to)` with `by · when · Decided-on · Source`. The Audit Log stays the cross-candidate trail. No CSV export.
  - **5.2 (approval queue + detail, under Interviews → Levelling)** — the LEVEL APPROVALS queue (`levellingQueue`, page + rail leaf both read "Level Approvals", Maryam 25 Sep 2026) orders by 24h deadline (`pendingApprovalsSorted`, overdue-first). Its columns are Candidate · Agent · Recommended level · Report filed · **Due Time** · Actions (Maryam 25 Sep 2026): the "Tal's suggestion" column is REMOVED, the level is PLAIN text `levelLetters(...)` NOT a `lvlLtrChip`, and the old "Deadline" column is renamed **Due Time** and shows the LIVE time left (`dueTime(iv)`/`dueLeftLabel`) — **under 1 hour left or overdue reads `--accent` #f9423a, an hour or more reads the platform's `--in-progress` #b34d00 orange**; `dueInMin` is authored placeholder minutes-remaining on the pending rows (§74, signed so both colours show). The detail (`marketApproval`) now has **TWO TABS — "Recommendation" and "Transcript"** (`filterStrip('apptab', 'recommendation', …)`, the `.iv-tabs`/`data-filter` mechanism, no new handler; Maryam 25 Sep 2026: "this page should have two tabs Recommendation and Transcript"). **TRANSCRIPT tab** = the full `ivTranscript(iv)` (a Failed transcript states "No transcript exists … settle the level on the agent report in the Recommendation tab") — the old inline `ivtxopen`/`ivtxclose` "Read the transcript" toggle + its audit-on-open are GONE (the toggle handlers stay defined, unused). **RECOMMENDATION tab** MIRRORS the Level Change Detail's recommendation UI (`levellingChange`, Maryam: "follow the ui … on the levelling log detail page"): the `.iv-rec` recording card, then ONE merged **"{agent} and Tal's Recommendation"** `.sec.sec-noline` — the flowing `.tal-eval` read + the "Tal reads {first} as a **{talType}**." line, a 4-cell `.facts pf-facts` band (Agent's recommendation / Tal's suggestion / Final level once decided else Report filed / Candidate rating), then `ivEvalBlock` — with NO separate "Tal's read" / "Recommended level" headings and **NO ladder** (`.iv-ladder` removed, matching the levelling bar dropped on `levellingChange`), then the decision actions. THREE actions — **Approve agent proposal** (`askapproveagent`, the black primary with a tick), **Approve Tal proposal** (`askapprovetal`, hidden when there is no Tal proposal), **Reviewer override** (`overridelvl`, level + 1–6 letters + reason 1–500). The action row draws NO foot divider (`.sec-noline`, Maryam 25 Sep 2026); the two ghost buttons (`.appr-out`) carry a VISIBLE light-grey outline `--border-subtle-02` #c7c6c3 (the ghost's default `--rule` #efefef edge is near-invisible on white); the override button leads with the `edit` glyph; and the Tal button leads with the platform's STATIC Tal blob (`borbMark('appr-orb')` from the linked DS, no live state so §135 renders the flat rose blob, sized to 22px from its 288px default). (Maryam 25 Sep 2026) A decision (`decideApproval`) sets the candidate's level AND letters, records a keep-scattered history row with Decided-on (Agent proposal / Tal proposal / Reviewer override) and Source (First interview / Re-interview / Reviewer override), and audits both proposals + reason. The Level Report release and the candidate notification are hifi candidate-portal scope and are STUBBED here. This supersedes the earlier two-action "Approve Evaluation / Override Level" description above. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
  - **5.3 (override)** — `candoverride` uses the FULL ladder E1–T5 (was the MVP subset) pre-selected to the current level, a 1–6 letters multi-select pre-selected to the current letters, and a required reason; Apply Override is disabled until the level OR the letters differ (`gateFn`) and opens a CONFIRMATION PROMPT ("Change [name] from [level+letters] to [level+letters]? They will be notified.", the `S._pending` two-step → `docandoverridedo`) before it commits. The override is offered only on a LIVE level and is suppressed (with a note) while a level is awaiting approval. The commit writes a history row Source = Admin override (Decided-on / Proposed-by blank) and audits before/after + reason; the candidate notification is stubbed.
  - **5.3 modals** — the REVIEWER-OVERRIDE sheet (`overridelvl`, from the approval detail) and the candidate override (`candoverride`) lay their sections on ONE 32px rhythm — the body is wrapped in `.ovr-body` (flex column, `--s07` gap, child `.f` margins zeroed via `.app .sheet-b .ovr-body > *`, since the sheet's own `.f{margin-bottom:--s07}` alone left the kv block a different distance) — Maryam 25 Sep 2026 ("each section should have 32px gap"). The TALENT-letters picker chips are ROUND pills (`.ltr-chip{border-radius:999px}`, both modals), and the "Pick one to six …" hint is dropped via `lettersPicker(sel, {hideHint:true})` (the param leaves the candidate-override hint intact); the override sheet's closing "Overriding substitutes …" paragraph is removed. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
  - **5.4** — no dedicated read-only decision detail is built (keep-scattered: no log to open a "View" from); a decided `marketApproval` reads back its outcome and the per-candidate history row carries Decided-on/Source.
  - **5.5 (Levels & Tracks, `cohortsLevels`)** — each level now shows a per-level DESCRIPTION slot ("Description awaiting client content" until the rubric lands, `LEVEL_DESC`) and its LEADING RIGHTS tied to story 3.3's E5+ rule (`levelCanLead`: E5 and every Builder/Trailblazer level "Can lead a cohort", below E5 "Cannot lead yet"). The Edit Bucket feature is KEPT (a deliberate divergence from 5.5's strict read-only, Maryam's call). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- EPIC 5 — LEVELLING IS NOW ITS OWN MODULE (Maryam, 25 Sep 2026, reversing the same-day keep-scattered
  decision above: "let's introduce a levelling module and take levels & buckets module in it as well").
  A **`Levelling`** module (`ic:'chart'`) is surfaced in the rail **immediately after Interviews**, with
  three sub-pages — **Level Change Log** (`log`), **Level Approval Queue** (`queue`), **Levels & Tracks**
  (`levels`) — plus hidden drill-ins **Level change** (`change`) and **Level approval** (`approval`). What
  moved: **Levels & Buckets left Cohort Management** (`cohorts/levels` → `levelling/levels`, `V.cohorts`
  drops `levels:`), and the **Awaiting-Approval tab left Interviews** — `marketBookings` is now the single
  "Upcoming Interviews" register (no `bktab` strip), and the queue is the standalone `levellingQueue`
  (`levelling/queue`); the approval detail (`marketApproval`) moved to `levelling/approval/<id>` and its
  back arrow points at `levelling/queue`. The two surfaces skipped under keep-scattered are now BUILT: the
  **5.1 Level Change Log** — `levellingLog` over a platform-wide `LEVEL_LOG` (written by `decideApproval`
  and `docandoverridedo` via `pushLevelLog`, authored-placeholder seed §74), columns Candidate · From(+letters)
  · To(+letters) · Direction · Proposed by · Approved by (name only, no role) · Source · Date · Actions, with
  Source + Direction `filterDd`s and name/email search, a row opening the change detail and an **Override**
  row action (live candidate not awaiting a decision); date-range filtering is a follow-up (seed dates are
  free-text, the story marks the range Optional). And the **5.4 Level Change Detail** — `levellingChange`
  (`levelling/change/<id>`), READ-ONLY, opening with the CANDIDATE IDENTITY HEADER (the candidate detail's
  own `.idhead` — round portrait, name + verified seal, "Track · Level · email" — not an aiHead "Level change"
  heading; Maryam 25 Sep 2026, resolved by `candId` then `candByName`), then the decision facts, both proposals side by side with the decided one
  marked, the report (reasoning / strengths / growth + an AI-raw-score slot noted as permission-gated,
  screens-only), and the internal override reason; no edit control. The per-candidate `candLevellingPanel`
  history + override on the candidate detail STAYS (a per-candidate view of the same record). The standing
  "only hidden modules are Levelling and Vetting" ruling is amended: **only Vetting & Approvals is hidden
  now**; Levelling is surfaced. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- THE "DECIDED ON" COLUMN IS DROPPED from the Level Change Log AND the change detail (Maryam, 25 Sep 2026:
  "what if the level suggested by Tal is also confirmed by the agent? why do we need such column?"). When the
  agent and Tal propose the same level + letters the "Agent proposal / Tal proposal" distinction is arbitrary,
  and the **Source** column already carries the meaningful split (First interview / Re-interview / Reviewer
  override / Admin override). So the story's `Decided on` column is not shown; the `decidedOn` data field is
  still written (harmless, and the change detail still marks which proposal the reviewer settled on when the
  two differ). This diverges from story 5.1/5.4's stated column set, by instruction. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- THE LEVEL-CHANGE-LOG **SOURCE IS THE INTERVIEW ONLY** — "First interview" or "Re-interview" (Maryam, 25 Sep
  2026: "the source column ... should either be first interview or re-interview, do not let admin override it
  out of nowhere during nothing"). A reviewer override still happened DURING an interview's approval, so it
  keeps that interview's source (not "Reviewer override"); an ADMIN override (5.3) is tied to no interview, so
  its Source is BLANK ("—"), never a fabricated "Admin override" source. The Source `filterDd` therefore offers
  only All / First interview / Re-interview. `decideApproval` sets `source` from `agIvIsRe(iv.kind)`;
  `docandoverridedo` writes `source:''`. (The `decidedOn` field and the per-candidate history sub-line still
  record the override kind for their own use; this rule is the log/detail Source only.) (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- THE LEVEL-CHANGE-LOG **"APPROVED BY" SHOWS THE NAME ONLY, not the role** (Maryam, 25 Sep 2026: "do not show
  the role of the approved by person") — the log cell and the change-detail kv drop the "· Super Admin" /
  "· Reviewer" suffix; `approvedRole` stays on the record, unshown. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- A **FIRST INTERVIEW SETS A LEVEL, IT DOES NOT MOVE ONE** — so its **From is blank ("—") and its Direction is
  blank ("—")**, never Up/Down/Held (Maryam, 25 Sep 2026: "how could a person move from e1 to e2 in the first
  interview?" / "for the first interview candidates, how can we say they are leveled up, down or held?").
  Up/Down/Held needs a prior level to compare against; a first interview has none. This is an INVARIANT in the
  code, not just clean seed data: `levelDirection` returns '' when `from` is empty, and `decideApproval` derives
  Source from the DATA — `from ? 'Re-interview' : 'First interview'` (a change with a prior level is a
  re-interview; only a blank prior level is a first interview) — so the log can never show a From→To move
  labelled "First interview", nor a direction on a first interview. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- PRELIMINARY QUIZ IS A LEVELLING SOURCE (Epic 5.1/5.4, Maryam 28 Sep 2026) — a candidate's FIRST levelling
  record is the quiz result becoming current: a `LEVEL_LOG` row with **From blank, To the quiz-derived level +
  letters, Proposed by blank, Decided on "Preliminary quiz result", Approved by "System", Source "Preliminary
  quiz"**, plus a `quiz` evidence object (`{result, answers:[[q,a],…]}`, authored placeholder §74). The Levelling
  Log Source filter gains **Preliminary quiz** (`levellingLog`'s `filterDd`); the 5.4 change detail
  (`levellingChange`) renders a quiz row with NO proposals / Tal's-read / transcript (the `face` helper prints
  **System** as text with no photo, the Transcript card reads "—"), and instead shows a **"Preliminary quiz
  result"** evidence section — the derived result + the supporting answers (`cardRow` list). This closes the story
  gap where the quiz baseline did not exist. It also makes the first-interview From-blank invariant above
  CONDITIONAL: a candidate who took the quiz FIRST holds the quiz level, so their first human-approved row uses the
  **quiz-derived level as its From** (5.1: "the first human-approved result uses the quiz-derived level and TALENT
  letters"), e.g. the seed's Ava Stone — quiz `— → E1`, then first interview `E1 → E2` (Up). The runtime
  `decideApproval` path is untouched (a no-quiz first interview still writes a blank From). Seed: Ava Stone
  (quiz E1, then interviewed), plus quiz-only Noah Park (E1) and Mia Torres (E2). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 28 Sep 2026)
- THE LEVELLING LOG HAS A DATE-RANGE FILTER (Epic 5.1, Maryam 28 Sep 2026) — a **From** and a **To** date, both OUR
  own month-grid pickers (never a native date input, §3), Optional and empty by default (reads "Any"), combining
  with Source, Direction and search. `filterDate(key, label)` reuses `dateGrid` but writes the page FILTER state
  (`setFilter` + `render()`) via the `.fdate-filter` branches in the click router (`data-datetoggle` open,
  `data-datenav` month-nav — now reading `[data-dfval]` when there is no form `[data-f]` — `data-dateset` pick,
  `data-dfclear` ×). Row dates (free-text "12 Aug 2026" seed / MM-DD-YYYY `today()` runtime) are parsed to ISO by
  `logDateISO` and compared lexicographically; an unparseable date is never excluded (the story's "returns every
  date"). Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 28 Sep 2026)
- THE ROLE SWITCHER LIVES BESIDE THE PERSON-DETAIL PAGE'S KEBAB, NOT THE APP BAR (Maryam, 25 Sep 2026: first
  "a dropdown beside the menu icon", then "i didn't need the switcher here, i wanted it WITH the menu icon on the
  page" — the "menu icon" is the page's `⋮` kebab, not the app-bar hamburger). On a person-detail page for someone
  who holds MORE THAN ONE role, a **"View as …"** dropdown sits in `.idhead-act` just before the kebab, letting the
  Super Admin read the same person through each role's own detail page: **View as Candidate / Cohort Leader /
  Talent Agent / a Staff**, listing ONLY the roles that person actually holds (a single-role person shows nothing).
  It is a NAVIGATOR between the existing per-role detail routes (`candidates/detail` / `cohorts/leaderdetail` /
  `agents/detail` / `users/staffdetail`), NOT impersonation (the read-only `S.viewAs` "View As" of SA-11 is a
  separate concept). `ROLE_VIEWS` maps role→label→route; `switchPerson()` returns the USERS record for the current
  person-detail page (all such pages key on the USERS id via `S.id`); `personRoleViews(u)` filters to the held
  roles (staff = `u.internal`); `roleSwitch()` is the markup and `placeRoleSwitch()` (a post-render pass like
  `placeTopbar`) injects it into `.idhead-act`. Open state is `S.roleSw`; the menu opens downward, right-anchored
  under the pill; a pick navigates via the shared `data-go`. Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- THE COHORT LEADER HAS ITS OWN SINGLE-PAGE DETAIL (`cohortsLeaderDetail`, `cohorts/leaderdetail/<id>`, a hidden
  sub of Cohort Management; Maryam 25 Sep 2026) — the target the role switcher's "View as Cohort Leader" needs, since
  a candidate-who-also-leads had no standalone leadership view. Keyed by the USERS id; `detailHead('cohorts/assign')`
  back arrow, an `.idhead` (photo, name, "Cohort leader · volunteer" / "Moderator · admin-added", email) with a
  kebab (Remove as Cohort Leader, reusing `rmleader`), a four-fact `.facts pf-facts` band (Cohorts led / Leader type /
  Level / Member of) and a "Cohorts led" table (`leaderCohorts`, all statuses). Read-only; leader admin lives on the
  Cohort Leaders table. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- THE CANDIDATE DETAIL "LEVELLING" SECTION IS HIDDEN AGAIN (Maryam, 25 Sep 2026: "hide the levelling section",
  reversing the same-day Epic 5 re-surfacing) — the Level Change Log is a full Levelling module now, so the
  per-candidate history/override no longer needs a home on the candidate detail. `candLevellingPanel` stays defined,
  wrapped so it does not draw (`${'' && candLevellingPanel(c)}`). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- THE LEVEL CHANGE DETAIL TOP BLOCK IS AN 8-CARD GRID (`levellingChange`, Maryam 25 Sep 2026) — one attached
  `.facts pf-facts` band (two rows of four ≥900, §105.1a) replacing the old 4-card band + kv rows: **Levelling**
  (`from to to`, e.g. "E1 to E2") / **Talent Agent** (`r.proposedBy` + a small photo) / **Approved by**
  (`r.approvedBy` + a small photo — a DIFFERENT person from the agent) / **Direction** / **Acronyms**
  (`lettersLabel(r.toLetters)`) / **Date** / **Source** / **Transcript** ("Ready" with a trailing arrow → the
  transcript page, `market/recording/<bookingId>` or the recordings register). The two person cards carry a REDUCED
  18px photo (`.lc-face`/`.lc-face-ph`, smaller than the cohort head's 24px `.cl-lead-ph`). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- THE LEVEL CHANGE DETAIL REPLACES "THE TWO PROPOSALS" + "THE REPORT" WITH ONE APPROVAL-STYLE SECTION (Maryam,
  25 Sep 2026: "should have proper tal's read section and what the Talent agent proposed evaluation" → "since we
  are adding the talent agent proposed evaluation so remove 'The report' section" → "change the heading to '{talent
  agent name} and Tal's Recommendation' ... remove the other two headings and their desc ... remove the divider" →
  "the heading update was in place of Tal's read, no other heading will be there"). It is now a SINGLE `.sec`
  headed **"{talent agent name} and Tal's Recommendation"** — that heading REPLACES the old "Tal's read" heading, and
  there is no other heading and no inner divider. Under it: the flowing Tal's read (`.tal-eval`, `r.tal` rich HTML with
  its accent `<b>` highlights joined into one paragraph, + "Tal reads {first} as a **{talType}**."), then the
  `.facts pf-facts` band (Agent's recommendation / Tal's suggestion / Final level / Candidate rating), then the
  `ivEvalBlock` (Strengths / Growth / the agent's note). Every subsection sits a uniform **32px** (`--s07`) apart —
  `.tal-eval + .facts` and the `.signed` both take `margin-top:--s07` — and there is NO divider after the facts band:
  the DS forces `.app .page .sec > .signed{border-top; padding-top:--s07}` (0,4,0), so an admin-local
  `.app .lc-body.iv-detail-body > .sec > .signed` (0,5,0) drops both (Maryam 25 Sep 2026: "remove the divider after
  the blocks and keep the same gap between every subsection that is 32px gap"). The 15-rung `.iv-ladder` "levelling
  bar" was REMOVED (Maryam 25 Sep 2026: "remove the levelling bar"). Without an agent proposal (`r.agentLevel` — an
  admin override) it falls back to just Tal's read under a "Tal's read" heading. The seed LEVEL_LOG rows gained authored-placeholder
  (§74) `tal` / `talType` / `note` / `rating` / `filed` consistent with each row's own `to` level, and each row's
  `proposedBy` (agent) is a different person from `approvedBy` (reviewer/admin). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- THE LEVEL CHANGE DETAIL RUNS A FLAT 32px SECTION RHYTHM WITH NO DIVIDERS (Maryam, 25 Sep 2026: "whole page
  section gaps should be 32px. remove the section dividers"). The wrapper carries `.lc-body` (on top of
  `.iv-detail-body`), which overrides that shared page's 72px gaps and §14 foot hairlines: each `> .sec` pays
  `--s05` (16) top and bottom — 16 + 16 = a 32px gap between any two sections — the extra consecutive-section
  `margin-top` is dropped, and every section's `::after` divider is switched off. Scoped to the level change page.
  (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026) — with these refinements the same day: (a) the agent-notes `.signed`
  block drops its shipped `border-top` + 32px `padding-top` here (`.lc-body .signed{border-top:0; padding-top:0;
  margin-top:--s06}`) — "this is one section, why we have this much gap ... remove the divider after What the agent
  recorded after the interview"; (b) the Transcript card's `.lc-tx` link fills the cell (`display:flex; width:100%`)
  with the arrow pushed to the right end (`svg{margin-left:auto}`) — "the arrows in the transcript block should be on
  the right end". (An intermediate step added a `.lc-hr` divider between Tal's read and the recommendation with 20px
  above/below; it was dropped when the two merged into one section — see the merged-section rule below.)
  (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- THE COLLAPSED-RAIL LEAF TOOLTIP CENTERS ITS TEXT BOTH AXES (Maryam, 25 Sep 2026: "the text should be centered
  align from both sides" / "why it is still touching the bottom? center aligned it horizontally as well") — the
  `.sn-item[data-tip]::after` chip is `display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; text-align:center`,
  so the label sits centred vertically and horizontally in the white chip. (`#admin-rail-tip`, tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- ~~THE INTERVIEWS MODULE IS A LIST OF BOOKED INTERVIEWS, NOT A TABBED REGISTER TABLE~~ (SUPERSEDED 21 Sep 2026 by the two-tab rule above — the diary list was reverted to the Upcoming table). Original ask (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "show only the booked interviews by different talent agents ... no tabs, no tables, just keep the search and filter should be by type and by talent agent name"; off her reference — the agent portal's booked-diary row). It is scoped to `bkStage === 'booked'` (held / reported / not-held / resolved no longer appear), and the whole page is: the `searchHead` heading + search, a toolbar with **two `filterDd`s only — Type** (`bkkind`: Any type / First interview / Re-interview) **and Talent agent** (`bkagent`: Any agent + each `agentNames()`), and a `tile-stack` of **`.bk-row`** rows (the DS diary row, §31.5) — a left day/time column against a hairline (`ivChip` parses `b.slot`), a round candidate photo (`agentFace(b.cand)`), a "**{Type} · {Candidate}**" title (First interview / Re-interview via `agIvIsRe`), a "**{agent} · 45 minutes, recorded · {escrow note}**" sub-line (`ivEscrowNote`: complimentary / "$X already in escrow" / released / refunded), and a trailing arrow → the interview detail (`agents/interview/<id>`, which resolves a BOOKINGS id). The status tab strip (`filterStrip('bkstage', …)`), the stage/escrow **table**, the **Needs-attention** toggle (`attnToggle`) and the per-row Resolve action are all REMOVED from this module (Resolve/held handling lives off this list). This SUPERSEDES the 20 Sep register-table columns below (Candidate · Agent · Type · Scheduled · Fee · Escrow · Actions) — that table no longer exists; the naming rule below still holds. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- THE MARKETPLACE "BOOKINGS" SUB-PAGE IS NAMED **"Interviews"** (Maryam 20 Sep 2026: "instead of bookings name this module Interviews"). Display label only — the nav sub-item title, the `searchHead` heading, the export label, the empty state, the dashboard quick-link and the portal-switcher summary all read "Interviews"; the ROUTE KEY stays `bookings` (`market/bookings`, `PAGESUM['market/bookings']`, `V.market`'s `bookings:` view, `KINDS.booking`) so no hash link or state key breaks. ~~The parent module stays "Marketplace & Interviews".~~ **REVISED 21 Sep 2026 (Maryam: "the top header ... should be 'Interviews' and the page heading should be 'Upcoming Interviews'"):** the parent MODULE title is now **"Interviews"** (`MODULES` market `t`, the app-bar crumb), the bookings SUB-NAV label stays **"Interviews"** (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: the rail flyout should read "Interviews", not "Upcoming Interviews" — so the rail is Interviews › Interviews / Recordings & Transcripts), and only the bookings PAGE HEADING (`searchHead`) is **"Upcoming Interviews"**. The type/agent toolbar filters default to **"All"** (was "Any type" / "Any agent"). Route key still `bookings`. The register table carries NO row-select checkboxes (`table(…, {select:false})`) — it is read-only with no bulk actions, so selection serves no purpose (Maryam 20 Sep 2026: "none of the tables on the interviews sub module should have the checkboxes"). Its columns are trimmed (Maryam 20 Sep 2026): person cells carry NO email sub-line (`personCell(b.cand, '')` / `personCell(b.agent, '')`), the **Kind** column is renamed **"Type"** with the "(discounted)" parenthetical stripped (`b.kind.replace(/\s*\([^)]*\)/,'')` → "First" / "Interview" / "Re-interview"), and the **Stage** and **Report due** columns are removed — leaving Candidate · Agent · Type · Scheduled · Fee · Escrow · Actions. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 20 Sep 2026)
- THE INTERVIEWS TAB HAS ITS OWN COLUMNS (Maryam 20 Sep 2026): `financeTransactions` builds `cols`/cells conditionally on `cf === 'interview'`. On that tab the "Course" column becomes **"Interview"** and shows the interview TYPE only ("Interview" / "Re-interview"), and a new **"Talent Agent"** column sits next to it with the agent's name AND photo (`personCell`). The `kind` is "Interview — <agent>"; split on the SPACED dash (`/\s[—–-]\s/`) so the agent name comes off while "Re-interview" (its own hyphen has no surrounding spaces) stays whole. Every other tab keeps the 7-column Course layout; `table()` sorting is off so the differing column count needs no `sv` realignment. The agent name stays searchable via `t.kind`. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 20 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN TRANSACTIONS METHOD COLUMN SHOWS THE BRAND ICON ONLY, never the card number (Maryam 20 Sep 2026: "represented only by its payment provider/card icon ... Do not display the card number, last four digits ... use real coloured icons"). `methodMark(t.method)` renders the provider's real brand logo from `METHOD_ART` (SVG recreations of Maryam's reference logos, 20 Sep 2026 — a pasted image can't be embedded, so the marks are drawn in brand colours, no surrounding card box): **VISA** the classic navy wordmark with the gold flag; **Mastercard** the bare red/amber interlocking discs; **Amex** the blue box (not in the reference set, kept for Amex rows); **Stripe** the `#635BFF` wordmark, shown for an ACH / bank transfer (`METHOD_ART.ach`/`.bank` = the Stripe mark — a bank transfer carries no card brand, so the processor's mark stands in). Keyed by `methodBrand()` (the first word of `t.method`, before the bullet); an unknown provider falls back to the brand word, none to a dash. The last-four in `t.method` is never rendered AND never searchable (the search `hit` reads `methodBrand(t.method)`, not the full string). Scoped to the Transactions module only; payouts/invoices/statements keep their own columns. Admin-local (`METHOD_ART`/`methodMark`, `.pay-mk` CSS). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 20 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN PAYOUTS PAGE (`financePayouts`) drops the manual REVIEW-HOLD (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "remove hold option ... the hold state will be removed from wherever it is mentioned"): the `review` status, the "Hold For Review" / "Clear Hold" buttons and their handlers (`askhold`/`dohold`/`askclearhold`/`doclearhold`), and the "Under review" tab are all gone; a payout is either pending settlement or settled. The `held` STATUS KEY stays (the pending-settlement bucket the Release action operates on) but DISPLAYS as **"Pending"** everywhere via `TAGLABEL.held='Pending'` (payouts + a transaction in escrow), keeping the amber hue — so the word "Held"/"hold" is off the UI. The tabs are just **"Payments"** (the `held` bucket, renamed from "Held" and the default) and **"Settled"** — NO "All" tab (Maryam 15 Sep 2026). The per-row **Release** and the header **"Release All"** (renamed from "Release All Held") are NO-BACKGROUND ACCENT LINKS — `.acc-link` (transparent, `--accent-text` label + accent arrow, flush to the edge), not the black `.btn-p` (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "remove the black bg of the release button and give the text and arrow the accent color"). Seed: the two `review` payouts became `settled` (a $0 first-interview discount) and `held` (a net still owed). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- On the SUPER ADMIN portal a headed section's HEADING SITS FLUSH WITH ITS CONTENT on BOTH sides (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "the roles heading row has extra padding from both left and right sides, please align them with other content"). The universal label-column opt-out rule (`.app .main .sec:has(> .sec-h) > .sec-h`, (0,5,0), later than the DS link so it decides the heading's padding) had copied the DS opt-out's `padding:0 var(--pad-x)`, which DOUBLE-inset the heading — the section already pads `--pad-x` and the table/kv content sits flush at that edge — so the title read 32px right of its table and a heading action (New Role, Add User…) 32px left of the table's right edge. It is `padding:0` now: title on the content's left edge, the action button on the content's right edge; the tab strip (`.cs.lst-filt`) still bleeds past both by design. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- EVERY SUPER ADMIN DETAIL PAGE PUTS ITS HEADER OPTION ACTIONS IN A KEBAB MENU, never a strip of inline buttons at the top-right (Maryam 18 Sep 2026: "i told you millions of times that i do not want option actions on detail pages on the top right, use the menu icon and show these actions in a menu dropdown … do this on the whole admin portal"). The head block's actions (Edit / Delete / Archive / Deactivate / Verify / Approve / Publish / Reset Password / View As / Grant SA / Change Role / Download Transcript / etc.) are built as `rowActs([[icon,label,attr], …])` and passed into the `aiHead` act slot (or `.idhead-act`), exactly like the candidate detail's kebab. A STATUS/STATE tag is NOT an option — a `tag()`/`verifyTag()`/`leadStageTag()` state chip, an "Active · week N"/"Archived" tag, a "Deactivated" tag, or a "protected" badge stays VISIBLE, placed before the kebab in a `display:flex;gap:var(--s03)` span. This is portal-wide: `candidatesDetail` (the reference), `usersStaffDetail`/`staffDetailView`, `agentsDetail`, `cohortsDetail`, `cohortsCourseDetail`, `marketRecordingDetail`, `employersDetail`, `vettingAppDetail`, `vettingEmpDetail`, `crmLeadDetail`, `crmCampaignDetail`. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 18 Sep 2026)
- ON THE SUPER ADMIN PORTAL A DETAIL PAGE'S BACK ARROW RETURNS TO THE SCREEN YOU CAME FROM, not a hardcoded list (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: an interview opened from the Interviews list sent Back to the agent detail; "walk through the whole portal and check back arrows"). A `S.hist` stack (added to state) mirrors the candidate portal's §78: `go()` pushes the route it is leaving (skipping no-ops and consecutive duplicates, capped at 30); `goBack(fallback)` pops it and navigates WITHOUT pushing (a back that pushed would ping-pong). `detailHead`'s `.ph-back` button is now `data-back="<fallback>"` (the fixed list it always carried), and the click router calls `goBack(d.back)` — so the arrow returns to the previous screen and only falls back to the fixed list for a deep link with an empty stack. One fix covers every `detailHead` page portal-wide. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- A row's actions are ONE kebab (`overflow` glyph) under an "Actions" column, opening a menu of labelled rows (`.rowmenu` → `.rowmenu-list` of `.rowmenu-i` icon+label), never a strip of separate icon buttons. `S.rowMenu` is the open id; the item keeps its own `data-*`, so a press still runs the action and closes the menu; outside-click closes it. `placeRowMenu` lifts the open list out of the sideways-clipping `.tbl-wrap` into `.main`, opens it up or down by the room below the kebab (the Tal dock's top is the usable foot), and clamps it inside the frame — never `position:fixed` (the prototype frame's fit-to-pane transform flips its containing block). A same-page `render()` preserves `.main` scroll and every `.tbl-wrap` scrollLeft, so opening a menu never jumps the table. (§125, Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- A table shows ONE page at a time — 5 rows by default, resizable (5/10/25/50) — never the whole list as a block. `table()` slices after sorting; the `.pgn` bar (rows-per-page · range · prev/numbers/next) sits under the table, outside `.tbl-wrap`. State is `S.pg[key]`, keyed by page AND a per-render table index (`TBL_SEQ`) so two tables on one page paginate independently; a size change resets to page 1; the page clamps when a filter shrinks the list; no bar when the list fits the smallest page (≤5). Rows-per-page is OUR dropdown (`.pgn-dd`, never a native select) — the trigger + a menu that opens DOWNWARD like every other dropdown (Maryam 12 Sep 2026, was upward); `S.pgOpen` is the open one, a choice or an outside-click closes it. Page numbers are `.btn`, small ROUND, the active one a red circle (`.pgn .btn-p{background:--accent;border-radius:999px}` — like the count badges); §63 owns the type, §64's trailing arrow is suppressed. (§127, Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- A table's ROW-SELECT CHECKBOXES appear ONLY on tables that have a row-action (kebab) column (Maryam 15 Sep 2026): `table()` derives `doSel` from `cols.some(c => c.act)` — a read-only table (no actions) draws no select column, header or per-row. `opts.select` still forces it either way. This reverses the 15 Sep "a checkbox on every table". (`table()` in tn-admin-portal.html)
- A table's COLUMNS DIVIDE THE FULL WIDTH — the `<colgroup>` is PROPORTIONAL (percentages of the content-sized total, sel column included), so `width:100%` fills the container instead of leaving space on the right, while the columns keep their content-proportional ratios stable page to page; `min-width` (the px sum) still lets `.tbl-wrap` scroll a genuinely-too-narrow table sideways. (Maryam 15 Sep 2026, `table()` in tn-admin-portal.html)
- THE SUPER ADMIN INTEGRATIONS LIST BREATHES — its `.cardrow` service rows carry a symmetric `--s06` (24) top and bottom (up from the shared 12) with `min-height:0`, so every row has equal air above and below its content and the divider (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "add some gaps between all rows and dividers equally"). Scoped to that one list via `.intg-stack` on the `.tile-stack`, admin-local (`#admin-row-hover` block), so no other `.cardrow` list moves. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026) — THE SERVICE-ICON CHIP CARRIES A LIGHT-GREY GROUND, `--layer-02` #f7f7f7 (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "the bg of these icons should be light grey"): `.cardrow-ic` ships transparent, and the DS zeroes it for any `.sec:has(> .tile-stack)` list at (0,4,0), so an admin-local `.app .main .sec:has(> .intg-stack) .cardrow-ic` restores it at (0,5,0) — a deterministic win by weight, no `!important`. Scoped to this list, so the dashboard's attention/decision `.cardrow-ic` marks stay bare §29 glyphs. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- THE INTEGRATIONS "Connect a service" FORM OPENS AS THE RIGHT SLIDER, not a centred modal (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "this form should also come as the right slider"). `'connect'` is added to `SIDE_SHEET_KEYS`, so `sheetView`/`sidePeek` render it as the in-flow `.peek` column like Add User / Add Staff. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- NO DIVIDER AFTER THE TOP SECTION BLOCK on any Super Admin detail screen — the identity/facts block right after the head band (`.modhead + .sec`). §14 draws a foot rule on any section that precedes a heading; an admin-local rule (`[data-portal="admin"]`, inside the ≥900 tier, `:not(#_)` to clear §14's high `:has()`/`:not()` specificity without `!important`) suppresses just the top block's. (Maryam 15 Sep 2026, tn-admin-portal.html)
- NO NATIVE DATE INPUT ON THE SUPER ADMIN PORTAL — every `t:'date'` field renders through `formDate`/`dateGrid` (our compact month-grid popup, opening on the RIGHT under a calendar icon), the same principle as `formDd` for selects. DOM-only (never `render()`): picking writes the hidden `data-f` input as ISO `yyyy-mm-dd` (what native stored) and an MM-DD-YYYY trigger label; the viewed month lives in the popup's `data-ym`. `data-datetoggle`/`data-datenav`/`data-dateset`. (Maryam 15 Sep 2026, tn-admin-portal.html)
- A `formDd` SELECT MAY START UNSELECTED, AND A RECORD SHEET'S SUBMIT BUTTON GATES ON ITS REQUIRED FIELDS (Maryam 20 Sep 2026: "nothing should be selected automatically in the first 2 fields ... in that case the button will be disabled"). A `t:'select'` field that carries a `ph` renders EMPTY — `formDd` shows the `ph` as placeholder text (`.fdd-val.ph`, `--text-placeholder`) and the hidden `[data-f]` holds `''` — instead of auto-picking the first option; a select WITHOUT `ph` still defaults to `opts[0]`, so this is opt-in and no existing select moved. `recSheet` returns `gate:true` + an initial `disabled` (any required field empty at open), and the footer submit button is stamped `data-submit="1"`; `syncSheetSubmit()` re-checks the same required-field rule `saveRecord` enforces and toggles `btn.disabled` — called after every `render()` and, WITHOUT a render (trap 9), on every dropdown pick (`data-formddset`), date pick (`data-dateset`) and keystroke (the `input` listener, gated to `.sheet-b [data-f]`/`.peek-form [data-f]`). So a sheet opens with its button disabled until every required field is filled and disables again if one is cleared; an EDIT sheet (fields prefilled) opens enabled. The refund sheet has TWO entry points (Maryam 20 Sep 2026): the generic **"Issue a Refund"** button opens BLANK ("do not select the payment refund automatically"), while a specific **row's Refund action pre-fills that charge and its amount** ("the form should have that record's selections already"). A dedicated flag `S.refundTxn` — set ONLY by `startrefund`, cleared by the generic `data-sheet` open and on sheet close — disambiguates the two (no reliance on a lingering `S.id`); `fields` reads it for the `txn`/`amount` defaults. `rtype` (Type) stays blank in both cases — it is a refund attribute, not a property of the charge. The submit button still gates on all required fields, so a pre-filled row refund is enabled only once Type + Reason are added. **The placeholder modifier class is `.fdd-ph` (and the date picker's is `.fdate-ph`), NEVER a bare `.ph`**: `.ph` is the DS page-header component (`padding:40px 16px 24px` + decorative `::before`/`::after`), so a bare `.ph` on `.fdd-val` inherited that and rendered the field ~87px tall with a big gap under the label — the "odd/broken" Type field (Maryam 20 Sep 2026). Portal-wide for record sheets, admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 20 Sep 2026)
- A DROPDOWN OPTION MAY CARRY A FACE — `formDd` options are `[value,label]` or `[value,label,imgSrc]`; the 3rd element renders a round avatar (`fddAv`) in the option and the trigger (`vl.innerHTML`, not textContent). The Leader picker (`leaderOpts`) shows each leader's `agentFace`. A MEMBER ROW's avatar takes a photo via `memRow(...,img)` → the DS's `.mem-av.mem-ph > .av-ph > img` structure (NOT a bare img — the DS's `.mem-av.mem-ph{width:auto}` collapses one). (Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- COURSE COVERS + COMPLETION-CERTIFICATE BADGES now ship in the design system — `COHORT_ART` and `CERT_ART` embedded by `build-ds.py` off `hifi/build/cohorts`+`/certs` (like `PF_ART`), in `DS_HAVE`. The Super Admin candidate detail draws a Courses accordion (cover + name + status; a completed course opens to its black completion-certificate card + the four-figure insight band) like the candidate portal's My Courses; each cohort member shows a per-member course (`memCourse`). (Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN COHORT FORM (`KINDS.cohort`): a "Maximum members" field (`cap`, default 10 — every "/10" reads `c.cap||10`), the "Cycle start" date field uses our date picker, the create button reads "Create Cohort", and the list's add button reads "Add New Cohort". The cohort DETAIL shows a four-block `.facts pf-facts` band (Leader / Cycle start / Progress / Members — no Length row), NO Interventions section, and a "Cohort Members" roster (photos + per-member course; each member's kebab has Open record / Move / Remove — Move opens `moveco:cohort:member` with the member locked). The Course Catalogue drops "Import From Library" and its "Add Course" hands off to LightSpeed (no in-portal add). Cohort Leaders (`cohortsAssign`) is a TABLE (no bootstrap-paradox banner; tabs All / Awaiting Interviews / Interview Pending; the "All"/approved pool carries no recommendation prose). (Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN COHORT LEADERS TABLE (`cohortsAssign`) treats its rows AS cohort leaders (Maryam 15 Sep 2026): the first column is **"Cohort Leader"** (was "Candidate"), and a **"Current cohort"** column names the cohort(s) each currently leads — `leaderCohorts(name)` = any non-archived `COHORTS` whose `leader` (minus its "(bucket)" suffix) is this person (a candidate-leader leads one, a moderator several), or "—". Row actions: **"Allot a cohort"** shows ONLY for a leader not currently leading one (gated on `leaderCohorts(...).length`); the removal action is **"Remove as cohort leader"** (was "Decline") — `askremovelead`/`removeAsLeader` revokes the leader (any cohort they lead returns to Unassigned, the `leader` role comes off their account, they leave the queue; High audit). The table also drives its own **bulk action bar** via `opts.bulk` — "Remove as cohort leader" over the selection (`bulkleadremove` → one `.conf` → `removeAsLeader` each); rows carry `id:r.id` so the select keys are ids. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN COURSE DETAIL (`cohortsCourseDetail`, `cohorts/coursedetail/<id>`): NO Tal summary (`detailHead` with no `sum`, back arrow only). It leads with an `.idhead` carrying the course COVER on the LEFT of the name (`.crs-idphoto`, a 96×64 rectangular artwork thumb via `courseArtAdm` off the first mapped level — a course is not a person, so no disc), name only, NO track subtitle (anyone can take the course, so which track it is for is not shown). The actions ride the right (`.idhead-act`): Edit and **"Delete Course"** (was "Delete"); the RETIRE button is gone (a live course is retired from the catalogue kebab), Publish stays for a draft. The Track / Maps-to-levels / Content rows are removed; Price / Length / Status show as THREE blocks in a row — the candidate-detail `.facts pf-facts` band with a `.crs-facts` 3-across modifier (repeat(3,1fr) at ≥900, so the row fills with no empty trailing cell), Status the `tag()` word in the third cell. The EDIT window (`KINDS.course.fields`) drops the Track and Maps-to-levels fields to match — name / price / length / status only; the seed's `track`/`levels` keys stay on the record (the catalogue Track column and the cover derivation still read `track`), and `create` defaults `track:'Explorer'` so a course still resolves a cover. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN LEVELS & BUCKETS page (`cohortsLevels`): FIVE levels per bucket (E1–E5 / B1–B5 / T1–T5, `[1,2,3,4,5]`, was three). The divider goes AFTER each bucket, not between its level rows (Maryam 15 Sep 2026): each bucket is a `.lvl-bucket` whose child `.kv` rows drop their per-row hairline (`border-bottom:0`) and which carries one `--rule` line below the block (`:not(:last-child)`), so a bucket reads as one clean list. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- A paginated table's COLUMN WIDTHS HOLD ACROSS PAGES — the columns must not re-size to each page's content as you page through (Maryam 12 Sep 2026). `table()` sizes the columns ONCE off the whole dataset (`ordered`, every row, not the visible slice) — a text-length estimate per column, header included, clamped 72–340px (the kebab `act` column a fixed 56) — and pins them with `table-layout:fixed` + a `<colgroup>` on `<table class="tbl" style="width:100%;min-width:<colSum>px">`. Same colgroup on every page → the page-1 grid, held steady. `width:100%` fills the container when it can (columns scale up on the same ratios, no gratuitous scroll); `min-width` lets `.tbl-wrap` scroll sideways when the container is genuinely too narrow, never squeezing a column below its content width. Different tables get different, content-proportional profiles; within one table every page is identical. (`table()` in tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 12 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN PORTAL IS ALIGNED TO THE USER-STORIES DOC (Talentnext_UserStories, 11 epics / 50 stories; Maryam 15 Sep 2026, "align our super admin portal according to these user stories"). Build every story's flow on EXISTING components, keep copy scoped to the doc (no filler sections, no invented info). Two standing rulings override the doc: (1) ~~the talent agent's call on a candidate's level is FINAL~~ **REVERSED 15 Sep 2026 (client): the agent does NOT set the level — Tal proposes and the final level is set admin/moderator-side after analysis.** The Levelling decision surface now LIVES in the admin portal (Maryam's active build); the agent portal owns none of it. See §3 "Talent Agent — V1 model". The candidate detail still shows level history, and the admin sets the level rather than only overriding it. (2) The only hidden nav module is **Vetting & Approvals** (Levelling was surfaced as its own module 25 Sep 2026 — see the Epic 5 Levelling-module rule); everything else is surfaced (CRM, Employers, Finance Transactions/Payouts, the System tree incl. Audit Log). Drill-in detail pages (staffdetail/detail/coursedetail/…) stay hidden — opened from a row, not the rail. Candidates and Talent Agents stay as their own top-level modules (not folded into one Users directory). (MODULES, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN "ONBOARDING QUESTIONS" MODULE is a FORM BUILDER for the intake quiz (Maryam 22 Sep 2026). It sits **directly after Interviews** in `MODULES` (`{k:'onboarding', t:'Onboarding Questions', ic:'document'}` — `ic:'document'`, NOT `quiz`: the DS icon set has no `quiz` glyph so it rendered blank; check `ic(name)` resolves to a path, not the empty-svg fallback, trap 7). One visible sub `questions` (rail leaf), no hidden page — the editor is the **right slider**. `ONBOARDING_Q` holds the questions (`{id, text, type, weight, options:[{label, score}], placeholder?, other?}`), seeded with the client's five. **Three answer types** (`OB_TYPES`): `single` (scored options + a per-question weightage), `text` (one-line free text with a placeholder, no weight), `dropdown` (a fixed option list, unscored, optional "Other" with a text field). The list (`onboardingQuestions`) is a `table()` inside **`<section class="sec">`** (every admin list is wrapped — without it the heading loses the `.modhead + .sec` top-padding and §128 layout) — # / Question / Answer type / Options / Weight / Actions (Edit + Delete kebab), an **Add Question** primary button, and a "Quiz total: up to N points across M scored questions" line (`obMaxScore` = Σ weight × best option score). The **editor is the RIGHT SLIDER** (`SHEETS.obedit`, key in `SIDE_SHEET_KEYS`, rendered by `sidePeek`) — like Add User / Staff, NOT a page (Maryam 22 Sep 2026). A row (`sheet:'obedit:<id>'` — `table()` now renders a row's `sheet` as `data-sheet` on the `tr`, like `go`) and the Edit kebab and Add Question (`obedit:new`) all open it. A working copy lives in `S.qEdit` (loaded once per id, kept across in-place renders — trap 9; cleared on sheet close). A segmented **Answer type** control (`.qb-types`, `data-act="obtype:<v>"`) swaps the form; text/weight/placeholder written caret-safe via a `data-qbf` input branch (no render); the add/remove **option list** uses `data-qbopt="<i>:<field>"` (caret-safe) + `oboptadd`/`oboptdel`. The in-slider actions RETURN `'keep'` so `doAction` does not close the sheet; `obsave` writes to `ONBOARDING_Q` + an audit line and lets `doAction` close it. **Classes are `qb-*`, NOT `ob-*`** — the design system already ships `.ob-opt` for the candidate onboarding SURVEY (§107, a pill), so `ob-*` here dressed every option row as a pill. Scores/weights are AUTHORED PLACEHOLDER (§74). Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 22 Sep 2026)
- THE STAFF MEMBERS SUB-PAGE IS A VISIBLE RAIL LEAF under Users & Roles (Maryam 22 Sep 2026: "bring back the hidden staff members submodule"). The `{k:'staff', t:'Staff Members'}` entry in `MODULES` is NOT `hidden` — the Users & Roles sub-nav is All Users › Staff Members › Roles & Permissions (Bulk Import and the staffdetail drill-in stay hidden). This matches the standing MODULES ruling ("the only hidden nav modules are Levelling and Vetting & Approvals") — the internal-staff directory is a surfaced sub-page, reached from the rail, not just a deep link. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 22 Sep 2026)
- THE AGENT/USER INVITE FORMS CAPTURE FIRST + LAST NAME SEPARATELY, AND COMMISSION IS OPTIONAL (Maryam, 28 Sep 2026: "in all these forms take first name and last name separately … make commission not required"). Applies to the **Add a talent agent** invite (`KINDS.agent`), the **Add a user** slider (`SHEETS.adduser`, both the Talent Agent and Cohort Leader paths) and **Mark as Talent Agent** (`SHEETS.markagent`): the single "Full name" field is split into two required `first` / `last` fields (`splitName`/`joinName` helpers; `KINDS.agent.fields` prefills each with `get: x => splitName(x.name).first|last` on edit, `create`/`update` and `submitadduser`/`submitmarkagent` rebuild the name with `joinName`). The **Commission (%)** field drops `req:1` (still `def:20`, ph "…defaults to 20%") and is removed from every gate — `adduser`'s `gateFn` now needs `first && last && email && rating` for an agent (`first && last && email` for a leader/leader-invite), and the `markagent` submit gates on rating only; commission defaults to 20% when left blank. Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 28 Sep 2026)
- EVERY SUPER ADMIN FORM THAT TAKES A PERSON'S NAME USES FIRST NAME + LAST NAME, never one "Full name" (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "on all super admin forms where we are taking full name, change it to first name and last name"). Extends the 28 Sep split to the last two person forms that still had a single field: **Add/Edit Candidate** (`KINDS.cand`) and **Add/Edit User** (`KINDS.user`) — each `{k:'name', l:'Full name'}` becomes two required `{k:'first'}` / `{k:'last'}` fields (`get: x => splitName(x.name).first|last` on edit), and their `create`/`update` rebuild the stored single `name` with `joinName(v)`. Non-person `k:'name'` fields (Role / Cohort / Course / Company / Department / Campaign / Discount / Service / holiday names) are unchanged. Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN ADD-USER FORM (`SHEETS.adduser`, the `users/users` slider) offers only **Talent Agent** and **Cohort Leader** as roles (Maryam 24 Sep 2026: "remove candidate and employer options in the roles") — candidates self-serve and employers are out of the system (19 Sep); `roleOpts` filters `ROLES` to `['agent','leader']` and the shared `ROLES` array is untouched (filters, permissions matrix and every other surface keep the full set). Picking **Talent Agent** reveals a `.adduser-agentonly` block — a **Commission (%)** field and a 5-star **Initial rating** picker (`starPick('rating')`, story 3.2, Maryam 24 Sep 2026: "take the commission percentage and the 5 stars rating option as well so the user can set their initial rating") — so a new agent carries its per-agent commission and starting Agent Score from creation (`submitadduser` sets `rec.commission`/`commissionAt`/`rating`/`interviewed`, the KINDS.agent shape, commission defaulting to 20% when blank; this REVERSES the 24 Sep "1–5 starting score deliberately NOT captured" note FOR THE ADD-USER PATH — the score is now the visible gold star Agent Score). The block reveals by pure CSS (`.adduser-form:has(input[value="agent"]:checked)`), like the Cohort-Leader candidate picker, so a radio change fires no render() and the half-typed form survives. The star picker is radio siblings + `flex-direction:row-reverse` so `input:checked ~ label` fills the checked star and every lower one (hover previews the same); `data-f="rating"` is a radio group `readForm` reads. The **Cohort-Leader "Choose an existing candidate"** picker shows each candidate's EMAIL right-aligned on the option row (Maryam 24 Sep 2026: "show candidate email addresses on the same row of the name at the very right in the dropdown") — the email rides `formDd`'s 4th tuple element (`.fdd-meta`, `margin-left:auto`), read off the USERS record. **A "Send Invite" creates an INVITED account** — `KINDS.user.create` opens the new agent/leader at `status:'invited'` with a dated `invitedOn` (was `status:'active'`), so the directory shows the 7-day **"Expires in N days"** countdown (`extInviteStatus`, matching the staff invite) rather than "Active" (Maryam 24 Sep 2026). The **Users row menu no longer offers "Merge into another account"** — the merge sheet/`askmerge`/`domerge` stay defined but unsurfaced (Maryam 24 Sep 2026: "hide merge into another acc option"). A radio-group form-draft bug in `render()`'s side-sheet preserve (snapshotted radios last-value-wins and restored `.value` not `.checked`, flipping the Role radio to the last option on any mid-flow re-render) is fixed by snapshotting/restoring the CHECKED member by `[data-f, value]`. Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 24 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN "INVITE TALENT AGENT" FLOW (Talent Agents page, `KINDS.agent`; Maryam 24 Sep 2026). The page CTA and the form submit both read **"Invite Talent Agent"** (was "Add Talent Agent"); `KINDS.agent.create` makes an **invited** account (`status:'invited'` + dated `invitedOn`, not approved-active), so the "Expires in N days" countdown shows until accepted. The form is **name / email / commission / initial rating** — Current role (`job`) and Interview fee (`fee`) fields are REMOVED (the agent sets their own fee at onboarding), and the info banner is REMOVED. The **initial rating** is a 5-star picker like the Add User agent invite: a new `t:'stars'` field type (`fieldHtml` → `starPick(key, cur)`, radio siblings + row-reverse fill), captured on the invite only (`...(a ? [] : [rating])`), read into `rec.rating`. Submitting **stays on the agents list and closes the sheet** — `saveRecord` honours `K.stayOnCreate` (skips the detail navigation) with `K.createdMsg`/`K.createdAudit` for invite wording; the row lands in the new **Invited tab**. The Talent Agents page gains an **Invited** filter tab (`agState` returns `'invited'` for `status:'invited'`; `AG_STATE.invited` amber "Invited"); an invited row shows the `extInviteStatus` "Expires in N days" in the Listing column and View / Resend invite / Cancel invite actions. It also gains a **Deactivated** tab — a deactivated ACCOUNT is distinct from a paused LISTING (both sit at `status:'inactive'`, but deactivating via `deactext` also stamps `erasure`/`deactivatedOn`; `agDeactivated`/`agState` return `'deactivated'`, ahead of `notlisted`, since deactivating sets `onboarded:false`). Its row shows View / Edit / Reactivate / Delete. Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 24 Sep 2026)
- THE ADD USER → COHORT LEADER "Choose an existing candidate" picker lists ONLY candidates at level E5 or above (Maryam 24 Sep 2026: "the level should be E5 or above any level") — the same E5+ gate `leaderEligible`/`LVL_CODES` enforce — and shows each candidate's LEVEL in accent (`.cand-lvl` = `--accent-text`) beside the name, with the email right-aligned. The level rides an HTML option label (`formDd` now renders a `{html:'…'}` label raw instead of escaping — a general, backward-compatible extension; `restoreFormDdDisplay` copies the option's innerHTML so it survives re-renders). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 24 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN STAFF MEMBER row menu gains **"Mark as Cohort Leader"** and **"Mark as Talent Agent"** for an active staff account (Maryam 24 Sep 2026). Cohort Leader reuses the `askmarkleader` invite confirm — `askmarkleader` now allows any INTERNAL STAFF account (as well as an E5+ candidate). Talent Agent opens `SHEETS.markagent`, a modal capturing **Commission (%)** + a **5-star Initial rating** (both required, so the generic gate disables Send Invite until filled); on submit it grants the `agent` role and stores `commission`/`commissionAt`/`rating` (`onboarded:false`), High audit. Shown only when the account does not already hold that role. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 24 Sep 2026)
- A CANDIDATE AT LEVEL E5+ (not already a leader) carries a **"Mark as Cohort Leader"** action in BOTH the All Users table row menu (`usersRowMenu`) and the candidate detail kebab (`candidatesDetail`, already present) — gated on `leaderEligible(u) && !u.roles.includes('leader')` (Maryam 24 Sep 2026). It opens an INVITE confirmation (`askmarkleader` → `.conf` "Invite {name} to lead a cohort?" / "An invitation … is sent … Once accepted, their account can be placed on a cohort; their main role is unchanged." / **Send Invite**), which grants the leader capability (`domarkleader`, High-adjacent `notice` audit "Invited as cohort leader"). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 24 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN ADD USER form (`SHEETS.adduser`) DEFAULT-SELECTS Talent Agent (its fields open on load) and GATES the **Send Invite** button until the required fields are answered (Maryam 24 Sep 2026): name/email/commission/initial-rating carry req markers, and a conditional `gateFn(vals)` (a new `syncSheetSubmit` hook, since a static `rules` array cannot express it) disables the button until a role is chosen and — for an AGENT — name, email, commission and rating are filled, or — for a COHORT LEADER — either an existing-candidate pick or a name+email. Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 24 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN DELETE/DEACTIVATE clearing modal (`clearingUI`, `delext`/`deactext`; Maryam 24 Sep 2026): the "Clear before continuing" row icons are BARE glyphs at the standard **18px** page-icon size (`.clr-stack .cardrow-ic`). The **cohort task shows only when the person actually leads a live cohort** (`leaderCohorts(u.name)`), and each such row carries a replacement-leader picker (`.clr-repl` `formDd` over `leaderReplOpts`) sitting **BELOW the cohort text** inside the row body (Maryam: "choose a new leader field should be below, not on the same row"), replacing the old text hint. The **Deactivate/Delete button is DISABLED until everything is cleared** (`clearingDisabled` via the `gateFn` hook): a reason is written (`S.clearNote`, whose input re-checks the gate), no escrow is still held, and every live cohort has a replacement. The commit (`dodelext`/`dodeactext`) reassigns (`c.leader = repl[c.id]`); picks survive an escrow-release re-render via `S.clearRepl` (`stashClearRepl`). The replacement picker (`.clr-repl`) runs FULL WIDTH (Maryam 24 Sep 2026), and a **gated danger button reads as DISABLED** — `.btn.danger[disabled]` greys to `--layer-02` instead of staying full red (it looked clickable while disabled); enabled it is the danger red and clicking advances to the confirmation. Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 24 Sep 2026)
- A COHORT LEADER (candidate with the leader role) carries a **"Remove as Cohort Leader"** action in the Users table row menu (`usersRowMenu`) and the candidate detail kebab — this REVERSES the 20 Sep "no remove on the candidate detail" note (Maryam 24 Sep 2026). It opens `SHEETS.rmleader`, a modal that requires a REPLACEMENT leader chosen first (a `formDd` over `leaderCandOpts` — the same E5+ candidate picker as the invite form, level in accent + email meta), gated until picked; on submit (`submitrmleader`) the replacement gains the leader role, every cohort the removed leader led moves to them, and the removed person loses the capability. `leaderCandOpts(exceptId)` is the shared E5+ options builder. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 24 Sep 2026)
- EVERY SUPER ADMIN SHEET/MODAL WITH A REQUIRED FIELD DISABLES ITS SUBMIT BUTTON until that field is answered (Maryam 24 Sep 2026: "if a reason is required then disable the button — everywhere"). `sheetView`/`sidePeek` now always stamp the primary button `data-submit="1"`, and `syncSheetSubmit` gained a GENERIC fallback: for a sheet with no `gateFn`/`rules`/`save:` binding, it disables the button when any `.f` carrying a `.req-star` has an empty `[data-f]` (a radio/checkbox group is satisfied if any member is checked). So a sheet needs no explicit gate to be gated — Pause Listing, Reactivate, level/score overrides, refunds etc. all follow. Runs after every render (no flash) and on every input/pick. A gated DANGER button greys (`.btn.danger[disabled]` → `--layer-02`) rather than staying red. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 24 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN REACTIVATE modal (`reactext`) drops its info block, sits the Reason a section-gap (`--s06`) below the read-only context, and gates the Reactivate button on the required reason (Maryam 24 Sep 2026). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 24 Sep 2026)
- A SUPER ADMIN CUSTOM ROLE CAN BE DELETED (story 11.x, Maryam 24 Sep 2026): the Delete row action (custom roles only) is refused while any account holds the role — `KINDS.role.guard` returns "Move every holder to another role first." (and "Built-in roles can't be deleted" for a built-in) — else a `.conf` "Delete {role}? …" removes it from the role list AND the permission matrix/role selector (`KINDS.role.remove`), with a High audit recording the role and its permission set (`delAudit`, captured before `remove` clears `PERMSET`). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 24 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN AGENT INTERVIEW DETAIL (`agentsInterview` past view): the head section sits TIGHT to the About/Transcript tabs — a single `--s05` gap, no wasted space (Maryam 24 Sep 2026: "remove the unnecessary gap above the tabs"). The head carries `.iv-head`, which zeroes its `padding-bottom` and its heading's `margin-bottom` with the sanctioned `!important` (the section-rhythm `:not()` chain owns this section's padding and no plain selector outweighs it — the authtight-card precedent). And **Tal's read runs the FULL content width** (`.tal-eval` / `.ai-body` / `.ai-body p` → `max-width:none`), off the ~68ch reading measure the DS caps it at (Maryam 24 Sep 2026: "give tals read full page width"). Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 24 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN TALENT-AGENT MENUS are **View · Edit · Pause/Restore Listing · Deactivate · Delete** on the Agents table (`agentsAll` rowActs) and **Edit · Pause/Restore Listing · Deactivate · Delete** on the agent detail kebab (no View — it is the current page) (Maryam 24 Sep 2026).
