"Set assessment range" is dropped from the menus and instead the **Edit sheet now carries an editable Assessment range** — two level pickers (`from`/`to`, `get: x => agRange(x)[i]`, `LADDER` options) that default to the current `a.range` and are written back by `update` ("the edit page should also show assessment range which is editable"). The Users table row menu drops **"View as"** (impersonation hidden; `askviewas` stays defined). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 24 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN AGENT DETAIL "Listing details" panel and the CANDIDATE DETAIL "Levelling" panel are HIDDEN (Maryam 24 Sep 2026: "hide listing details section" / "hide leveling section"); `agentListingPanel` and `candLevellingPanel` stay defined, just not drawn. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 24 Sep 2026)
- EPICS 3 & 4 — USERS + ROLES story coverage (Maryam 24 Sep 2026, from the user-stories gap analysis; `tn-admin-portal.html`). Scope was locked by decision, question by question. ~~**Epic 6 (Marketplace) is EXCLUDED for now**~~ — SUPERSEDED 28 Sep 2026: Epic 6 (6.1–6.4) is now BUILT, on a **5-star Agent Score** (rubric 70% + candidate rating 30%), a quarterly score review, and a training gate — see the "TALENT AGENTS & MARKETPLACE" rule below. (The excluded model was the *out-of-100* score; the 5-star Agent Score is the client's V1.) **Talent agents are created as their own accounts** (never promoted from an existing user), so story 3.9 (Grant Talent Agent Role) and multi-role directory handling are out. Everything is **screens-only** (no `can()` enforcement). What was built:
  - **3.1 Directory** — an **Erasure** column + filter (None / Deletion in progress / Overdue-in-red / Deleted, `erasureState`/`erasureCell`) and a real **CSV Export** (`usersexport`, mirroring the staff `staffexport` — visible columns of the current filtered set, `EXPORT_ROW_LIMIT`, audit line). Status filter gains Deletion in progress / Deleted. `usersRowsFiltered` is the one filtered set the view + export share; `usersRowMenu` gates a deleting/deleted row to View only.
  - **3.2 Add Talent Agent** — a per-agent **Commission %** field on `KINDS.agent` (there is no platform-wide figure; stored `commission`/`commissionAt`, shown on the agent Listing panel). The 1–5 starting score is deliberately NOT captured (it only fed the excluded out-of-100 score). Invited external rows show the staff-style **expiry** (`extInviteStatus`: "Expires in N days" / "Invitation Expired").
  - **3.3 Cohort-leader removal** — when the leader holds running cohorts, `askremovelead` opens the `removelead` sheet: a replacement-leader dropdown per cohort, Remove refused until every one is filled (`doremovelead2` reassigns then revokes without unassigning). No live cohort keeps the plain confirm.
  - **3.4 / 3.5 / 3.6 Account lifecycle** — the FULL lifecycle now applies to candidates and agents (REVERSES the same-day "delete directly" note on the external delete, a deliberate scope decision). Deactivating schedules a **grace-period erasure** (`u.erasure = {beginsOn, by}`, a reversible countdown, badge admin-vs-holder-closed via `closedByBadge`); reactivating cancels it outright and its sheet shows the read-only context (deactivated-on, note, erasure-due). Deleting runs the **staff 5-processor erasure record** (`DEL_PROCESSORS` + the shared `confirmproc`) on external accounts — status Deactivated → Deletion in progress → Deleted, a 30-day deadline, Overdue in red (`externalErasurePanel`, appended to candidate + agent detail). The deactivate/delete clearing is now **actionable + blocking** (`clearingUI`/`clearingBlocked`): held escrow gets a per-payout Release (`clearpayout`) and the action is refused until escrow is cleared and cohorts reassigned; the reason note lives in `S.clearNote` (with a `clearNoteFor` per-id reset) so releasing a payout does not wipe it.
  - **3.7 Candidate detail** — three panels: **Levelling** (`candLevellingPanel` — level, position, provenance, history + an Override-level action `candoverride`/`docandoverride`), **Cohorts** (`candCohortsPanel`), **Payments** (`candPaymentsPanel`). Activity panel stays out (Audit hidden).
  - **3.8 Agent detail** — three panels: **Listing details** (`agentListingPanel` — commission, industries/intents/availability authored placeholder §74), **Earnings** (`agentEarningsPanel` — in-escrow / paid / next / destination), **Reports filed** (`agentReportsPanel` — proposed level parsed from Tal's read + rating). Score/training excluded.
  - **EPIC 4 Roles** — THE PERMISSIONS SURFACE IS ONE GRID PER MODULE, ROLES DOWN THE FIRST COLUMN (Maryam 25 Sep 2026, off the Taskora reference: "multiple tables, each showing each role in the first column, each table specific to a module"; this SUPERSEDES the same-day single roles-as-columns matrix AND the Module-rows catalogue). Under the role LIST, `usersRoles` draws, in the "Permissions" section, one BORDERED GRID per `PERM_MODULES` entry (`moduleGrid` → `.tbl-wrap.perm-wrap > table.tbl.perm-grid`): the MODULE NAME in the top-left header cell (`.perm-grid-name`), ALL FOUR CRUD actions across the top (View/Create/Edit/Delete — every grid, Maryam 25 Sep 2026), every ROLE (`permRoleCols`) as a row, and a tick (`pmYes`) / dash (`pmNo`) per cell (`cellFor`). THE SUPER ADMIN ROW IS ALWAYS ALL TICKS — every column of every module, never a dash (Maryam 25 Sep 2026: "super admin cannot delete a user? wtf") — because the Super Admin is unrestricted and holds every permission, including where a capability is modelled elsewhere (user deletion is the sensitive Accounts: Delete, not a Users module permission). For every OTHER role a cell is a tick only when the action EXISTS for the module (`acts.includes(a)`) AND the role holds it; an action the module does not carry shows the SAME dash as a not-granted permission, so for a non-SA role a column like Dashboard's Create/Edit/Delete or Users' Delete reads dashes. Below the grids, the **Sensitive permissions** and **Reserved to Super Admin** groups (NOT modules) keep their Permission + Governs list tables (`.perm-gov`, `PERM_SENSITIVE`/`PERM_RESERVED` third element is the Governs prose; each reserved name carries the `permInfo` `info` glyph "This permission is held by Super Admin only and cannot be granted to another role."). THE MODULE ACTION SETS ARE EXACTLY THE STORY 4.1 CATALOGUE — a module carries only the actions the catalogue lists: Users has no Delete (external delete is Accounts: Delete), Levelling is View-only, Marketplace/Interviews are View+Edit, Cohorts is View+Create+Edit, Finance is View+Edit; reserved row 2 is "Override the training-module listing gate". Grid CSS is admin-local (`.perm-grid`/`.perm-wrap`): a full `--rule` border per card, `--surface-2` header row, light `tr+tr td` row dividers, action columns centred, and an override of the DS admin `.tbl tr:first-child + tr td` s06 first-row offset so every role row is even. THE ROLE LIST HAS NO TYPE COLUMN (Maryam 25 Sep 2026: "remove the built-in column") — columns are Role · Description · Accounts · Actions. EVERY ROLE EXCEPT SUPER ADMIN IS TREATED AS A CUSTOM ROLE, so **Edit + Delete** show on all but Super Admin (`r.k === 'admin' ? '' : rowActs([editBtn, delBtn])`; the guard blocks only Super Admin, then refuses a held role until its holders move). **Edit** opens the `KINDS.role` record sheet to change the role's NAME + DESCRIPTION only. PERMISSIONS ARE EDITED IN THE GRIDS: the Permissions section header carries an **Edit Permissions** button (`editperms`) that turns every grantable cell (an existing action on a non-Super-Admin role) into a toggle (`toggleperm`, `checkFilled`/`checkOutline`; Super Admin and non-existent actions stay read-only), swapping the header for **Cancel + Update Permissions** — Update runs the "N added, N removed across M roles, affects K accounts" confirm (`saveperms` → `dosaveperms`, High audit); Cancel restores the snapshot. `PERMSET` holds the per-role grants; toggle CSS is `.perm-grid .btn[aria-pressed]`. The older `CAPS`/`MATRIX` Program-Business wall is untouched. (Epic 4, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- EPIC 1 — SA ACCESS & PROVISIONING (staff lifecycle; built 15 Sep 2026, `tn-admin-portal.html`). The internal-staff directory is its OWN sub-page `users/staff`; **All Users (`users/users`) is EXTERNAL accounts only** — the doc's rule "an account never appears in both", so `usersAll` filters out `u.internal`, drops the Admin role tab, and Add User offers external roles + non-internal origins only. Internal accounts are created and managed only through Staff. Built on the shared table/dropdown/sheet/confirm components. Pieces: `usersStaff` (directory — columns Name+email / Role / Job title / Department / Status / Last signed in / Added; Role/Status/Department `filterDd`s; row actions by status + guards), `staffDetailView` (the 1.7 detail — reached via `staffdetail`, branches on `u.internal`), and sheets `addstaff` (1.1), `editstaff` (1.8), `changerole` (1.3), `deactivate` (1.9), `reactivate` (1.10), `delstaff` (1.11). `addstaff`/`editstaff` open in the SLIDER (matching Add User, `SIDE_SHEET_KEYS`); the four guarded two-step actions stay centre `.conf` modals. Status vocabulary: `invited` / `active` / `inactive` → "Invited / Active / Deactivated" (`STAFF_STATUS_LABEL`). Guards: `staffProtect` (the original Super Admin `u.orig` and the actor's own account — no role change / deactivate / delete) and `lastSAGuard` (never remove access from the only Super Admin). Every write logs audit (invite/change-role/deactivate/reactivate/delete = High, edit = Medium `'med'`). Seed: u9 Derek is the original SA (`orig:true`, protected), u43 a second SA (so the last-SA guard is demonstrable), u46 Invited, u47 Deactivated. (Epic 1, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- STAFF AUTHENTICATION IS PASSWORD + A ONE-TIME CODE BY EMAIL OR SMS — no authenticator app, no trusted device, the channel chosen every time (stories 1.2 / 1.4 / 1.5). The auth scene (`authView`, `S.auth`): login → `deliver` (channel choice, none pre-selected) → `code` (6 digits, 40s resend gate) → `setpw` on the reset flow; `S.authFlow` says sign-in vs reset. Activation (1.2) is a full-frame PREVIEW scene (`activateView`, `S.activate`) reached from an Invited row's "Preview activation" (there is no invited persona in the console); completing it flips the account to Active. A prototype accepts any six digits and stores no real password (ruling: build the flow and its screens, not the send). The resend cooldown is enforced behaviourally (an early press is refused), not ticked live, to keep the OTP field's caret. This REPLACED the old "authenticator app + backup code" sign-in and the "reset link by email" flow. (Epic 1, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- STEP-UP (a fresh code before the action commits, `askConfirm({stepup:true})`) is on the doc's FOUR highest-severity actions (story 1.6): change a fee (`feeStepup` → `dosavefee`), approve a refund (`askrefappr`), delete an account (staff `delstaff`, external `askdel:user`) and delete a role (`askdel:role`). The doc says "no other action triggers the prompt", but the OLD SA-journey step-ups on grant / revoke Super Admin / View As / release-all payouts were LEFT in place (removing them is the riskier direction) — FLAGGED for Maryam to rule on. `SEC.stepup` toggles the gate. (Epic 1, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- EPIC 2 — ADMIN CONSOLE (built 15 Sep 2026). 2.1 Navigate: the console shell/rail already existed and follows standing ruling #2, NOT story 2.1's exact 12-item list (the doc hides Employers/CRM/System and shows Levelling/Consultant Requests; the ruling reverses that) — nav left as-is, the discrepancy is deliberate. 2.2 Dashboard: a period selector (This week / This month / This quarter, This month default, `S.period` + `setperiod`) and an "as at <time>" stamp on `homeOverview`'s first panel — PRESENTATIONAL (the seed has no time series; SA-03 E1), the surface not a live feed. 2.3 Notifications: the bell's "View all" opens a full list page `home/notifs` (`homeNotifs`, hidden sub) — All/Unread filter, per-account read state (`S.read`), 90-day-retention note, "not the audit log". 2.4 My Profile (`settingsProfile`): only First/Last editable; Email/Job title/Department read-only with a note on where each is changed; the role shown WITH its permission chips; Save logs Medium (`'med'`), no notification. 2.5 Login & Security (`settingsSecurity`): three groups — Password (current/new/confirm, rules, differ-from-current, ends every other session, High audit), Phone number (current masked, new + current password → OTP sub-step `S.secPhone='code'` → verify, High audit, old number holds until verified), Signed-in sessions (device/browser/location/started/last-active, current marked no sign-out, per-row Sign Out + Sign Out Everywhere Else, auto-sign-out read-only) — plus a Two-factor group (always on + the step-up toggle). Nobody changes anyone else's credentials. (Epic 2, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN DASHBOARD (`homeOverview`) MIRRORS ONLY THE LIVE MODULES, OPERATIONAL-FIRST, WITH LEAN CHARTS (Maryam 21 Sep 2026). Every band maps to one of the nine VISIBLE modules and routes into it; NOTHING on the dashboard reads a hidden module. Employer analytics (Employer-unlocks revenue stream, employer-verification attention row, Placement fees) and the whole CRM "Growth & acquisition" group (Leads by source, the `newLeadsCard`/`trendChart` new-leads line, the quiz→placed acquisition funnel, the "Where users come from" origin `.stats`) are REMOVED — they surfaced modules the admin can no longer open (Employers "not in the system for now" 19 Sep; CRM hidden). Order, top→bottom: (0) period bar (kept, presentational — no time series, SA-03 E1); (1) **Needs your attention** — the operational LEAD: a `.tile clk gcard` tile-stack (NOT `.qa-c` quick-action cards, whose copy may not carry live queue data §98.3), one row per queue, drawn ONLY when its count>0 (§60), all routing to live modules (`cohorts/assign` leaders-ready `LEADER_REQS` feedback-stage + cohorts-without-a-leader; `market/bookings` overdue held interviews `bkOverdue`; `finance/payouts` `heldPayouts()`; `home/rulings` blocked+open `DECISIONS`); (2) Platform at a glance — TWO `.stats` rows of exactly four (people row + money/throughput row); (3) People & access — Users by role + by status (`hbars`, employers excluded); (4) Candidates & cohorts — bucket `donut` + "where candidates sit" `kv` + the cohort-health `table`; (5) Marketplace & interviews — booking-mix `donut` + interviews-by-state `kv`; (6) Finance & settlement — revenue streams `hbars` (Course / Interview / Scene only) + payments & payouts `kv`; (7) System & activity — platform-health `kv` (`SVC_HEALTH`/`INTEGRATIONS` live counts, degraded, open DSARs) + recent-audit timeline; (8) Open decisions & risks — the blocked `DECISIONS` tiles + "Open the register". Charts stay `donut` (2–4 slice split) / `hbars` (ranked list) per "data over decoration" (§5); NO trend line (placeholder). Do NOT re-add employer/CRM/placement panels. (tn-admin-portal.html `homeOverview`, Maryam 21 Sep 2026) — UPDATED 22 Sep 2026 (Maryam, inspo graphs): the dashboard now runs Needs your attention → Platform at a glance → **Platform Users** (`barChart`, populations by month) → **Earnings & Payouts** → **Candidates by Track** (`bucketCard` sparklines) → **Interviews** (`lineChart`); Cohort health, System & activity and Open decisions are HIDDEN (kept behind `false`). The **Earnings & Payouts** section (`group('Earnings & Payouts')` heading over a `.pay-row`) sits directly after Platform Users: LEFT a Platform-earnings breakdown card (headline + `.pstat-chip` + stacked revenue-source bar + % legend — bar palette **black / accent / #6B6B6B** matching the Platform Users chart, NO "the platform's take" sub-line), RIGHT a Payouts card (headline + `.pstat-chip`, NO note line, NO "Agent payouts" legend — the period filter alone rides the legend row via `lineChart`'s `noLeg`). This is the exception to the 21 Sep "NO trend line" clause — the client asked for the inspo statistics graphs; figures + % change are AUTHORED PLACEHOLDER (§74, SA-03 E1). The **% chips are one component everywhere** — `.pstat-chip` matches the Candidates-by-Track `.bcard-chip` (11px, `padding:3px --s03`, light-green success). The two cards are **equal width** (`.pay-row` `1fr 1fr` ≥900, stacking on a phone). The Payouts graph is a **semicircle gauge** (`gauge()`, Figma node 1-601), NOT a line chart: a 180° arc where **only Settled fills the arc** in `--accent` over a light-grey `--gray-20` **background track** (In progress is that grey background, not a coloured segment — Maryam 22 Sep 2026 "use light grey instead of pink and it should be in the background"), a **circle pointer** (white knob + accent ring) at the tip of the filled arc, a centred "Total payouts / N", and a legend showing **only Settled + In progress** (Maryam 22 Sep 2026 "we are only showing payouts that are pending and settled" — no Failed). The card also carries a `.pbrk-sub` "**$X pending to release**". Arc-length dash math uses the real `L = π·R`, NOT `pathLength` (unreliable — it let the `50 50` dash repeat and paint a stray accent cap at the far end). Counts/amount read off the `PAYOUTS` seed (4 settled · 4 held · $744 pending · 8 total; SA-03 E1 demo ledger). NO period toggle (a status snapshot). The earlier `payoutChart`/`ampPath`/`PAYOUT_PATH` line renderer was REMOVED. **Every dashboard graph carries a no-background accent nav link** (`chartHead(title, {go, label})` → `.acc-link`) to the page its data belongs to: Platform Users→users/users, Platform earnings→finance/transactions, Payouts→finance/payouts, Candidates by Track→candidates/candidates, Interviews→market/bookings (Maryam 22 Sep 2026). The Platform Users bars were also toned down so black Candidates sit just above the accent Talent-agents bars (base 10–18 vs 7–15, axis 0–25). (tn-admin-portal.html `homeOverview`/`gauge`/`chartHead`, Maryam 22 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN DASHBOARD IS ALIGNED TO STORY 2.2 (View Dashboard / Console Landing), ADDITIVELY (Maryam 30 Sep 2026 — every gap confirmed per-panel). The 22 Sep inspo charts (Platform Users bars, Earnings/Payouts cards, Candidates-by-Track sparklines, Interviews line) are KEPT; the story's panels sit alongside them, so this REVERSES the 21 Sep hides for Cohort health only. Decisions taken: **(1) PERIOD selector is a DROPDOWN in the heading row** (Maryam 30 Sep 2026, replacing the tab-strip `periodBar` which is left defined, unused) — `filterDd('period', 'month', [This week / This month / This quarter], 'Period')`, This month default, sitting in the `aiHead` action slot NEXT TO a From/To DATE-RANGE filter (the shared `filterDate('dashfrom'/'dashto', …, 180)` month-grid pickers Finance/Levelling use), all inside the `.dash-dates` wrapper on the RIGHT of the "Welcome Back, Derek!" row. Both presentational (no time series, SA-03 E1); the per-chart Weekly/Monthly/Yearly filters stay on the inspo charts. SEED: five `status:'invited'` accounts u48–u52 (three agents `onboarded:false` + two internal staff) were added so "Users by status" reads a visible Inactive chunk (Maryam 30 Sep 2026); this bumps Total Users to 41 / Staff to 11 consistently. **(2) PLATFORM AT A GLANCE is a fixed EIGHT cells Maryam named**, in order: Total Users (`people.length`) · Candidates · Cohort Leaders · Talent Agents · **Staff Members** (`USERS.filter(u=>u.internal)`) · Courses · Running Cohorts · **Earnings** (`platTake`, "platform take") — icons `group/candidates/user/agents/shield/book/cohorts/money`. This replaces the old money/escrow/payouts cells. **(3) USERS BY STATUS** — sits BELOW Platform Users (Maryam 30 Sep 2026), and is ONE stacked bar + a ONE-LINE legend in the Platform-earnings `.pbrk-bar` treatment (Active `--accent` / **Inactive** `--text-primary` #111 / Deactivated #6B6B6B — Active is the accent, Maryam 30 Sep 2026; segments carry a 6px min-width), NOT `hbars` — → users/users. The legend is a horizontal `.ubs-leg` row (dot + name + count per item, a `--border-subtle-02` `|` between), each dot's colour matching its bar segment; the middle label reads **"Inactive"** (Maryam 30 Sep 2026, its value is the invited-status count `uInvited`). **(4) MONEY AT A GLANCE** — a **4-card `.stats` row** (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "4 cards in a row, remove the active rebooking credit"): gross taken · held in escrow · released to agents (`poSettled`) · moved to TalentNext (`platTake`) — the rebooking-credit figure is dropped — → finance/transactions. Revenue-by-line is the existing Earnings breakdown card. **(5) PAYOUTS DUE** — the Payouts card's `.pbrk-sub` now reads "`$X in escrow · $Y releasing this week`" (`poReleasingWeek` = held payouts with a release date, report filed, not blocked). **(6) MARKETPLACE THIS WEEK** — a `kv` of interviews booked / held / reports-filed-within-24h (`reported` bookings stand in for on-time, §74), → market/bookings. **(7) BOOKING MIX** — a 2-slice `donut` First vs Re (`agIvIsRe`), kept ALONGSIDE the monthly Interviews trend. **(8) COHORT HEALTH restored** (the 21 Sep `false` → `true`). **(9) LEVEL CHANGES THIS PERIOD** — Moved up/down/overridden `hbars` off `LEVEL_LOG` (`direction`, `/override/i.test(decidedOn)`), → levelling/log. **(10) AGENT STANDING** — two `kv` columns, top by `agScore` (stars via `starMark`, "· Starting" when `scoreApproved===false`) and by `interviewed`, → agents/agents. **(11) NEEDS YOUR ATTENTION** gains "Outstanding invitations" (`USERS` status invited) and "Agents active but not listed" (`agState==='notlisted'`) — BUILT but SUPPRESSED behind `ATTN_EXTRA_ON=false` (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "add the functionality so we can show later but not now"); flip it true to surface, wire the directory pre-filter then. **(12) FRESHNESS as-at stamp — NOT shown** (Maryam: "skip"; `asAt` stays computed, unused). Permission-gating of panels stays unsimulated (single Super Admin, ruling #3). Admin-only, no rebuild. (tn-admin-portal.html `homeOverview`, Maryam 30 Sep 2026) — HEADING-ROW REFINEMENTS (Maryam 30 Sep 2026): the period dropdown DROPS its "Period:" prefix (reads just "This month" — `filterDd('period', …, '')`; `filterDd` renders no `label: ` prefix when the label is empty, a general backward-compatible change) and sits to the RIGHT of the From/To date filter (`.dash-dates` order is From, To, then a `.dash-period` wrapper with `margin-left:--s04` for a gap so they are not attached). And the whole `.dash-head` section closes with a section divider — an inset `::after` hairline at `--pad-x` (`--rule` #efefef), boosted over the admin "no divider after the top block" rule (`.app .modhead + .sec:not(#_)::after{content:none}`) with a `:not(#_)` on `.app .main .sec.dash-head:not(#_)::after`. The line is CENTRED in the gap (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "the divider should be in the middle of both sections, not attaching the first"): the head pays `padding-bottom:--s06` and the next section already pays `--s06` on top, so the line has 24 above == 24 below — but the aiHead's `.sec-h` carries a dead `margin-bottom:24px` (nothing follows it) that would stack into 48 above, so `.app .main .sec.dash-head > .sec-h{margin-bottom:0 !important}` zeroes it (both !important, the sanctioned section-rhythm override). The "View Cohorts" button on the Cohort health header is the `acc-link` accent link + arrow (matching "View Interviews"), not a grey `btn-g`. COHORT HEALTH drops the "View Cohorts" button; each cohort ROW is clickable to `cohorts/detail/<id>` with a trailing right-aligned `.row-arrow` (18px, `--text-secondary`, accent on row hover) (Maryam 30 Sep 2026). MARKETPLACE-THIS-WEEK + BOOKING-MIX + INTERVIEWS are MERGED into ONE **"Interviews"** section (Maryam 30 Sep 2026): the three counts (booked / held / reports-filed-within-24h) show as THREE `.stats` blocks in a row (`iv-stats`, pinned to 3 columns) each with a fitting description, the Booking-mix donut is DROPPED, and the monthly line chart's note reads "Interviews this month" (was "interviews this period"). LEVEL CHANGES THIS PERIOD is HIDDEN (Maryam 30 Sep 2026, behind `false`, kept restorable). AGENT STANDING is ONE `table()` (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "shift Agent standing into a table, show the talent agent picture with their name, remove the Top-by-Agent-Score / Most-interviews-conducted headings — both sides are the same agent"): columns Agent (`personCell` photo + name) · Rank (`#1..`, after Agent) · Agent Score (stars, one decimal, "· Starting" when unapproved) · Interviews conducted · a trailing `.row-arrow` → the agent detail (the whole row is `go:'agents/detail/<id>'`); no "View Agents" button. `{paginate:false, select:false, evenCols:true}` — `evenCols` is a new `table()` opt that opts out of the person-cell width-pin so ALL columns divide the space equally (else the pinned name column bunches Rank against it). The two `.dcol` kv columns + sub-headings are gone. And the **Earnings & Payouts** `.pay-row` is `minmax(0,1fr) max-content` at ≥900 (was `1fr 1fr`): the Payouts card (2nd, ring + legend) takes only its required content width and Platform earnings (1st) takes the rest (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "the payouts card should be of required width, give the other width to the platform earnings card"). The PLATFORM EARNINGS card drops its stacked bar + legend and Scene purchases, showing the division as TWO figure blocks under the `$platTake ↗12%` headline (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "remove the bar", "remove scenes earning", "show the earnings division in 2 blocks ... show the desc in each block"): `stats([[book,'Course Payments', money(courseRev), '{n} Purchased Courses'], [video,'Interview Payments', money(interviewRev), '{n} Re-Interviews']], 'pearn-stats')` — the titles renamed from Course/Interview fees, each sub-line the count off TXNS (course-in purchases; interview-in = re-interviews, a first interview being complimentary). Each earnings field also carries a little green LINE GRAPH just left of its value (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "add little line graphs on the left of both these values", then supplied two exact SVGs — pasted verbatim, `#04CE00`, one per stream, `.pearn-spark` sized by height 26 with the viewBox aspect kept; the reused Candidates-by-Track `sparkline()` was dropped for these). The card is custom `.pearn-rows` markup now (mark `.pearn-ic` 18px · `.pearn-txt` title+desc · sparkline · `.pearn-v` value), replacing the earlier `.pearn-stats` block. `.pearn-stats` is a SINGLE column with NO block frame (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "take both into two vertical fields", then "remove the blocks look and just separate them with a divider"; supersedes the first 2-column pass): `.app .page .pearn-stats{grid-template-columns:1fr; gap:0; border:0; background:transparent}` strips the `.stats` card chrome (the DS's `.app .page .stats` border + `--rule` grid ground + 1px gap, so the `.page` specificity has to be matched), `> .stat{padding-left:0; padding-right:0}` aligns the icon/title/value to the `$274` headline's left edge, and `> .stat + .stat{border-top:1px solid --rule}` is the single hairline between the two rows. `+ margin-top:--s05` for the removed bar's gap. `pbrk-bar`/`pbrk-leg`/`sceneRev` now unused on this card. The heading-row From/To date filters DEFAULT to the selected period's range (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "since This month is selected, show the default dates of a whole month") — `filterDate` gained a `def` (ISO fallback) param and the dashboard passes `periodDateRange(getFilter('period','month'))` (week = Mon–Sun, month = 1st–last, quarter = the calendar quarter), so From/To read whole-period dates instead of "Any" and follow the period dropdown until a custom date is picked. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
- THE DASHBOARD PAYOUTS CARD IS A CONCENTRIC 3-RING RADIAL, not the semicircle gauge (Maryam 30 Sep 2026, off a supplied SVG). Three payout STATES, one ring each (outer→inner): **In escrow** `--accent` (r 129.583), **Paid** `--text-primary` #111 (r 97.048), **Returned** #6B6B6B (r 63.765) — the platform's red/black/grey categorical set (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "red, black, grey just like other graphs"), each over a #E5E5EF track. ALL THREE COLOURED ARCS share ONE stroke width `ARC=17.038` — the outer ring's (Maryam: "same width and follow the width of the most outer circle") — over THINNER `TRK=8.519` light tracks (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: the grey lines are thinner than the coloured, per the reference). The **viewBox is padded to `-10 -10 292 292`** so the outer arc (r + ARC/2 ≈ 138 from the 135.64/136.316 centre exceeds 272) is not clipped on any side. The coloured arc = that state's share of the total AMOUNT (a `stroke-dasharray` on a circle rotated −90° so it starts at 12 o'clock, round caps). Values are MONEY (`p.net` sums; e.g. In escrow $780 / Paid $196 / Returned $120), centre "Total payouts $N". The coloured arc `ARC` is **14** (was 17.038) — a little thinner but still clearly thicker than the grey track `TRK` 8.519 (Maryam 30 Sep 2026); the Payouts card's ring↔legend gap carries `+20px` to widen the max-content card by 20px (Maryam 30 Sep 2026). Layout: graph LEFT (`svg` 160px), a VERTICAL `.pring-leg` legend on the RIGHT (In escrow · Paid · Returned outer→inner, each `.pring-row` = dot + name + money) — the reference layout. No `.pbrk-sub` escrow/releasing line (removed). `gauge()` kept defined but not called; `.pring-*` CSS admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html `homeOverview`, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
- THE DASHBOARD SECTION DIVIDERS ARE HAND-CURATED (Maryam 30 Sep 2026, four asks): NO divider above OR below **Candidates by Track** (the Earnings & Payouts section above it and Candidates-by-Track itself carry `.dash-nodiv`), and a CENTRED divider BELOW **Cohort health** and BELOW **Interviews** (both carry `.dash-div`, so a line sits between Cohort health→Interviews and between Interviews→Agent standing). The auto foot-line otherwise varies by content (a `.stats`/`.facts` block hides its `::after`, a plain section draws a full-bleed one), so two admin-local markers force it: `.dash-nodiv` drops a section's foot `::after`/`::before`; `.dash-div` draws a clean 1px `--rule` line inset to `--pad-x` at the section foot (both sections pay `--s05`, so 16 above == 16 below). `:not(#_)` boosts past the DS `.sec:has(> .stats)::after{display:none}` suppression. Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html `homeOverview`, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
- THE PLATFORM USERS GROW-IN PLAYS ONCE, AFTER THE PAGE LOADS (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "for the very first time when i load the page the Platform Users graph should have an interaction just like it has when i switched it ... start the interaction once the loading is complete"). The `.lc-anim` grow-in is armed at boot NOT synchronously (that fired it during the initial content load) but on `window.load` + an 80ms settle: `armPUGrow()` toggles `.lc-anim` on the Platform Users chart (`.lchart` holding a `[data-act^="gper:pu:"]`) directly — no re-render, once. setTimeout not rAF (trap 17). Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html boot, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
- THE DATE-FILTER PILL IS A FULL BLOCK, not a bottom stroke — portal-wide (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "the date filter component in the whole portal should be in a block, not only bottom stroke"). The border sits on the WRAPPER `.fdate-filter` (not the trigger) so it ENCLOSES both the trigger and the clear × (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "include the cross icons in the blocks not out of it") — the × can't nest inside the trigger `<button>`, so it's a sibling inside the bordered wrapper. `.fdate-t` is borderless/transparent (a focus rule keeps it so §12's `.inp:focus` can't draw an underline inside); `.fdate-x` sits inside at the right. The box (`1px solid var(--rule)` + `--layer-01` ground) matches the `.fdd` filter dropdowns. On the dashboard, Finance and Levelling. Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
- EPIC 3 — USERS (external directory; 3.1–3.6 built 15 Sep 2026, 3.7/3.8 detail enrichment still TODO). 3.1 `usersAll` is the EXTERNAL directory: role TAB strip (Maryam's rule overrides the doc's Type dropdown), Status dropdown (All/Invited/Active/Deactivated), columns User / Type (`typeLabel`) / Cohort leader (a `.tag` marker) / Level (`candLevel`, candidates only) / Status (`userStatusTag`) / Joined / Last active; a row opens the OWNING module's rich detail via `userDetailRoute` (candidate→`candidates/detail`, agent→`agents/detail`, else the generic `staffdetail`) — CANDS/AGENTS ids match USERS ids, so this is safe. Screen actions: Bulk Import · **Add User** (Maryam 15 Sep 2026 overrode the doc's agent-only invite — she wants to add any external type, so the generic `rec:user:new` with a role picker is the primary CTA, not Invite Talent Agent; `SHEETS.inviteagent` stays defined but unused). 3.2 `SHEETS.inviteagent` (first/last/email → Invited agent, High audit, slider) is BUILT but not surfaced, per that override. **EXPORT is hidden everywhere** (`exportBtn` is a no-op — it was a toast-only placeholder with no CSV and no audit line; Maryam 15 Sep 2026 "hide the export button, it's still not working"); make it real to bring it back. 3.3 `askmarkleader`/`askremoveleader` + `leaderEligible` (candidate at E5+ via `LVL_CODES`, or any internal staff) — a capability, not a main role; the button is on the candidate detail (disabled with the E5 reason when below) and the staff detail; removal is refused while `leadingCount>0`. 3.4/3.5/3.6 `deactext`/`reactext`/`delext` (reason note + `extClearing` summary of an agent's upcoming interviews+listing and a leader's live cohorts; delete adds step-up; High audit) — wired to the directory row actions (Invited rows offer Resend/Cancel invite instead). The candidate/agent MODULE detail pages keep their own suspend/erase for now. NOTE: the doc's per-role permission gating of panels/actions is NOT simulated (single Super Admin, ruling #3). (Epic 3, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- TALENT AGENTS & MARKETPLACE — EPIC 6 (6.1–6.4), the module split and the Agent Score / training-gate model (Maryam, 28 Sep 2026, `tn-admin-portal.html`). The module is renamed **"Talent Agents & Marketplace"** with TWO visible submodules, honouring Maryam's intent (which reverses the stories' own naming — she was explicit): **"All Agents"** is the WHOLE directory (every talent agent whether listed or not) and the account-admin home (Invite / Edit / Deactivate / Delete); **"Marketplace"** is the LISTED-ONLY, ranked bookable roster — what candidates see and in what order. Because All Agents holds every listing state, the full story-6.1 columns live there (its Listing-state and Training filters need every state and the "who is held back" story reads there); Marketplace is the lean listed-only view. `MODULES` agent module `t:'Talent Agents & Marketplace'` with subs `[{agents:'All Agents'},{marketplace:'Marketplace'}, detail, interview]`; `V.agents` adds `marketplace:marketplaceView`.
  - **THE AGENT SCORE (6.1/6.3)** — ONE 5-star score, one decimal, the only rating candidates see: `agProposedScore = round1(rubric×0.7 + candidateRating×0.3)`, both out of 5 (`a.rubric`, `a.rating`). `agScore(a)` returns the APPROVED score (`a.approvedScore`) once the first quarterly review lands, else the STARTING score set at invite (`a.startingScore`, while `a.scoreApproved === false`), shown marked "Starting score, set at invite · first due [quarter end]". `scoreLabel(n)`: 4.3–5.0 Excellent, 3.5–4.2 Strong, 2.8–3.4 Developing, <2.8 Needs review. The two components print as "Rubric N/5 · Candidate rating N/5 (count)". All authored placeholder (§74 — no rubric telemetry in the seed): `rubric` / `candRatings` / `scoreApproved` / `approvedScore` / `scoreAt` / `startingScore` / `quarterEnd` / `scoreHistory`, seeded across the six agents to exercise every branch (u2 starting-score, u13 training-overridden, u45 not-listed with two failing gates).
  - **RANKING (6.1)** is by Agent Score highest first, WEEKLY-frozen (not live), ties on interviews conducted; only LISTED agents carry a rank (Paused/Not listed show "—"). `freezeRanking` sorts by `agScore`; a banner states last/next recalculation (`RANK_LAST`/`RANK_NEXT`, the Mon–Sun payout cycle). A score change does NOT re-rank immediately — the rank holds until the next recalc (6.1/6.3), so `doagscore` does not call `freezeRanking`.
  - **LISTING STATE IS GATE-DERIVED, never set by hand (6.4)** — `agState`: invited → deactivated → paused (an admin took them out, `a.paused`/status inactive) → (`agListable` → listed : notlisted). `gateReasons(a)` evaluates the FOUR gates TOGETHER so more than one can fail and EVERY failing reason is shown: active account (`agAccountActive` — a paused account is still active; only deactivated/invited fails), payout destination on file (`agPayoutOnFile`), training complete-or-overridden (`agTrainingGateOK`), valid public profile + fee (`agProfileOK`). The assessment range and the Agent Score are NOT gates (a low score changes ranking, never listability). The agent detail carries a **Marketplace standing** panel (`agentMarketPanel`) showing the score + components + training + rank + Listing state and the 6.4 Listable/Not-listable verdict with the blocking reasons.
  - **6.2 PAUSE / RESTORE / OVERRIDE TRAINING GATE** — Pause (`agpause`, mandatory reason 1–500, internal, never shown to the agent) removes the LISTING immediately, leaves the diary/money/portal untouched, clears rank next recalc. Restore (`agrestore`) commits only where every OTHER gate passes — if one is failing the restore is refused and the blocking reasons shown (`doagrestore` re-checks `gateReasons`, reverts to paused, toasts). Override Training Gate (`agoverride`, Super Admin only, offered only where training is Not complete) lifts ONLY the training gate — the account/payout/profile gates still block; sets `training:'overridden'` with the admin's name + date (shown in the Training column), a mandatory reason + a confirmation prompt. The Training column reads Complete / Not complete / Overridden (`agTrainCell`).
  - **6.3 REVIEW AGENT SCORE** (`agscore`) — shows the proposed score, its two components, the source period and current score; ONE form collapsing Approve + Override: an "Approved score (1.0–5.0)" field prefilled to the proposed score (leave it = Approve; change it = Override) and a reason gated by `gateFn` (required only when the score differs). `doagscore` keeps the prior score in `scoreHistory`, sets `approvedScore` + `scoreAt`, and the rank follows next recalc. Both Approve and Override write a High audit line with from/to.
  - **Row actions** — All Agents kebab: the marketplace actions (`agMarketActs`: View, Pause/Restore, Set Assessment Range, Review Agent Score, and — SA-only, training-not-complete — Override Training Gate) PLUS the account-admin actions (Edit, Deactivate/Reactivate, Delete). Marketplace kebab: the marketplace actions only. Both carry a toast-only **Export** (`agentsexport`/`marketexport`, audit + "download link in your notifications, valid 7 days" per the 25 Sep export convention — no browser download). Filters: All Agents has the state tab strip + Training (`agtrain`) + Assessment range (`agrange`) `filterDd`s; Marketplace has Assessment range only (all listed). Both paged at the table default. Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 28 Sep 2026)
- ~~The TALENT AGENTS module page (`agentsAll`) heading is "Agent directory"~~ — SUPERSEDED 28 Sep 2026 (the page is "All Agents" now, see the Epic 6 rule above). Original (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "this is a talent agents page, do not name it as marketplace listing") — parallel to the candidate module's "Candidate directory". It carries an **"Add Talent Agent"** primary button (`addBtn('agent', …)` → the `KINDS.agent` create sheet, which makes an APPROVED active agent — distinct from the Users page's Invited-only invite flow; the KIND help says use it "for a known agent you have already vetted elsewhere"). `KINDS.agent.label` is "talent agent" (was "listing"), so its sheet reads "Add a talent agent" / edit / audit lines consistently; `okNew` is "Add Talent Agent". The "Listing" column and the Listed/Paused/Not-listed states keep the listing vocabulary — that is the agent's marketplace state, not the page's name. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- An admin rail is a tree: the open module lists its sub-pages as `.sn-sub` rows, queue counts as `.badge`; the render gates the sub-rows, not the CSS. (§120)
- In the COLLAPSED 72px rail, hovering a module icon that has sub-modules opens its sub-list as a FLYOUT beside the icon (a `.sn-subs` panel floating right of the rail, no module-name header — the sub-list names itself). The sub-block now renders for EVERY parent (the render drops the old `on && S.nav` gate); the CSS routes it — inline under the current module in the open rail / mobile drawer, hover-only flyout in the collapsed rail. The parent's `.sn-node` wrapper is the hover region and is armed in `HOVER_KEEP`; the collapsed rail goes `overflow:visible` so the flyout escapes its 72px. (§120.1c, Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- A COLLAPSED-RAIL **LEAF** module's hover label (and Log out) is a WHITE CHIP matching the parent flyout panel, not a solid black pill (Maryam, 25 Sep 2026: "the hover ui should be the one we were using previously"). The `.sn-item[data-tip]:not(.sn-parent)::after` tip uses `--layer-01` ground, `--text-primary` ink, a `--rule` 1px border and the flyout's `0 8px 24px rgba(17,17,17,.12)` shadow — so a leaf tooltip (Course Catalogue, Candidates, …) reads the same as a parent's `.sn-subs` flyout, instead of the old `--gray-100`/`--on-dark` pill. Admin-local (`#admin-rail-tip`); reverses the 23 Sep dark-tip look. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- The rail is the ONLY sub-navigator: a module with sub-pages draws NO in-page tab strip for them, even when the rail is shut. `subTabs()` is a no-op; to switch sub-page you open the side nav and pick one. (§120, Maryam 7 Sep 2026) — ONE EXCEPTION: the SETTINGS module (below).
- THE SETTINGS MODULE IS ONE PAGE WITH INNER TABS, mirroring the candidate profile — NOT a rail sub-tree (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "instead of having submodules of settings, profile and notifications ... follow the candidate profile structure where the profile module has inner tabs"). All five Settings subs (`general` / `profile` / `notifications` / `security` / `payments`) are marked `hidden` in `MODULES`, so the rail draws the footer gear as a single leaf (no chevron, no flyout); the subs stay in `_sub` so `settings/<key>` is still routable + deep-linkable and the account-menu / rulings-register links keep working. `V.settings` (no longer `modView`) renders `head + settingsTabs(cur) + SETTINGS_PANES[cur]()`: a `.sec.sec-cs.set-tabs` `.cs` tab strip (General / My Profile / Notifications / Login & Security / Payments & Cards) whose tabs are `data-go="settings/<key>"` buttons (so a tab click navigates and the URL follows), over the active pane (the pane renderers are unchanged). `.set-tabs` shares the interview page's `.iv-tabs` spacing (strip `::after` off, one `--s05` gap to the pane). This is the single reversal of the "rail is the only sub-navigator" rule, Settings-only. The Settings rail item (the footer gear) is now the PROFILE/person icon (`ic:'profile'` → `user`, since Settings is the account page and the System module already owns the gear), and because the admin hides Tal's summary portal-wide, `V.settings` draws a plain `aiHead('Settings', …)` page heading + description above the tabs (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "the settings module icon should be profile icon", "the page heading and desc is missing"). The strip is balanced --s06 (24) above AND below — the `.set-head` heading's bottom padding + `.sec-h` margin are zeroed (`!important`, the section-rhythm chain again), leaving the strip's own top padding as the single gap above, and the pane's top padding the gap below (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "unnecessary space after page desc to the tabs, and less space between tabs and content"). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- EPIC 11 — SETTINGS REORGANISED + PLATFORM SETTINGS (11.1) + NOTIFICATION TEMPLATES (11.2) (Maryam, 29 Sep 2026). THE SETTINGS RAIL ICON IS A GEAR now (`ic:'settings'`, was `ic:'profile'` — the System module reads `ic:'link'` so the gear is free), reversing the 21 Sep "Settings gets the profile icon". **My Profile and General Settings LEFT the Settings module to become their own STANDALONE PAGES**, reached from the top-header account-menu chevron (`data-go="profile/profile"` / `general/general`), NOT tabs. Each is its own hidden top-level module (`{k:'profile'}` / `{k:'general'}`, off the rail) with a single view — `V.profile` / `V.general` render `head(pageSummary()) + aiHead(...) + settingsProfile()/settingsGeneral()` with no tab strip. General Settings keeps the identity/display fields (org name, support email/phone, timezone, language, date format, currency); **Payments & Cards was dropped from Settings entirely** (`settingsPayments` stays defined, unreferenced); Departments moved OUT of General into the Platform screen. The **Settings module** (gear) now holds four tabs: **Platform** (11.1) · **Notification Templates** (11.2) · **Notifications** (your own prefs) · **Login & Security**. `SETTINGS_TABS`/`SETTINGS_PANES` updated; `V.settings` defaults to `platform`. `syncSettingsSave` runs for `settings` / `general` / `profile`. — **11.1 PLATFORM SETTINGS** (`settingsPlatform`, the Platform tab): nine `.sec` groups, each with `data-setgroup` and its OWN Save (grey until that group changed, via `syncSettingsSave` per `.sec`; a Save presents a `.conf` confirmation naming each changed value's before → after, e.g. "Change the auto sign-out period from 30 minutes to 45 minutes?"). Values live in a `SETTINGS` object (defined near `DEPARTMENTS`); the wired ones reassign live constants on commit (`applySettingsLive()`: `EXPORT_ROW_LIMIT`, `REPORT_DEADLINE_HRS`, `REBOOK_WINDOW` — all now `let`), the rest are screens-only (no engine). Numeric fields (`setNum`) are whole-numbers-only min–max with the one error pattern "Enter a whole number between [min] and [max] [unit]."; groups + defaults: Departments (staged CRUD), Sessions (autoSignout 30, 5–240), Exports (exportLimit 50000, 1k–250k), Interviews (reportDeadline 24 / rebookWindow 14; the candidate promise = reportDeadline + fixed `REVIEW_WINDOW_HRS` 24, shown live in the hint), Cohorts (attendanceMin 5 / leaderReconnect 10), Tal (talLimit 25, 5–200), Money, Working days (staged CRUD), Account closure (closureGrace 30, 7–90). **Money** carries the Change fee permission: its Save routes through a reason sheet (`SHEETS.moneyconfirm`, before/after + a required 1–500 Reason → `moneyReason`) THEN a step-up `askConfirm({stepup:true})` → `moneyCommit` (audit `high` with the reason); fields are payoutDelay (0–30 working days) + payoutRunDay (our `formDd`, Mon–Sun). **Departments** is now `[{name, status}]` (status active/retired — RETIRED not hard-deleted, staff records point at it; `deptNames()`=all, `activeDepts()`=the invite dropdown); **Working days** is `HOLIDAYS [{date, name}]` (Date/Name/Year columns, weekends refused). Both are STAGED in `S.setDraft.{dept,hol}` (working copies snapshotted from live on first mutation; go() clears them so leaving discards unsaved staged CRUD) and commit only on the group Save's confirm (`setdeptcommit` / `setholcommit`, applying renames onto `USERS[].dept`). — **11.2 NOTIFICATION TEMPLATES** (`settingsTemplates`, the Notification Templates tab): a `table()` of `NOTIF_TEMPLATES` (71 in-app templates across Internal staff / Talent Agent / Candidate / Cohort Leader recipients, each Condition-based or Informational; defaults are authored placeholder §74 until Design's copy lands, one line per event) — columns Notification / Recipient / Type / Sent when / On/Off / Last edited, Status + Recipient `filterDd`s, title-only search, paged 5, no add/delete (a new event needs a release). A row opens the EDITOR — a right slider (`SHEETS.tpledit` in `SIDE_SHEET_KEYS`, working copy `S.tplEdit`): a Show-this-notification toggle (`tpltoggle`, pending), Title (1–60, live count) + Body (1–500, live count) edited via `data-tplf` WITHOUT a render (trap 9, caret-safe; `S.tplField` tracks which field for placeholder insertion), allowed-placeholder chips (`data-tplins`, insert-at-caret; a token outside the event's set is refused on Save), a LIVE Preview (`tplRender` substitutes `NT_SAMPLE` values), Send-a-test (`tpltest`, posts to the actor's own `ADMIN_NOTIF`), Cancel / Save (`tplsave` — validates, sets `by`/`at` in the "12 Mar 2026, 14:30" format, audits). All admin-local in `tn-admin-portal.html`. (Maryam, 29 Sep 2026)
- A module WITH sub-pages carries a chevron at the row's end (`.sn-chev` in a `.sn-trail` group), so you can see it opens a tree; its queue count sits to the chevron's LEFT (`badge` then `chev`). The chevron flips down→up when the module is expanded; it shows wherever the labels do (the open desktop rail and the mobile drawer) and is hidden in the collapsed 72px rail, where the count keeps floating on the icon (`.sn-trail` goes `display:contents` there, so the collapsed rail is byte-for-byte as it was). (§120, Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- The admin's open drawer is WIDER (`--drawer-w:310px`, admin-scoped) so its long module names fit on one line — the candidate/leader/agent rails keep 260/280; and the open-rail count circle is a size smaller (18px box, 10px numeral via §63 §55). (§120.1b, Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- Status is plain COLOURED text, never a chip: `tag()` stamps `.statw` (green `--support-success-ink` / amber `--support-attention` / red `--support-error`, inks in §63 §53), no box. The categorical label chips that remain (roles, levels, tracks — `.tag`) are ROUND (`border-radius:999px`), admin-only via `[data-portal="admin"]`; the other portals keep the platform's radius-0. (§126, Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- A rating reads star-first: `★ 4.9`, the star inline (`display:inline-block` over the DS `svg{display:block}`), never stacked. The rating VALUE star is FILLED gold — `star` at `--star` in `starMark` (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "i never said to remove the fill of the yellow stars, fill them again", reversing the same-day `starOutline`), so every admin rating (the agents table + the agent detail figure) matches. What is OUTLINED instead is the DECORATIVE Rating-block mark — the star icon on the LEFT of the agent-detail Rating figure cell is `'starOutline'`, so it loses its black FILL (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "remove the black fill from the star icon on the left of the rating block"); a decorative mark is inked #111 (§29), so it reads as a black outline. (`ratingCell` / `starMark`, Maryam 7 & 15 Sep 2026)
- A COHORT IS NOT BASED ON A TRACK OR A COURSE (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "cohorts are now not based on a track or a course ... remove the track column and implement this ... wherever in the flows it is stated"). The Track column is gone from every cohort table (the Cohorts list and the dashboard's Cohort-health table); the New Cohort / Edit sheet has no Track field; the cohort detail drops its Track fact and its "{track} cohort" subtitle (now "Led by …"); the cohort Tal summaries no longer name tracks; and the leader flows (invite, allot, merge, move) no longer tie a leader or a target cohort to a track — merge offers any active cohort with room, not a same-track one. The `track` key lingers in the seed data, unread by the UI (a cohort is just a named peer group with a leader and its own sessions). COURSES keep their own track (the course catalogue is a separate entity). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- COHORT NAMES DO NOT RELY ON A LEVEL OR TRACK — they are generic "Cohort N" (Maryam 17 Sep 2026: "cohort names are strictly not relying on the levels, so please give other names to cohorts. like Cohort 1 and so on"). The four seed cohorts renamed Explorer 12 → **Cohort 1**, Explorer 14 → **Cohort 2**, Builder 3 → **Cohort 3**, Explorer 15 → **Cohort 4** (the orphan CANDS membership "Builder 4" → **Cohort 5**); every reference moved with them — `CANDS[].cohort`, `USERS[].cohorts` (keeping the `(member)`/`(leads)` suffix), the audit line, the seed comments, and the two "Led Explorer 9/7" past-cohort mentions in leader pitches (generalised, not renumbered). The New Cohort name placeholder is "e.g. Cohort 6". The leader's own `(Builder)`/`(Trailblazer)` bucket suffix is a person's level bucket, not a cohort name, and stays. The hifi leader portal already used generic names (Cohort 41/33). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- EPIC 7 — COHORTS & COURSE CATALOGUE (stories 7.1–7.6, Maryam 29 Sep 2026, `tn-admin-portal.html`, admin portal ONLY — the hifi leader/candidate portals keep their own status words this pass). FOUR gating decisions overrode the stories, each confirmed by Maryam before the build: (1) **COHORTS ARE NEVER LINKED TO A COURSE** ("cohorts are never linked to any course, we have updated this flow, do not follow this requirement of user stories") — so 7.2's "{Course} N" naming is NOT adopted (cohorts stay generic "Cohort N", the 17 Sep rule stands), the cohort list drops the course line + Course filter, the detail header drops course, and 7.6's "another cohort **on the same course**" is just "another suitable cohort". This also guts the 7.1 parts that depend on linkage: the *Running cohorts* / *Next start* columns, the per-course *Cohort size (min/max)*, the *Completed delay* on the course, and the *Start dates* list are all DROPPED. (2) **ADDING A COURSE NEVER CREATES A COHORT** ("i dont think that adding a course should auto create a cohort. so do not do anything like this") — no auto-creation; cohorts are created by hand as before. (3) **FOUR-STATE LIFECYCLE** (7.2/7.4) replaces active/draft/archived platform-wide in the admin: **Forming** (created, enrolling, start ahead) → **Running** (started, day 1–90) → **Completed** (the per-cohort delay after day 90) → **Cancelled** (terminal, via intervention). Admin only; hifi untouched, flagged. (4) **SIMULATION = states seeded + live human-triggered transitions**; time-based flips are shown as states, not ticked by a clock (there is no live clock in a static prototype). Per story:
  - **7.1 Course catalogue** (fuller catalogue REBUILT, reversing the 15/20 Sep stripping — these are Tal-recommendation inputs per 18.1, NEVER enrolment gates; a candidate with a level may enrol on any Live course). `KINDS.course` form: name (unique), Description (`t:'textarea'`, info icon "Tal reads this description when it chooses which course to recommend…"), **Track** (Explorer/Builder, no default), **Levels** multi (`t:'checks'`, the five rungs of the chosen track E1–E5/B1–B5, gated until a track is chosen and CLEARED + re-scoped on track change via a DOM-only `syncCourseLevels` hook in the `data-formddset` handler — no render, trap 9; need not be consecutive), **Intent** SINGLE-select (`t:'select'`, one of the three onboarding intents `INTENTS` — Maryam 29 Sep 2026: "the intent could be only one", reversing the initial multi; the catalogue column and detail show the one intent, the seed carries a single string, `intentSummary` still tolerates a legacy array), Length (read-only 90), **Chapter count** (1–90) + derived "90 days over N chapters, about X.X days per chapter", **LightSpeed course** (`t:'select'`), Price, Status radio Draft/Live. Catalogue columns: Course, Track, Levels, Intent, Status, Length, Chapters, Price. Filters: Status as a TAB STRIP (`filterStrip` All/Live/Draft/Archived with counts, Maryam 29 Sep 2026 "i need statuses in tabs" — matching the cohort list), plus Track (All/Explorer/Builder) and Intent (All + the three) as toolbar dropdowns. Search by name. Row actions View / Edit / **Archive** (Archive closes to new enrolment; `status:'archived'`). The table opts into the **bulk action bar** (`selectKind:'course'` + row `id`, `BULK_STATUS.course` = Archive): selecting rows shows Archive All / Delete All (a single row shows Edit / Archive / Delete), Maryam 29 Sep 2026. Add Course opens the create sheet (was a LightSpeed toast). **PRICE CHANGE = STEP-UP**: price is a normal field on CREATE; on EDIT it is read-only with a "Change price" kebab action that runs the reason + `askConfirm({stepup:true})` fee-change flow (7.1 → story 1.4), mirroring `feeStepup`. Trailblazer stays out of the V1 course catalogue (Track offers Explorer/Builder only). `desc`/`levels`/`intent`/`chapters`/`lsvt` are real fields on the record now; the seed courses were backfilled (§74 authored placeholder for desc where none existed).
  - **7.2 Cohort list** (`cohortsList`): columns Cohort / Status / Start date / Members (size/max) / Leader / Progress ("Day D of 90, chapter C of N", blank until Running). A below-minimum Forming cohort shows the shortfall on the start-date cell ("3 short, starts in 5 days"); an Unassigned Forming cohort reads Unassigned. Filters: Status strip (All/Forming/Running/Completed/Cancelled). Search by cohort name. (The 7.2 **Needs attention** toggle was built then REMOVED at Maryam's request, 29 Sep 2026 — `cohortNeedsAttn` stays as the helper the dashboard/summary read.) Sort start-soonest-first, paged at 5. Row actions View / Assign|Change Leader / Intervene. Export is the toast-only convention.
  - **7.3 Cohort detail** (`cohortsDetail`): facts band Start date / Progress (day/week + chapter) / Members (size / min–max) / Completed date (= start + 90 + the cohort's own `completeDelay`); a **missed-start** note when Forming and past its date (reason: below minimum / no leader / both). Leader panel: current leader + `leadFrom` assignment date + `leadHist` replacement history, or "No leader assigned" + Assign. Members table: real name, handle (`@firstname`, authored), approved level, enrolment date, course progress, membership state; row actions Open / Move / Remove. Progress line notes the LightspeedVT last-read. Sessions table kept. Header actions in a kebab (Edit / Assign-Change Leader / Intervene / Start / Cancel / Delete) with the state tag beside it.
  - **7.4 Lifecycle**: `cohortState`/`cohortMissed`/`evalStart` helpers. `evalStart(c)` re-evaluates the start conditions live and flips Forming→Running the moment a blocker clears (assigning a leader to an at-minimum overdue cohort, or the last member arriving) — the demonstrable live trigger. Manual "Start Cohort" (kebab) forces the same when min+leader are met. Completed and the completed-delay countdown are shown as states. Cancelled is via intervention only, terminal. `tag()` gains forming(amber)/running(green)/cancelled(red); completed(green) already shipped.
  - **7.5 Assign a Cohort Leader** (`SHEETS.assignlead`, cohort-centric, Assign on a leaderless cohort / Change where one is placed; Forming & Running only): leader dropdown = every account holding the Cohort Leader capability NOT currently leading an active (Forming/Running) cohort — candidate OR staff, **never an agent** (the old `leaderOpts` wrongly included agents); each option names the person, candidate/staff, and a candidate's level. Reason required on a change only; a change shows a confirmation prompt. On assign: `c.leadFrom`, a `leadHist` entry on a change, role ensured, audit + notification copy, then `evalStart`. Handover (discussion read-only, private threads not inherited, notes stay) is represented in the confirmation/audit copy + the history entry (there is no discussion data admin-side). One active cohort at a time; a cohort is never left leaderless (removal always names a replacement, per the existing 3.3 flow).
  - **7.6 Intervene** (`SHEETS.intervene:<id>` chooser → sub-sheets, only the interventions valid for the state): **Move start date** (Forming; date picker + reason), **Move a candidate** (Forming/Running; member + destination = any Forming/Running below max + reason; carries progress, no money moves), **Remove a candidate** (Forming/Running; reason + confirmation "loses their place and their course fee is not returned" — forfeits the fee), **Add a candidate** (Forming/Running; candidate with a level and no cohort place + reason; may exceed max; joins at the current chapter), **Cancel the cohort** (Forming/Running; each member gets an editable proposed destination from Forming/Running below max, else **Awaiting cohort**; reason + confirmation → Cancelled, members moved/awaiting, leader released). NO merge/dissolve (7.6: "There is no merging of cohorts") — the old `mergeco`/`dissolve` are removed. **Awaiting cohort** = `CANDS[].cohort = 'Awaiting'` sentinel, rendered specially; an Awaiting candidate is auto-placed into the next suitable cohort when one opens (the live trigger; keeps the paid place, carries progress, no new payment). Every intervention writes an audit line naming the actor, cohort, intervention, candidates affected and the reason. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 29 Sep 2026)
- EPIC 7 — THE MANUAL/AUTOMATIC MODE FRAMING + THE MANUAL-COHORT MODEL (Maryam 29 Sep 2026, second pass; `tn-admin-portal.html`, admin ONLY). The full 7.1–7.6 user stories were re-read and their **Cohort creation setting (11.1)** framing adopted: **Manual is the mode in force, Automatic is documented but NOT selectable.** This RECONCILES the first-pass "cohorts are never linked to a course" gating decision above — that decision IS Manual mode. Two gating answers Maryam gave: (a) **Manual framework only** — add the setting surface with Manual in force and Automatic shown-disabled; build only Manual behaviour; every Automatic-only course field (min/max cohort size, start dates) and cohort-list column (Cohort size, Next start, Running cohorts) stays hidden until Automatic is built; (b) **adopt the story's manual-cohort model**, reversing the first-pass min/cap model. Pieces:
  - **11.1 setting** — `SETTINGS.cohortCreation:'manual'` + `cohortMode()`; a **Cohort creation** group on the Platform Settings tab (`cohortModeGroup()`, between Interviews and Cohorts) showing Manual (filled accent disc, "In force" green tag) selected and Automatic (hollow disc, "Not yet available" amber tag, dimmed) — informational, no Save (there is nothing to change until Automatic ships).
  - **Every cohort is a MANUAL cohort** — it belongs to no course and has **NO minimum and NO maximum** (7.3/7.4/7.7). `KINDS.cohort` drops the `min`/`cap`/`chapters` fields; the create/edit form is **Cohort name · Completed delay (days, 0–30, def 7) · Cohort starts on**. The `min`/`cap`/`chapters` keys survive on the seed rows, unread. `placeAwaiting()` is now gated `cohortMode()==='automatic'`, so in Manual mode Awaiting candidates are **never** auto-placed (7.6: "In Manual mode … waits until staff add the candidate").
  - **Start condition = leader + ≥1 member + start date reached** (7.4), no minimum. `evalStart`, `cohortMissed` ("without a leader / with no members / both"), `cohortNeedsAttn` (no-leader only) and `cohortStartNote` (missed-start red tag only; a manual cohort is never marked below-minimum) all reworked. The list's Needs-attention toggle itself stays removed (29 Sep first pass); `cohortNeedsAttn` remains the helper the dashboard/summary read.
  - **Headcount shows ALONE** (no `/cap`) everywhere — the cohort list Members cell, the detail facts + subtitle, the assign-leader panel, the intervention destination options, the candidate-form cohort picker meta and the dashboard "How full" Q&A all read "N member(s)". `cohortProgress` is **day-only** ("Day D of 90", no chapter — a manual cohort has no single chapter count). Cohort naming keeps the generic "Cohort N" (17 Sep rule) and the list Cohort cell carries a **"Manual cohort" sub-line** (7.2's "the course beneath it, or 'Manual cohort'").
  - **Members table gains a Course column** (`memCourse(m)` per member, 7.3). Intervention destinations (`moveco`, `cancelco`) drop the max-capacity filter (no maximum). **Remove a candidate** branches on state (7.6): from a **Forming** manual cohort the paid enrolment **returns to Awaiting cohort** (fee kept, "Their paid enrolment returns to Awaiting cohort"); from a **Running** one the fee is forfeited (unchanged).
  - **Assign leader (7.5)** — `leaderPool` now excludes only an **external (candidate)** account already leading an active cohort; a **staff** account is listed whatever it already leads (may lead more than one).
  - **NOT adopted in Manual mode** (Automatic-only, hidden until built, or a prior explicit decision): the course **Cohort size / Next start / Running cohorts** columns; the course **min/max cohort size + start dates** fields; the course-level **Completed delay** (it lives on the manual cohort per 7.4/7.7, so the course form has none); the cohort-list **Course filter** (all cohorts are manual, so it offers nothing); course-driven **"{Course} N"** naming and course auto-creation. Course **Intent stays SINGLE-select** (the 29 Sep "the intent could be only one" decision stands, against 7.1's multi-select). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 29 Sep 2026)
- A COMPLETED COHORT KEEPS ITS ROSTER, AND ADDING A MEMBER TAKES NO REASON (Maryam, 8 Oct 2026). A finished cohort's graduates have moved on, so their `CANDS[].cohort` names their CURRENT place; the completed cohort reads its members off its own `alumni` id list (`cohortRoster(c)`, which `syncCohortSizes` and `cohortsDetail` both use), each row State "Completed", progress 100%. Cohort 7's six alumni are authored placeholder (§74). The Add Member sheet (`SHEETS.addmember`, "Add candidates to …") is a MULTI-SELECT picker alone, no Reason field: `formDdMulti` (options lead with the table's row-select box, the menu stays open while picking, the trigger reads "N selected", the picks show as round removable `.fdd-chip`s under the field, `data-formddmulti` DOM-only toggles, hidden `[data-f]` a comma list); one audit line per candidate added. A centred modal's form dropdown floats past the sheet edge: cap 360px (was 260, whose scroll ended flush with the sheet foot and read as clipped) with the rail flyout's shadow. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 8 Oct 2026)
- SUPER ADMIN SETTINGS OPEN READ-ONLY; NOTHING IS AN OPEN FORM ON FIRST VIEW (Maryam, 8 Oct 2026). Login & Security: Password shows only its heading, description and ONE grey button (no masked password); Phone number shows the FULL number as plain body text (not a read-only field) and ONE grey button — "Change Password" opens `SHEETS.secpw` (current / new / confirm), "Change Phone Number" (`openphone`) opens `SHEETS.secphone`, whose second step is the code to the new number (`S.secPhone === 'code'`); a validation miss keeps the modal open (`'stay'`). Platform: every numeric group and Money read as a `.kv-ends` record with an "Edit" in the heading row; Edit (`S.setEdit = <group>`) swaps in the fields with Cancel + Save, a committed save or Cancel returns to the record, leaving the page resets it. Departments and Working days have NO Discard / Save: "Add Department" / "Add Holiday" sit in the heading's action slot and add / rename / retire / restore / delete apply at once (`deptApply` / `holApply`, one audit line + toast each). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 8 Oct 2026)
- THE LEADER ROSTER KEBAB IS PLACED AGAIN AFTER THE RENDER SETTLES (8 Oct 2026, "the dropdown opened this far"). `placeLeadRowMenu` runs in the leader render wrapper, before the later passes that still move the content above the roster, so the menu landed ~130px off its row; it now also runs on a zero-delay `setTimeout` (not rAF, trap 17). Also: a cross-file `const` (e.g. `LEADER`) read during the boot render of a `#leader/...` deep link is in its temporal dead zone and `typeof` does NOT guard it — read it in a try. (lead.js / views.js, 8 Oct 2026)
- NO "INTERNAL STAFF" / "STAFF MEMBER" WORDING IN NOTIFICATION TEMPLATES — a recipient reads its ROLE NAME (Maryam, 8 Oct 2026). `NT_RCP.staff` is "Admin" (the Recipient filter too), and the internal group's rows name the role: "An admin's role is changed", "An admin's details were changed", "A deactivated admin account is restored", "{admin} can now lead a cohort"; the placeholder is `{admin}` (sample Dana Whitfield). The data key stays `staff`. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 8 Oct 2026)
- THE APP-BAR ACCOUNT CONTROL SHOWS THE PERSON'S NAME AND ROLE, ONE COMPONENT ON ALL FOUR PORTALS (Maryam, 8 Oct 2026). Order: bell, face, a two-line name/role stack (`.acct-id` > `.acct-n` + `.acct-r`), chevron. Name is `--t-desc` 13.5 at `--t-w-strong` 500 in `--text-primary`; role is `--t-label` 12.5 at 400 in `--text-secondary` (§63). Below 600 the stack is hidden (face + chevron only). The markup is ONE builder, `acctTrigger(img, ini, name, role, open)` in views.js, shipped as `dsAcctTrigger` (DS_BUILDERS); the agent (Owen Clarke · Talent Agent) and admin (`ME` · `staffRoleName`) portals call it; candidate reads `PF.general.name` · Candidate, leader `LEADER.n` · Cohort Leader. Change the builder or §78.3/§63, never a portal's copy. (§78/§63, views.js, Maryam 8 Oct 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN MY PROFILE FOLLOWS THE CANDIDATE PROFILE'S STYLING, ON ITS OWN CONTENT (Maryam, 8 Oct 2026). `settingsProfile` read view: no heading band, the `.idhead` (round portrait, name, email) with a ghost "Edit Details" pencil right, an eight-fact `.facts pf-facts` grid (Role / Job title / Department / Status / Member since / Invited by / Last signed in / Phone), then a "Permissions" `.skl` chip cloud. "Edit Details" (`S.meEdit`) is the candidate's in-place edit: Discard / Save in the head row (`.pfe-acts`), a three-across `.pfe-g pfe-g3` grid, only First and Last name editable, the rest `.pfe-ro` read-only. Replaces the stacked form + "Account" kv. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 8 Oct 2026)
- THE TALENT AGENT PORTAL HAS NO "View candidate profile" BUTTON (Maryam, 8 Oct 2026) — removed from the dashboard and Interviews call cards and the calendar popover; the cards carry Join alone. The recommendation form's "Your suggested level" drops its "Tal recommends {level}" line (Tal's level is stated once, in the paragraph above). The candidate Achievements points list drops "Sign in 100 times" (`PTS`, with `GAME.got`/`last`, `PTS_MK` and Cohort Champion's indexes shifted). (tn-agent-portal.html, hifi data.js/views.js, Maryam 8 Oct 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN TAL SUMMARY IS HIDDEN PORTAL-WIDE (Maryam 17 Sep 2026: "hide tal summary from the whole super admin portal"). The "Summary by Tal" `.talsum` band is dropped from BOTH `head()` and `detailHead()` (the `sum` arg is kept in the signature but no longer drawn, so no call site changes); the `dsTypeSummary` render pass is guarded to no-op when the band is absent. A list-page head is then only an empty breadcrumb, so an admin-local rule (`.app .modhead:not(:has(.ai-aura)):has(> .ph.ph-bare){display:none}`, `#admin-nosum`) hides that empty band while keeping the element in the DOM so `.modhead + .sec` first-section spacing still fires; a detail head keeps its back arrow. Admin-only — the other three portals keep their summaries. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN ADD-SESSION SHEET (`SHEETS.addsess`) schedules by a concrete DATE + TIME, not a "Week" (Maryam 17 Sep 2026: "instead of week selection we need to give date and time fields to logically set when this session will happen"). Fields are Session title / Date (`t:'date'`, our month-grid picker) / Time (`t:'select'`, business-hours dropdown) / Length; the **Mode** field and the "added to this cohort's outline" note are removed. `doaddsess` stores `{date, time, dur}` and derives a `week` from the cohort start (`sessionWeek`) so the roster table can still sort/label; a session with no date falls back. The Sessions table on the cohort detail: its "Week" column becomes "**When**" (shows `dpFmt(date) · time`, else "Week N" for a seed session), Mode renders `s.mode || '—'`, and it carries **no row-select checkboxes** (`{select:false}` — Maryam 17 Sep 2026: "remove the checkboxes from this table"). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- ON THE SUPER ADMIN COHORT DETAIL, "Add Session" and "Add Member" are ACCENT LINKS, not black buttons (Maryam 17 Sep 2026: "give add session text and icon accent color", "this add member button should also be like the add session button"): `.btn.acc-link btn-sm noic` — transparent, the leading `+` glyph and the label both `--accent-text`, no fill/border/padding (`.acc-link`, the payouts Release pattern). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 17 Sep 2026)
- The TALENT AGENT DETAIL (`agentsDetail`) drops the Tal summary band (`detailHead` gets no `sum`, keeping only the back arrow) and leads with an `.idhead` — the agent's `agentFace` PORTRAIT on the LEFT of the name, the state tag + listing actions on the right — mirroring the candidate detail (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "remove tal summary from this page" / "add image of the agent ... on the left of the name part"). (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- THE VERIFIED MARK beside a candidate or talent agent NAME (`.idverify` on the detail `.idhead`) is the scalloped **`verified` seal** glyph in green (`--support-success-ink`), NOT a green disc with a bare `check` (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "use the highlighted icon as the verified icon against candidate or talent agent", off a Hugeicons stroke-rounded reference). The seal carries its own tick, so `.idverify` sheds its background circle (just `color:--support-success-ink`) and the glyph draws at 18px. Shown on BOTH the candidate detail (`candidatesDetail`) and the talent agent detail (`agentsDetail`, before the rating). The DS icon set this portal links is stroke-based on a `0 0 24 24` box (`inner('verified')` → a stroked seal), so the marker keeps `viewBox="0 0 24 24"` — this is NOT the hifi portal's Material Symbols set. Admin-local. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- A CLICKABLE admin table row (`tr.clk`) lights its FIRST-COLUMN NAME on hover — the black anchor text turns `--accent-text` (#c2332d, the accent as ink, §3); the grey `.t-caption` sub-line beside it keeps its own colour (its own rule beats the inherited accent, so nothing names it). `cursor:pointer` on the row too. Written as a PLAIN `:hover` in the admin portal's inline `<style>` because the DS's `:hover`s are disarmed (`.__nh`, trap 6) and never armed in this file, so a portal-local rule is the one that fires. Admin-only. (tn-admin-portal.html `#admin-row-hover`, Maryam 13 Sep 2026)
- Every list a Super Admin sees has create, edit, delete and status changes — through the `.sheet` and the `.conf` dialog, and every change writes an audit line. (admin 4 Sep)
- A CSV EXPORT NEVER TRIGGERS AN INSTANT BROWSER DOWNLOAD — no `a.click()` on a `data:`/blob URL, because that pops Chrome's OWN download chip in the top-left, which is browser chrome we do not control and do not want (Maryam 25 Sep 2026: "we have decided not to show the downloaded toast on the top left ... like how we are doing in the other modules"). A prototype export (and every "Download receipt" / "Download All" / "Download as zip" action) is TOAST-ONLY: it checks the empty/`EXPORT_ROW_LIMIT` refusals, writes ONE `audit(...)` line (`notice`), and confirms with the app's OWN toast (the bottom-right `.pt-toasts` host, `toast(...)`), whose copy narrates the story's model — "A download link will reach your notifications, valid 7 days." No file leaves the browser; the real file + the 7-day notification link need a backend this prototype does not have. This is the standing convention `bulktxdl` and the per-row receipts already set; `usersexport` / `staffexport` / `levellingexport` were retrofitted to it (their `a.click()` + CSV-string build removed). Applies to EVERY export/download on the portal. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 25 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN PORTAL IS BUILT AGAINST THE SUPER ADMIN JOURNEY (Draft v1.0, 6 Sep 2026: sixteen use cases SA-01…SA-16, 51 alternatives, 37 exceptions; 2 confirmed, 7 open, 5 blocked). Every case gets its SURFACE with its primary alternative drawn; every OPEN or BLOCKED branch is a line on the Rulings Register (`DECISIONS` tagged `sa` + `kind`, drawn at `home/rulings`), never an invented control. The register (in the Dashboard sub-nav) is the ONE place those branches live: module pages carry NO inline ruling note — Maryam, 7 Sep 2026, "remove all such sections like 'SA-09 · Account actions — Open ruling' … this is not the info a user would want to see." This REVERSES the earlier "one `ruling('SA-nn')` note per waiting page"; `ruling()` is now a no-op, its call sites removed, and the SA data behind the register is untouched. (Maryam 7 Sep 2026, §129)
- The register's owners are FIRST NAMES (Umair · role scope, ACL, portal architecture; Derek · override authority, who holds the role; Reed · pricing and settlement) — the seed and the journey spell the surnames differently and the portal does not pick. (§129)
- The four highest-severity actions — grant Super Admin, revoke it, start View As, release all payouts — confirm through a STEP-UP authenticator code (`askConfirm({stepup:true})`, six digits), switchable in Login & Security (`SEC.stepup`). A two-step sheet carries its values in `S._pending` from the sheet to the confirmation. (SA-02 A1, 7 Sep 2026)
- A Super Admin grant is TIME-BOXED BY DEFAULT (30/90 days or permanent), needs a second approver and a reason, and expires on its own (`expireGrants()` runs on every render). Refused: self-escalation and a holder who is also an agent or leader; a sole holder grants single-approver, logged high. Revocation names its kind (planned / emergency / expiry) and refuses the last remaining holder. (SA-01, SA-16)
- A capability granted across the Program / Business wall (`CAP_SIDE`) is WARNED and asked, not refused — the refusal is the open ruling. (SA-10 E1)
- VIEW AS is read-only by default: `S.viewAs` opens the `.viewas` bar (§129) under the app bar naming who, the scope, that interview video is excluded, and since when; the router refuses every write while it is open; another admin and a user with an interview today are refused; both ends and every line in between are audited. (SA-11)
- A level override offers MVP levels only (`levelOpts` — Trailblazer stays in the data model, off that picker), asks a DOWNWARD move twice, records the disclosure choice, says when it lands mid-cohort, keeps a per-record history and can be reversed with both lines kept. Bulk correction is its own sheet. (SA-04) — EXCEPTION: the Add/Edit Candidate record form (`KINDS.cand` Level field) offers the FULL ladder `LVL_CODES` (E1–E5, B1–B5, T1–T5), not the MVP subset (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "show all levels here, from e1 to t5"), so a candidate can be created or edited at any level including Trailblazer. The COURSE field is READ-ONLY WHEN EDITING (Maryam 20 Sep 2026: "the course row should show the course name and it should not be editable" / "the candidate is taking Communicating with Impact ... show this in the edit window ... but it should not be editable") — a `t:'text'` `ro:true` locked field via `get: () => candActiveCourse() || 'No course yet'`, where `candActiveCourse()` reads the candidate's IN-PROGRESS course (else most-recent completed) off `CAND_COURSE_LIST` (the admin's authored per-candidate course read, §74 — so it agrees with the detail page's Courses section, e.g. "Communicating with Impact"). `update` no longer writes `c.course` (a candidate's course follows their own enrolment, not the admin). On CREATE it stays the live-course picker.
