(tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 20 Sep 2026) — The COHORT picker names each cohort's MEMBER COUNT, RIGHT-ALIGNED on the option row (Maryam 22 Sep 2026: "show the member count against a cohort name in the cohort dropdown" / "the count should be right aligned on the row"): the count (`${x.size} / ${x.cap||10}`, the same `size / cap` the roster and cohort tables show) rides the option's 4th tuple element (`ometa`), NOT baked into the label. `formDd` renders a 4th element as a `.fdd-meta` span and puts `.has-meta` on the row (and on the trigger's `.fdd-val` when that option is selected) — `.fdd-opt.has-meta` goes `display:flex` and `.fdd-meta{margin-left:auto}` pins the count to the row's right edge; the pick handler carries `.has-meta` onto the trigger so the collapsed field shows the count right-aligned too. The option VALUE stays the bare name (what `create`/`update` write to `c.cohort`), and "No cohort yet" takes no meta. Admin-local CSS + `formDd`; a 4th tuple element is a general, backward-compatible `formDd` feature (plain selects are untouched). `c.size` is kept current by `syncCohortSizes()` in the render pipeline, so it is accurate at open. And the candidate form carries NO info block — the `KINDS.cand` `help` note ("A candidate is also an account…") is removed (Maryam 22 Sep 2026: "remove the info block in the form"); other KINDS keep their own `help`. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 22 Sep 2026)
- THE SUPER ADMIN CANDIDATE DETAIL SHOWS "LINKED ACCOUNTS" = the candidate's SOCIAL accounts (Maryam 20 Sep 2026: "linked accounts means social accounts ... show the linked accounts after the skills section", with the candidate-portal Linked Accounts tab as the reference). `candSocials()` renders the candidate portal's `.lacc`/`.lacc-grid` cards (§105, shipped in the DS this portal links) READ-ONLY, appended inside `candCV` AFTER the Skills section (order: … Skills → Linked accounts → Quiz results). ONLY THE CONNECTED accounts are shown (Maryam 22 Sep 2026: "only show the linked accounts") — `candSocials` filters `SOCIAL_ADM` to `CAND_LINKED[s.k]`, so the "Not connected" cards (their placeholder-disc + no-CTA state) are dropped; the whole section is omitted when the candidate has linked none (no empty grid under the heading). Each surviving card is a brand LOGO on its own `0 0 24 24` box (trap 7 brand-mark exception) tinted by `--brand`, showing the handle + the candidate's photo (`agentFace`) + a green "Linked". Non-interactive: `.lacc-ro` gives a default cursor, no `data-link` toggle. `SOCIAL_ADM` (SVGs + handles) and `CAND_LINKED` (linkedin/instagram/twitter on) are authored placeholder (§74) mirroring the candidate UI. (tn-admin-portal.html, Maryam 20–22 Sep 2026)
- Deactivating an account states its ROLE-SPECIFIC consequences before the press (candidate paused from visibility; an agent's in-flight interviews marked `reassign`; a leader's cohorts unled; sponsored candidates unsponsored; a mid-flight payment named). Merge is a sheet; hard delete keeps receipts and lets a recording run out its retention. (SA-09)
- A refund carries its KIND (interview / cohort pro-rata / sponsored employer pays / goodwill credit) and its source; approval is refused while a chargeback is in flight on the same charge and warns when the agent's payout has already settled; with no threshold set every refund is the Super Admin's. (SA-06)
- `audit()` carries a before/after (`{from, to}`); the log has a date facet and a READ-ONLY line sheet — a sheet with no `ok` draws no save button and its Cancel reads Close. (SA-15)
- The admin signs in through the design system's auth composition: `.auth-card` is a CHILD of `.app` (one element wearing both classes matches nothing), email + password then the authenticator code; log out returns to it; a deep-linked hash skips it. No role picker for a one-role account. (SA-02)
- The TAL ONBOARDING gate opens on CHAT, never the spoken voice intro (Maryam, 9 Sep 2026: "hide this screen, the user will directly land on the chat screen"). ob.js's `S.obMode` default is `'chat'` AND `setStage('onboard')` sets `'chat'` too — the reset was the half still forcing `'voice'`, so the STAGE picker walked back into the orb. The voice machinery stays, unreachable. The chat WELCOME (`obChatScreen`, `S.obChatOpen===false`) draws NO dock/field at the foot — the way forward is the body's "Let's Get Started!" button; the composer and the question thread arrive together only once it is pressed (`live`). Its greeting is **"Welcome to TALENTnext, Maryam!"** — `OB_CHAT[0]`, kept EXACTLY as supplied (the wordmark casing is Maryam's; supplied copy is never silently normalised). (§107, ob.js + views.js `setStage`, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- **THE LOAD CONVENTION — "add a load on this click" MEANS THE TN LOADER FOR 2 SECONDS BETWEEN THE TWO SCREENS** (Maryam, 18 Sep 2026, a standing default). When Maryam says to add a load / loader on a click, it means: on that click, play the three-blade `.tn-loader` (the `loader.html` asset — near-black `--icon-primary` blades, 90px, `tn-relay` animation, centred on the destination's ground) for **2 seconds**, then complete the navigation to the next screen. Implement it by holding a loading flag, `render()` to paint the loader, and a `setTimeout(…, 2000)` (never rAF — trap 17) that clears the flag and performs the real navigation; keep the loader on the CURRENT stage's render path so it paints before the handover. The hifi onboarding is the worked example (`S.obLoading` + `obLoaderScreen`, on both "Let's Get Started" and "Find an Agent"). 2000ms is the one duration; do not invent per-click timings unless Maryam names one.
- THE ONBOARDING QUESTIONS ARE A STEPPED SURVEY, NOT THE CHAT THREAD (Maryam, 18 Sep 2026, with a reference). IT IS THE REFERENCE IN LIGHT MODE — the reference is dark, the product is light, so the palette is translated, not copied (Maryam 18 Sep 2026: "we need to show it on light mode so the colors will be different"): white `--background` ground, `--text-primary`/`--text-secondary` ink, `--layer-02` pills on a `--rule` hairline, an accent-tinted selected pill (`color-mix(--accent 8%, --background)`) with an `--accent` radio disc, `--rule` progress track with an `--accent` fill, and the platform's own `.btn-s`/`.btn-p` for Back/Next. "Let's Get Started" on the welcome (`obChatScreen`) now plays a **2-second loader** (`obLoaderScreen`, `S.obLoading` — the three-blade `.tn-loader` relay off the `loader.html` asset, on the survey's light ground) and then advances `S.obStep` to 1 (with §107 §0g's leave animation) instead of opening the old chat thread; `obScreen` paints `obSurveyScreen()` for steps 1..`OB_N` and keeps the read-back on its `.auth-card`. Each survey screen is: a SINGLE progress bar pinned to the frame's TOP WALL (`.ob-sv-top`, `top:0`, full width) whose fill TRACKS THE STEP — `(S.obStep - 1) / OB_N` — so it fills on Next and RETREATS on Previous, and does NOT move when an option is selected (Maryam 18 Sep 2026: "the top bar should fill in when I click on next, not when I select an option ... on going back the bar should also go back"); question one is 0% and the completion screen 100%; it ANIMATES from its last width via a keyframe fed `--ob-from`/`--ob-to` (the bar node is recreated each render, so a plain transition can't tween — `S.obBarFrom` carries the previous %); a brand row under the bar (`.ob-sv-head`) with the TALENTnext wordmark LEFT and **"You're almost there!"** (`.ob-sv-hint`, `--t-desc` `--text-secondary`) RIGHT, aligned (Maryam 18 Sep 2026); the `borb` orb TOP-LEFT (`.ob-sv-orb`, `borbMark('tal-mk', live)`) so it reads as Tal asking; the QUESTION ITSELF is the heading (`obTitle` in `.ob-sv-title` at `--t-h1`) — NO theme title and NO description line (Maryam 18 Sep 2026: "i want the questions to be a heading and forget about a description"); the question column is vertically CENTRED (`.ob-survey .main{align-items:center}`) and ~720px wide; radio PILLS (`.ob-sv-o`, `data-ob`/`data-obv` — the existing selection handler; `--layer-02` fill, NO border in the default view, an `--accent` edge + accent-tint fill + filled `--accent` disc when chosen; label `--t-body` 13.5, desc `--t-label` 12.5 `--text-secondary`); and a foot (`.ob-sv-foot`) with NO Previous on question one, a light-grey `--layer-02` **"Previous"** BUTTON with BLACK text + arrow from question two (`data-obgo`, LEFT), and a black `.btn-p` **Next** (RIGHT) — both content-width, gated by `obReady`. The left rail divider (`.app::after`) is suppressed on `.ob-survey` like the other full-bleed onboarding scenes (§14). The terminal screen is `obDoneScreen` (replacing the old read-back, Maryam 18 Sep 2026): the same light `.ob-survey` composition with the orb, a **"You're all set"** heading + one line, the journey steps (`obJourney` — quiz done, Interview & Levelling next), and a **"Find an Agent"** `data-obdone` CTA that sets `S.recKey`, writes the profile fields, then plays the 2s loader again before landing on the dashboard. It is a NEW SKIN over the existing step machinery — selection, Back/Next, the read-back, completion and the answer readers are unchanged; the chat machinery (`obThread`/`obCompose`/`obProgress`/`obAsk`/`obSay`) is kept defined but unreachable, and `obQuestion` (the old stepped screen) is removed. Type from §63's role tokens, radius 0 on pills and buttons (only the radio marks are round — CLAUDE.md's "build the reference in our components"). No free-type composer on the survey (client chose the full stepped model). (§107, ob.js, Maryam 18 Sep 2026)
- ~~The LIVE onboarding chat carries a PROGRESS BAR at the top~~ (SUPERSEDED 18 Sep 2026 by the stepped survey above; `obProgress`/the chat thread are no longer reached). The LIVE onboarding chat carries a PROGRESS BAR at the top (`obProgress`, §107.p, Maryam 9 Sep 2026, with a reference): an h3 **"You're almost there!"** left-aligned above a segmented bar, both centred on the same `minmax(0,720px)` column the thread and field use. One segment per question (`OB_N`); a filled segment is `--accent`, the rest are a plain `--rule` (#efefef) fill with NO border (Maryam 9 Sep 2026 — reversing the earlier #fcfcfc fill + #efefef hairline). Segments are 999px pills (a stated §56 curve, off the reference). `done` is `min(OB_N, max(answered, S.obQi))` — NOT `S.obQi` alone, because `obAnswer` zeroes `obQi` for the typing beat, and a bar keyed on it drops to zero while Tal loads the next question (Maryam 9 Sep 2026: "this should not happen"); the answered count only grows, so the bar holds through the beat and still lights the live question. §63 owns the h3 type (`.t-h3`). Live chat only. (§107, ob.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- THE ONBOARDING QUESTIONS ARE THE CLIENT'S FIVE (`OB_Q`, Maryam 21 Sep 2026, supplied list) — SUPERSEDES the quiz-led six below: (1) **Where are you right now?** (In a job I want to move up in / In a job I don't plan to stay in / Running something of my own, or trying to start it / Between things); (2) **Which of these sounds most like you? Pick one.** (Top Performer / Athlete / Leader / Entrepreneur / Not Sure / Team Player); (3) **What do you do now?** — FREE TEXT (`t:'text'`, placeholder "Operations coordinator, student, own a detailing business"); (4) **What industry is that in?** — our DROPDOWN (`t:'dd'`, `dd()`; Security / Franchise ownership / Retail / Food and hospitality / Trades and construction / Healthcare / Transportation and logistics / Sales / Education / Technology / Student / Other) whose **Other** opens a text field; (5) **How many years have you been working?** (Under 1 / 1 to 3 / 3 to 7 / 7 to 15 / 15 or more). NO option DESCRIPTIONS — every option is `[key,label]` and the survey no longer draws `.ob-sv-od` (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "remove the desc against each option as well"). The completion screen (`obDoneScreen`, "You're all set" + journey + "Find an Agent") is KEPT unchanged (Maryam: "just keep the last one where we tell the step they are on"). The two free-text fields (Q3 + Q4's Other) write into `S.ob` on each keystroke WITHOUT a render — a `[data-obtext]` input listener toggles the Next button's `disabled` in place off `obReady()` (the caret trap, trap 9/17); the dropdown re-renders on pick like a radio, its value read back from `S.ddVal['ob-'+k]`. `data-obdone` writes `PF.general.role` (Q3), `.industry` (Q4's label / Other text) and `.years` (Q5 label); the quiz-led `band`/`why`/`intent` and their readers are gone — `obAgent` falls back to the first recommended agent and `obMemo` (guarded on `S.ob.why`) returns nothing. hifi-only. (§107, ob.js, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- The onboarding QUESTIONS (`OB_Q`, 6 now): where → **industry** → **years** → band → why → **intent** (Maryam 9 Sep 2026: "What industry are you in? should come right after Where are you right now? and then the experience will come after the industry question"). `intent`'s heading is now "What do you want out of the next 90 days? This will help choose you your course category." (renamed from "Where are you trying to go?"). The old `want` question ("What do you want out of the next 90 days?") is REMOVED; its readers went with it — the `obMemo` row (ai2.js) and the read-back's "What you want" row now read `intent`, `OB_SPINE`/`obHeard` were reordered to match (each `st` is the question's step), and `obFit` quotes `obLabel('why')` by key rather than `obHeard()[2]` (the reorder moved `band` into that slot). `OB_N`/`obLast`/the "of N" count and the progress bar all derive from `OB_Q`. The onboarding welcome LEDE (`.ob-chat-lede`) is `--text-secondary` #828282 (a description, §63 §1). A new Tal turn UNFOLDS its height (`.ob-m:last-child .ob-m-t`, `ob-unfold` max-height) so a tall question card grows in rather than popping (Maryam 9 Sep 2026: "smoothly appear not a jerk"). (§107/§63, ob.js + ai2.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- The candidate PROFILE tabs are My Profile / **My Courses** / Notifications / Privacy Settings — "Become a Cohort Leader" is HIDDEN (Maryam 9 Sep 2026); `pfLead` stays defined and unreachable. **My Courses** (`pfCoursesView`, tab `courses`) lists the candidate's courses as `.acc.ol` accordion rows — collapsed to name + status (Completed / In progress / Not started), open to the four-cell `statCell` insight band the course pages draw (Chapters done / Assessment average / Time invested / Tasks on time). Each row leads with the course COVER on the left (`.crs-thumb`, 56×38, `courseArt` by the row's level — Maryam 9 Sep 2026). `MY_COURSES` is authored placeholder progress (flagged, §74 — no per-candidate course telemetry in the seed). The candidate NAME carries a `.rank-chip` "Rank #N" beside it (`GAME[stage].rank`, the real star-rank index) — a light-red pill (`color-mix` accent 12%) with `--accent-text` ink, `.t-label` size, only on stages that have a rank. (§105/§63, views.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- The COHORT LEADER dashboard carries a **"Certified Cohort Leader"** badge right after the call cards (`leadCertBanner`, lead.js) — the candidate's `.certban` completion banner reused (the shape ships in the DS), with no dismiss (a standing credential). The mark reuses `CERT_ART.explorer` as a placeholder (§74 — no Certified-Cohort-Leader asset in the seed; a real WebP is the file to drop in). "View" opens `leadProfile`. (lead.js, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- A `.rec-lab` "Tal recommends" label inks `--on-dark` on ANY `.dark-card`, not only `.rec-dark` (§63, Maryam 9 Sep 2026: "tal recommends text is missing with the star") — the enrolment card is a `.dark-card` and its label was setting `--text-primary` black-on-black, so only the sparkle showed. Both dark selectors are (0,5,0), beating the base (0,4,0).
- The Tal CHAT empty-state description (`.ask-page .tal-hero p`) follows the onboarding lede's size — `--t-sec-size` / 26 (§63 §5275, Maryam 9 Sep 2026, reversing the 4 Sep drop to `--t-desc-size`); the two greeting-plus-lede screens read at one size. And the chat header's frost is LIGHTER: `.ask-top` white .72→.55 / blur 12→8, its `::after` fade .72→.4 / blur 6 (§53, Maryam 9 Sep 2026: "lighten the tal row white blur"). (§63/§53, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- The Tal chat screen: the WASH is full-bleed (painted on `.main`, under the margins) but the CONTENT keeps its normal column — `.main`'s `clamp(--s06,12cqw,200px)` gutter stays, do NOT zero it (tried, reversed). There is NO divider under the `← Tal` header (§51.2's border off). The ground is now a TOP-AND-BOTTOM gradient, NOT a centred ellipse (Maryam 9 Sep 2026, Figma 892:7220, "follow the same values"): two `--accent`-at-5% bands over white — `linear-gradient(186.615deg, rgba(189,42,42,.05) 11.018%, rgba(255,255,255,.05) 52.274%)` at `top`, the same mirrored (angle `353.385deg`) at `bottom`, each `100% 590px` `no-repeat`. Bleeds down from the top edge and up from the bottom, white between. This REPLACES the 8 Sep centred rose ellipse (`radial-gradient(100% 40% at 50% 46%, rgba(245,75,135,…))`). The SAME two bands are the onboarding ground (§107) — one frame feeds both. Chat is candidate/leader only (§53); the token is `--askv-ground-wash`. (Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- The Tal FLOATING FIELD (`.askdock > .askline`, on every page, all portals) follows Figma 875:6598 (Maryam 9 Sep 2026, "follow this ui completely"): a white 999px pill, a solid **1px `--rule` #efefef border** (Maryam 9 Sep 2026: the static edge is a plain hairline, not the old gradient border; a same-day #828282 was tried and reversed — the animating comet rides over it), padding 16/24. Left group (**12** apart): the 32px Tal orb, the label **"Ask Tal"** (14px Regular `--text-secondary`, NOT "Ask Tal anything"), then the example question (14px SemiBold `#626262`). Right group (**16** apart): mic then arrow, bare glyphs (no box) from our Material Symbols set at **22px** (a step over the node's 20 — "a little larger than current", which was 16/20). The ANIMATING LINE (`.ai-run` comet, `AI_RUN` in ai4.js + each hand-written portal) takes the node's gradient — `linear-gradient(90.13deg, rgba(255,255,255,.7) 0%, rgba(255,110,36,.7) 49.04%, rgba(255,55,51,.7) 75%, rgba(255,255,255,.7) 100%)` — as **four** SVG stops at 70% (white/`#ff6e24`/`#ff3733`/white; the 22.6% `#d551d7` magenta stop was DROPPED, Maryam 9 Sep 2026) on a HORIZONTAL gradient (`x2=1 y2=0`, replacing §70's diagonal). Same ramp on all three portals. (§63, §117, ai4.js + agent/admin, Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- THE MIC IS SMALLER THAN THE SEND ARROW in the Tal bars (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "reduce the icon size of the mic icon" — the floating bar AND the chat-screen field). The mic is **18px**, the send stays **22px**. Two classes in two layers: the floating dock's `.askdock .askline-mic svg` in §117 (kept at (0,3,1) to beat §105.548's 16px dock rule), and the chat field's `.ask-page .askfield-mic svg` in §118 (the chat field uses `.askfield-mic`, not `.askline-mic`). Crosses to the DS (agent portal's bar follows). (§117/§118, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- The Tal chat header has NO BLUR — it is a SOLID white bar (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "remove the blur from the top header"). `.ask-top` stays `position:absolute` over the top of the page (`.ask-page` is `relative`) so the thread still scrolls behind it and `.ask-thread` still opens at `padding-top:92px`, but the band is now `var(--askv-column)` (#fff, opaque) with NO `backdrop-filter`; its `::after` keeps a plain white→transparent GRADIENT fade (no blur) so a line rising into the bar softens to white before the cut. This SUPERSEDES the 8 Sep frosted `rgba(255,255,255,.72)` + `blur(12px)` band and the 9 Sep lighten (.55 / blur 8) — the `backdrop-filter` read as a grey smudge over the top-right of the conversation. §53, candidate/leader (crosses to the DS). (Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- The chat thread and rail draw NO SCROLLBAR — the platform is scrollbar-less (§12 hides `.main`'s, §02/§03/§09 the strips'), and `.ask-thread` (the chat scroller, §53) + `.sn-main` (the rail) were the two vertical scrollers §12 missed, so on a machine set to always-show scrollbars the chat had the OS grey slab down its right. §136 hides them exactly as §12 hides `.main` (`scrollbar-width:none` + `::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}`); `.tal-body` (the floating panel thread) too. Scrolling still works (wheel/trackpad). NOT a slim custom bar — the rest of the app shows none. `.calw`'s deliberately-thin bar (§41) is left alone. All portals via the DS. (§136, Maryam 21 Sep 2026) — THE ONE SCROLLER THAT KEEPS A VISIBLE BAR IS THE NIL RUN-UP (`.nil-scroll`), and it is REFINED, not the OS slab (Maryam 4 Oct 2026: "reduce the scroller bar width to half of what it is right now, and its color should be #efefef"): ~8px wide (about half the ~15px native) with a `--rule` #efefef thumb on a transparent track, round ends; `scrollbar-width:thin`+`scrollbar-color` for Firefox, `::-webkit-scrollbar` for Chrome. It reads the `--rule` token, not the hex. hifi/NIL only (`30-nil.css`, excluded from the DS); everywhere else the platform stays scrollbar-less above. (§136/§30, Maryam 4 Oct 2026)
- A Tal chat response follows Figma 882:6701 (Maryam 9 Sep 2026, "apply the same look and feel to the other responses ... follow everything"). Its parts, down the content column, sit **20px** apart:
  - TITLE (`.tw-h`) is Medium (`--t-w-strong`) in `--text-primary`, sentence case, 13.5px — no longer the small grey uppercase eyebrow. The `twIc` mark is KEPT (§39.13's legibility argument stands; the reference simply did not draw it). (§63)
  - The STAT TABLE (`.tw-lines`) is a CARD again — `--surface-2` fill, 1px `--rule`, **radius 16** (a stated Tal-widget radius exception), padding 12/20, rows 12 apart with a `--rule` HAIRLINE between each. Label (`.tw-lines b`) is strong `--text-primary` in a 50px column; value is Regular `--text-secondary`. This REVERSES §39.1's "no inner frame" — the card returns, around the figures only (title and action stay on the bubble ground). (§39, §51, §63)
  - BULLETS (`.tw-list`) lead with a 12px red sparkle (`--ai-star` mask in `--accent`), 10px gap, `--text-secondary` text. (§39)
  - The ACTION button (`.tw-btn`, primary) is a LINK now: NO fill, NO border, NO pill — `--accent-text` text with a trailing `--accent-text` arrow (`I.arrowRight`, re-added to hifi's `twBtn`; agent/admin already had it), sentence case (`.tw-btn` dropped from §63's capitalize list). This REVERSES the 8 Sep red-fill pill. The ghost stays a quiet `--accent-text` label, no arrow. (§39, §63, views.js)
  - FOLLOW-UP CHIPS (`.tw-chips .chip-tal`) are the white rounded pill with a red star + red text — the greeting chips' shape (rounded to 999px here). (§39)
  - The LEVEL LADDER (`.tw-rungs`) is h40 segments in three states — passed `.done` black (`--gray-100`), current `.on` `--accent`, future `--rule` fill with muted text; `wLadder` marks the rungs before the current one `.done`. (§39, views.js)
  All four portals via the DS. (Maryam 9 Sep 2026, Figma 882:6701)
- The Cohort Leader has its OWN chat answers, not the candidate's: `lead5.js` (loads after ai8) wraps `talReply` with a `S.portal==='leader'`-gated `LEAD_ROUTES` table that intercepts before the candidate routes and answers the leader's questions with leader widgets (brief, attention list, cohort table, evaluations, draft check-ins), reading `LEAD_*` data. A leader question that matches no leader route falls through to the shared chain, which already owns the leader's REFUSALS (`cand()`→`leadNA`, off-topic, the support catch-all). Spec + coverage: `docs/tal-scenarios/02-cohort-leader-tal-scenarios.md` §4 (L-01…L-24). (Maryam 8 Sep 2026)
- The chat field's mic records a voice message in place (`.askfield.rec` + `.askrec`), built to the ChatGPT reference: a cancel disc, a LIVE waveform driven by the real mic amplitude (newest bar right, quiet past trailing left as dots; a smoothed synthetic meter when the mic is denied), a stop that transcribes into the field to edit, a send that transcribes and posts. Between press and words is a `.rectx` "Transcribing…" beat. Content is vertically centred in the pill. Over http/https the words are the reader's; where the mic or speech service is unreachable (e.g. `file://`), one of Tal's suggested questions stands in so stop/send always land a real, sendable message — never a silent no-op. The floating bar never records inline — its mic opens the chat and begins the take there. Candidate/leader only (ai4.js), not in the DS. (§121)
- Confirm every delete and log-out in a modal before acting. (memory)
- A confirmation is the design system's `.conf` centred-icon dialog (icon disc, title, description, button row — no header rule, no `.x`), NOT a plain `.sheet`; the disc icon names the action (logout → the logout glyph). The agent portal's logout reads "Are you sure you want to log out?" / "If you want to logout please confirm your action below". (Maryam 7 Sep 2026, agent portal)
- THE `.conf` MODAL IS ONE TREATMENT ON EVERY PORTAL, and it is the Super Admin's (Maryam 21 Sep 2026: "follow the same padding, same spacing/rows gaps, same icon ui of single solid bg and white icon on the other portals modals as well"). The disc mark is a SINGLE SOLID `--accent` circle with a WHITE (`--on-accent`) glyph — no light-tinted inner disc, no §105/§109 halo rings, no per-kind glyph hue (success/danger/neutral all read accent + white; the glyph carries the meaning). The frame is a 16px (`--s05`) outer padding on `.conf`, the body pays none (`.conf-b` padding 0, its flex column keeping the 12px `--s04` row gap between mark/title/fee/desc), and the button row sits 16px (`--s05`) below the text (`.conf-a` padding-top). This SUPERSEDES §105's tinted-red halo disc and §109's green success halo; it is stated once in `134-confmodal.css` (crosses to the DS) so candidate/leader/agent match the admin, whose own local copy (`tn-admin-portal.html`) is now the same values. (§134, all four portals, Maryam 21 Sep 2026)
- On the SUPER ADMIN portal the `.conf` disc icon is `--on-accent` WHITE on the accent circle, in every confirm modal (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "the icon in the circle needs to be white in all modals" — reversing the 14 Sep black glyph); and the DESTRUCTIVE confirm button (`.btn-p.danger`) fills the brand `--accent`, not the DS's darker `--danger-ink` maroon (Maryam 15 Sep 2026: "the deactivate button color should be accent"). So a danger confirm reads as the accent circle + white glyph over an accent Deactivate button. Admin-local, in `tn-admin-portal.html`. (Maryam 15 Sep 2026)
- The agent Profile edits IN PLACE, never in a modal, following the candidate profile's exact UI: each editable section's Discard/Save live in its heading row as a `.pfe-acts` group — "Discard Changes" (danger + `I.delete`) and "Save Changes" (`.btn-p` + `I.check`) — and the read control is a PENCIL-only `.pfe-ic` icon. "General details" edits Name, read-only Email (`[readonly]`+`.pfe-ro`) and About (photo carries the `.idphoto-edit` pencil). "General details" also edits the **Interview fee** — an ordinary `.inp` prefilled with `money(v.fee)` in the same auto-fit grid as Name and Email (Maryam 7 Sep 2026); the read view's "Your fee" card and this field open at the same number, and `SHEETS.fee`'s note carries the new-fee/escrow consequence so it is not repeated here. Specialities is the candidate Skills section: `.skl-c.pfe-chip` pills with `.pfe-chip-x` remove and a trailing `.pfe-chip-add` "Add a speciality +" that opens an inline field — live over `S.spec`, Discard restoring the snapshot. `S.pfEdit` is the open section. A section whose in-place form has no `.skl`/`.facts` trigger carries `.sec-h-act` on its heading actions so §10.15 does not squeeze the heading into the label column. (Maryam 7 Sep 2026, agent portal)
- In the training black card the step names align to the stepper: the label row is the SAME equal-width flex as `.prog-seg` (`flex:1 1 0`, 2px gap), so each name shares its segment's left edge. (`readyBand`, Maryam 7 Sep 2026, agent portal)
- The training lesson list is part of "Your progress" (no separate "The four lessons" heading, no `tint` ground); a locked lesson's tag leads with the lock glyph and is a round bordered pill. (Maryam 7 Sep 2026, agent portal)
- The COHORT LEADER Messages page (`.msg-mod`) is FULL-BLEED: NO page side margins. `.main`'s `clamp(--s06,12cqw,200px)` centring gutter is zeroed while it holds the `.msg-mod` page, so the two-pane inbox (conversation list rail + thread) runs from the nav rail to the frame edge, not the ~900px reading column. Only the OUTER margin goes; the panes keep their `--pad-x` inner padding. hifi leader only (§36.msg). (Maryam 9 Sep 2026)
- A whole-page empty state drops the Tal summary band — pass `noSum` from a view whose body is only a `.sec-fill` (agent Earnings before a payout destination). A page that keeps a tab strip or other content is not empty and keeps its summary. (Maryam 7 Sep 2026)
- The RATING + REVIEW section (`reviewCard`, all three surfaces — course / cohort leader / talent agent) is a CENTRED WHITE BOX that hugs its content, with CENTRE-ALIGNED content: `.review` is a `max-width:700px` (`--rev-w`) `margin-inline:auto` panel on `--background` (WHITE — Maryam 14 Sep 2026, was grey `--surface-2` for two turns) inside a `--rule` #efefef hairline, `--s06` inner inset, radius 0; the wrapping `.sec` is plain (not `tint`) so it carries the section gap above and below. Floated (capsule mode) it also carries the soft `--rev-shadow` (a lightened three-layer lift). A gap sits between the heading and the stars when no sub-line is between them (`.rev-head + .rev-rate`). The note field has NO visible label (aria-label + placeholder only). A LIT star is the ACCENT (`--accent`) — a scoped exception to "the rating star is always gold" (§3): that governs the read-only `.stars` rating; this is the review widget's own interactive picker (`.rev-star.on`), and the global `.stars` still reads `--star`. The textarea's FOCUS edge is a thin black `1px var(--text-primary)` (not the field default 2px accent), scoped `.app textarea.rev-ta:focus`. On SUBMIT the "Thanks for your review" state shows, then clears itself after ~3.5s (`setTimeout` → `r.done` → `reviewCard` returns nothing, so the whole float disappears; ai3.js); that thanks card is sized to its content (`.rev-float .review-done{width:auto}`), not the review measure. Centring: `text-align:center` on `.review`, `justify-content:center` on the head/stars/actions rows; the textarea keeps text left and fills the box. Below 650px it is full width. The card carries the heading + `.rev-head` mark (16px), the star row and the note only — NO "Rate your experience" label, NO character counter, and NO sub-line on the course surface (the `sub` param is empty; the other two surfaces may still pass one). The course heading is "How would you rate this course?" (matching the capsule). Stars are 26px (thinner outline). Below 700px it is full width. This SUPERSEDES the earlier full-width grey band and the 13 Sep "out of box". On the Course Progress page (`V.transcript`) the rating is moved to the FOOT (after the chapter list), day90 only; the other two surfaces rate in place. (§130 + views.js, Maryam 14 Sep 2026)
- On the Course Progress foot the rating opens COLLAPSED as a CAPSULE (Maryam 14 Sep 2026, with a reference): `reviewCard` takes a `capsule` label and, while `!r.open` and unsent, draws a centred pill (`.rev-cap`, radius 999px, WHITE ground `--background` + `--rule` hairline + the soft `--rev-shadow`, the `chat` mark + the label in `t-body`) sitting above the Tal dock; text "How would you rate this course?". Clicking it (`data-revopen` → `r.open`) replaces the pill IN PLACE with the full card, which then carries a top-right close X (`.rev-x`, `data-revclose`) that collapses back to the pill. `r.open` is a pure toggle (score/note preserved). ALL THREE surfaces pass `capsule` now (Maryam 14 Sep 2026): course "How would you rate this course?"; cohort (day90) "How was your experience with {leader first name}?"; interview report "Rate your interview with {agent first name}". None passes a `sub`. Handlers in ai3.js. (§130 + views.js + ai3.js, Maryam 14 Sep 2026)
- The capsule (and its expanded card) FLOAT PINNED above the Tal dock (Maryam 14 Sep 2026: "the capsule should be fix above the tal floating field with some gap"). `reviewCard` renders `.rev-float` and ai4.js `placeReviewFloat` lifts it into `.view-col` (the dock's own container — a fixed pin would centre over the whole frame including the rail, so it must share the dock's containing block); §130 pins it `position:absolute; bottom:calc(--s05 + 66px + --s04)` (one dock-height + the dock's floor offset + a gap) with the dock's exact width/centring box. On a page with NO dock (the cohort page), `placeReviewFloat` stamps `.rev-float-nodock` and it drops to `bottom:--s06` (Maryam: "since we do not have tal chat here ... the capsule will come little down"). The cohort page summary also switches at day90 from the Thursday-call line to a course-wrapped line (`PAGESUM.cohort` is a function of the stage, ai6.js). The capsule<->modal swap ANIMATES (`revMorphIn` keyframe, `.rev-anim`): render() rebuilds the DOM so the two cannot tween, so the element that appears on the toggle render plays a one-shot fade+rise+scale entrance; `.rev-anim` is stamped only on that render via the `S.revMorph` one-render marker (ai3.js), never on unrelated re-renders. The capsule ICON is accent ink (`--accent-text` fill, §130) and SIZED TO THE TEXT (14px, was 20px); the LABEL is accent ink too (`.rev-cap-t` → `--accent-text` in §63, since §63 owns text colour). (§130 + §63 + ai4.js, Maryam 14 Sep 2026)
- THE CANDIDATE RATES THE TALENT AGENT DURING THE HELD STAGE, NOT AFTER LEVELLING (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "the candidate could rate the talent agent before they got levelled ... add the rating above tal floating field in the whole prototype of interview held report pending, like on its all modules ... do not show the talent agent rating option if the candidate got levelled"). The "Rate your interview with {agent first name}" capsule (`reviewCard({key:'agent', capsule})`) is REMOVED from the levelled report (`V.report`, views.js) and instead floats above the Tal dock on EVERY page of the `held` stage (interview held, report pending). Because no held-stage view carries it, `placeReviewFloat` (ai4.js) INJECTS it — when `S.stage==='held'`, `reviewCard` returns non-empty (not yet rated) and the page rendered no `.rev-float` of its own, it appends the capsule into `.main` and then lifts it into `.view-col` above the dock like any other float. It is gone once a level is set (every levelled stage renders no rating). This SUPERSEDES the 13 Sep "put at the end of the report" placement for the agent rating. hifi candidate/leader (ai4.js + views.js `V.report`, Maryam 30 Sep 2026).
- Data over decorative charts: `.kv` rows and tables unless a proportion needs a chart. Admin-configurable numbers stay out of copy. (memory)
- A DASHBOARD is the "unless a proportion needs a chart" case: a split or a funnel reads faster as a shape. Two chart shapes, both admin (§120.1f) — `donut(segs)` for a 2–4 slice split (arc as a stroke-dasharray length, total in the hole, figures in the legend) and `hbars(rows)` for a ranked list or a funnel (`[label, valueText, barValue]`). Both draw from the `--dv-*` data-viz ramp — a green scale, NEVER the brand red (categorical data-viz never rides the brand colour) — and keep the figure in the legend / bar label, so the graph adds the shape without losing the number. Money lists (Revenue by line, Payments) stay `.kv`; wide stat rows and tables stay full-width. (§120.1f, Maryam 8 Sep 2026)
- A booking/interview card shows the interview FEE only (`v.fee`), never the agent's split (`v.share` / "of it yours") — the split is admin-configurable and belongs on the Earnings module, the one page whose subject is the agent's take. (Maryam 6 Sep, agent portal)

Responsive and mobile
- THE MOBILE PAGE SIDE MARGIN IS 24px, ALL FOUR PORTALS (Maryam 4 Oct 2026: "set the page left and right margins on mobile app to 24px ... the whole mobile design, not only on candidate and cohort leader portals but also on talent agent portal"). `--pad-x`'s mobile/tablet value was raised **16 → 24** at `10-lattice.css:383` (the ladder is now 24 below 1200, 32 at ≥1200), so a phone page sits 24 off each wall. One DS token — candidate + cohort leader (hifi) and talent agent + super admin (DS-linked) all follow from the one rebuild; the NIL microsite's hardcoded `--s05` mobile wrap was moved to `--s06` (24) to match (`30-nil.css`). This is a bigger content column inset, not an overflow risk; sweep 390/744 for a fixed-px child in the now-narrower column (trap 15). See §2 Space. (§15/§10, Maryam 4 Oct 2026)
- MOBILE INTEGRITY IS A SHIP GATE, NOT A FOLLOW-UP (Maryam 1 Oct 2026, off a phone recording). A screen is not DONE until it is clean at **390 and 744**, on every portal, against the three failures the recording showed and that recur here: **(1) NO sideways scroll and no odd scroller.** The frame scrolls VERTICALLY only — nothing pans left-right, there is no horizontal overflow, and no inner `overflow:auto/scroll` region fights the page or double-scrolls inside it. The ONLY horizontal scroller allowed is a deliberate carousel (`.scv`/`.cov-row`), which must be a real `overflow-x` container the test walks up to (respcheck already does), never a row the whole viewport has to pan. **(2) NOTHING BREAKS THE FRAME.** Every box sits inside the device/`.app` content width — no component, control, card, image, chip, table, kv value or divider hangs past the right wall, bleeds off the edge, or is clipped by it (a `--pad-x` negative-margin bleed must still land inside the frame; a fixed-px child in a narrow column is the usual culprit, trap 15). **(3) NO BROKEN COMPOSITION.** No overlap or collision, no two-line button label (§56), no desktop two-column / side-by-side block left un-stacked, no `.sec-h` heading colliding with its own content (trap 13's label column live below 900), no absolutely-positioned divider still drawn after the columns stacked (§72.3 — `content:none`, not re-pointed). Authored at desktop, VERIFIED at mobile in the SAME task — the desktop pass does not close the ticket. (§7, traps 3/13/15, supersedes the thin "Responsive is part of done" line below by stating what "done" means)
- THE SWEEP THAT ENFORCES IT: `cd hifi && node respcheck.mjs` for the compiled candidate + leader portals (390/744/1024/1280; `--edge` adds the 900 breakpoint seam, `--quick` triages), and `/design-check <portal>` for the hand-written agent + admin portals. Run one before calling mobile done; its silence is the pass, a finding is the fix-list. Measure with the Browser pane OPEN and motion OFF — a hidden pane reports `window.innerWidth === 0` and entrance animations (trap 2) both read a clean layout as broken. (§7)

Data and copy
- Nothing is typed twice: read the record (`bkStamp`, `AGENTS[k].price`, `CH`, `PF`, `COHORT_LEAD`). (§76, `bkStamp`)
- No invented claims or data; authored placeholder copy is flagged in its source note (`CH_SYL`, `COURSE_NAME.E1`). (§74)
- A dead control on a live surface is worse than a missing one; a control that can never act is not drawn. (§60)
- Dates are `MM-DD-YYYY`; the course is `90 days`; the document is the `90-day summary`; a small count is spelt when it opens a sentence. (memory, ai6)

How to read an ask
- "Reuse X" = port X's declarations exactly and change only the dimension named; extras in the screenshot are the other surface's. (memory)
- A reference screenshot is structure inspiration, built in our components at the reference's proportions. A **third-party product** in a reference is embedded, never redrawn (Calendly). (§76)
- "This portal is a previous design" = port the current component generation wholesale (`/portal-sync`). (memory)
- Design arrives in corrections: act on the newest message, keep the old shape's argument in a note, do not ask which she meant. (memory)
- Artwork must be a file on disk; a pasted image cannot be embedded — name the folder and stems. (memory)
- **Responsive is part of done**: 390 / 744 / 1024 / 1280 in the same task. (memory 31 Aug)

Talent Agent — V1 model (client, 15 Sep 2026). Four rulings, each REVERSING an earlier one:
- **THE AGENT SENDS A RECOMMENDATION AGAIN — RECOMMENDATION, NOT RECORDING-ONLY (Maryam, 18 Sep 2026, reversing the 15 Sep client "recording only").** On the interview screen (`V.interview`, `st:'report'`, `tn-agent-portal.html`) the agent RECOMMENDS a level across the FIFTEEN rungs (E1–T5), with **strengths**, **growth areas** and an **overall read** ("what you make of {first}"). Tal's suggested level is marked on a read-only LEVEL BAR (the DS `.ladder` markup, rung computed off the code so B/T mark correctly — the DS `dsRungOf` only maps E1–E5), and the picker + the three fields PREFILL from Tal's read (`talLevel` / `seedStrength` / `seedGrowth` / `seedWhy`) so the agent edits rather than starts blank. The level picker is the shared `dd()` dropdown (`LVL_OPTS` = the 15 codes as "Explorer – E1"…"Trailblazer – T5"; store `S.slotForm.evalLevel`, no new handler). The page also carries an **attached interview-recording THUMBNAIL** (`.iv-rec`, a 16:9 card reusing §111's `.scv-play`/`.scv-at` marks; geometry-only CSS in §124, `recStill(x)` = the candidate's own photo as the frame stand-in until a real captured frame is dropped in — a pasted image can't be embedded, so the file goes on disk). The CTA is **"Send Recommendation"** (`data-confirm="sendrec"`); Tal's read stays but is reworded to "weigh it against the call and set your own recommendation below" (no longer "not a decision you make here"). **TalentNext still CONFIRMS the final level after analysis** — the agent recommends, TalentNext confirms — so the candidate-side "TalentNext sets the level" strings are untouched. This REVERSES the ruling below for this screen; the level picker / strengths / growth / overall are BACK on the agent portal. (Maryam, 18 Sep 2026)
  - **The agent interview screen follows the candidate portal's block structure, translated to LIGHT MODE (Maryam, 21 Sep 2026).** (1) "Tal's read" is a SECTION HEADING (`.sec-h > h2`) like "Your recommendation" below it — not the blob + `.ai-label.bare` mark — and its read is ONE flowing paragraph, not separate lines (`talEval` a single `<p>`; `.tal-eval` kept only so `.tal-eval .ai-body p b` still highlights the accent phrases). (2) The recommendation LADDER (`.iv-ladder`) gets §59.2b's light-ground treatment restated for it (`59-priority.css`, crosses to the DS): a `--layer-accent-01` track for rungs ahead, solid `--accent` for rungs passed, a light accent tint for the suggested one, and grid-centred UPRIGHT tabular codes — the DS default (`--on-dark-fill` track, the `<i>{font-style:italic}` trap) is drawn for the dark level card and left the future rungs invisible and the codes slanted on the white section. (3) The interview-recording THUMBNAIL is a real captured frame of the video call (`IV_REC_STILL`, a 16:9 WebP supplied by Maryam, embedded as a data URI; source at `assets/agent/iv-recording-still.webp`), so `recStill(x)` returns it for every recording preview instead of the candidate-photo stand-in. (tn-agent-portal.html + §59, Maryam 21 Sep 2026) (4) The **"Interview recording" and "Tal's read" sections STACK** — heading above, content full width (Maryam 22 Sep 2026): §10.15's label column keys its opt-out on the content class and `.iv-rec`/`.tal-eval` are not on that list, so an agent-portal-local `<style>` block restates the DS opt-out (`display:block` + the `> .sec-h` reset) for `.sec:has(> .iv-rec)` / `.sec:has(> .tal-eval)` inside the ≥900 tier — they now read like "The interview" (`.stats`) and "Your recommendation" (`.eval-form`), and the Tal's-read paragraph runs full width. (5) NO divider between "The interview" and "Interview recording" — that section carries `.sec-noline` (Maryam 22 Sep 2026). (tn-agent-portal.html, Maryam 22 Sep 2026) (6) **The T·A·L·E·N·T TYPE** (Maryam 22 Sep 2026) — six lenses spelling TALENT: **Top Performer · Athlete · Leader · Entrepreneur · Not Sure · Team Player**, each with Maryam's supplied meaning (`TALENT_TYPES`, kept verbatim, no em dashes). Tal names which one the candidate falls in on the read ("Tal reads {first} as a **{type}**. {meaning}", the type in Tal's accent ramp; `talTypeOf(x)` = the record's `talType` else a stable per-id pick, §74 — Sofia is `talType:'Leader'`), and **the agent picks their own on the recommendation** — a "TALENT type" `dd('evalType', TALENT_OPTS, curType, {desc: talentDesc})` under the level picker, prefilled to Tal's read, its store `S.slotForm.evalType` (reuses the `dd()` handler, no new one). Refinements 22 Sep 2026: Tal's read shows only "Tal reads {first} as a **{type}**." (the meaning is NOT repeated there); the `dd()` gained a `cfg.desc` option that stacks each option's **description under its label** in the menu (`.dd-opt-2`/`.dd-opt-main`/`.dd-opt-d`, agent-local CSS), so the meanings live in the dropdown — there is NO separate meaning line under the picker. (tn-agent-portal.html, Maryam 22 Sep 2026) (7) **The level ladder IS the picker** (Maryam 22 Sep 2026): the "Your suggested level" label sits ABOVE the blocks and the fifteen rungs are **clickable** (`ivLadder(curCode, true)` — each `<i>` carries `data-ddset="evalLevel:<name>"`, caught by the shared `dd` handler; `.ladder-pick i` gets cursor/hover/focus), marking the agent's own pick (`curCode` off `curLvl`), not Tal's. The old `dd('evalLevel', …)` dropdown is REMOVED. Unreached rungs are `--rule` #efefef, not `--layer-accent-01` (Maryam 22 Sep 2026). (8) The recommendation form **breathes** — `.eval-form > .f` fields are 20px apart with a label-to-control gap (Maryam 22 Sep 2026). (9) Tal's-read paragraph runs **full width** — the DS caps `.ai-body`/`.ai-body p` at 68ch and lifts only `.ai-aura`; an agent-local rule lifts `.tal-eval` too. (10) **In-place renders keep the page's scroll** — `render()` captures `.main` scrollTop and restores it (with `scroll-behavior:auto` so it is instant, no jump-to-top-and-back — `.main` is `scroll-behavior:smooth`); real navigation still resets via the `data-go` handler's `scrollTop=0` after render (Maryam 22 Sep 2026). (tn-agent-portal.html, Maryam 22 Sep 2026)
- ~~**THE AGENT DOES NOT SET THE LEVEL.**~~ **SUPERSEDED for the agent screen 18 Sep 2026 (above): the agent recommends a level + strengths/growth/overall again; TalentNext still confirms the final level.** (Original 15 Sep ruling, kept for its argument:) The agent's job is to show up, run the interview well, and deliver a clean recording. Tal PROPOSES the read; the final level is set admin/moderator-side after analysis (the Super Admin portal Maryam is building). On the agent portal the level picker, "Send Evaluation", and the Why-this-level / Strengths / Growth-areas decision fields are GONE; Tal's read shows READ-ONLY, framed "proposed — TalentNext sets the level after analysis". The agent's post-interview surface is: confirm the recording is clean, plus an optional free-text notes field that feeds the admin decision (never a level, never strengths/growth-as-decision). This REVERSES §3's "the talent agent's call on a candidate's level is FINAL" ruling (line ~181) — that ruling and the "Levelling module stays hidden" clause are superseded; the admin gains the level-setting surface. (client, 15 Sep 2026)
- **PAYOUT RELEASES ON A CONFIRMED CLEAN RECORDING, not on a level report.** With the agent no longer filing an evaluation, the escrow-release trigger is the interview being held + the recording confirmed clean; the three-working-day clock starts at interview end. Across Earnings / Dashboard / Standing the "Report due / blocks your payout" state becomes "recording to confirm" (then Releases). This REVERSES the "the report is what releases the money" rule. (client, 15 Sep 2026)
- **NO ACCEPT/DECLINE — A PAID BOOKING IS A COMMITMENT.** A candidate books an open slot the agent published and pays at that moment; there is no request for the agent to accept or decline. The agent controls supply by which slots they publish (Availability), not by vetting who books. The release valve is CANCEL-WITH-A-REASON (reason recorded, within-24h counted as a late cancellation and seen at review, candidate refunded and returned to browse) — story 13.5. Remove the "New Requests" tab and the accept/decline step; diary tabs are Upcoming / To confirm / Past. This REVERSES the 4 Sep "New Requests" tab. (client, 15 Sep 2026)
- **NO IN-PORTAL TRAINING — HIRING IS OFFLINE FOR V1.** Agent hiring, onboarding and training run through LightSpeed offline; the platform just creates the user with the agent role. Remove `V.training`, the four lessons, the calibration, the certification GATE/journey, and the Training rail item. Certification survives only as a READ-ONLY status on the profile (13.9: set outside the platform). The `setup` stage stays (profile / fee / payout gate, Not listed until done); the `training` stage is deleted. This REVERSES the training/enablement journey. (client, 15 Sep 2026)
- **THE PAYOUT DESTINATION IS ONE STRIPE REFERENCE, NOT A CARD WALLET (13.1).** Earnings shows a single destination as Stripe describes it (bank + last four) with its STATE and, when verified, the date; `S.payout.state` is one of none / pending / verified / failed (`DEST_STATE` maps each to a `.flag-t` hue + icon; `failed` shows Stripe's own reason via `payoutDest`). The action is **"Update payout destination"** (the shared Stripe modal); changing it returns the state to Pending while the agent stays listed and bookable. The old `S.payCards` card-list is gone (data kept, unused). (client, 15 Sep 2026, 13.1)
- **LISTING = DESTINATION + CERTIFICATION + PROFILE (13.1/13.9).** `listingLine(v)` states whether the agent is "Listed and bookable" or "Not listed", naming the missing piece (a verified payout destination, certification, or a written profile) — surfaced on Earnings under the destination and in the gate. Either missing is Not listed. (client, 15 Sep 2026)
- **THE LEDGER CARRIES SIX SETTLEMENT STATES (13.7):** In escrow, Recording to confirm (the one ON the agent), Releases [date], Paid [date], Returned, On hold (an admin hold, no reason shown to the agent). A **"What's holding up your pay"** panel lists every held interview whose recording is unconfirmed with a route to confirm. (client, 15 Sep 2026, 13.7)
- **THE FEE SHOWS ITS NET LIVE, AND A CHANGE CONFIRMS (13.9).** The Interview fee field carries a live "You keep $X of $Y. TalentNext keeps 20%." line (`feeNetText`/`feeNet`, inline `oninput`, no render so the caret holds). Saving the General form with a changed fee routes through the **`feechange` confirmation** ("interviews already in escrow keep the rate they were booked at; the new fee applies only to bookings made after this") whose OK carries `data-feedone` to clear both the confirm and the edit. (client, 15 Sep 2026, 13.9)

TALENT AGENT PORTAL — REFINEMENTS (Maryam, 2 Oct 2026, `tn-agent-portal.html`; the shared-DS items also build-ds):
- LIST-ROW LEADING MARKS ARE BARE ICONS, NOT BOXED CHIPS — portal-wide, `.cardrow-ic` sheds its 40px box / background / border, leaving the glyph ("remove this block of icons, only icons are enough"). Agent-local `.app .main .cardrow-ic` (0,3,0, beats the DS `:has(> .tile-stack)` rule by loading later). A row that WANTS a face uses `.mem-av` + `avatar` instead (see the Earnings holding list), so this only touches rows that keep an icon.
- The RECOMMENDATION form (`.eval-form`, interview screen) sits every field **32px** apart (`> .f{margin-top:var(--s07)}`, was 20) — "all these sections should have 32px between them".
- SEND EVALUATION CLOSES THE PAGE — the `sendrec` confirm's OK carries `data-go="interviews"`, and the `d.go` router branch now clears `S.confirm`, so the dialog and the interview page both close and the agent lands back on Interviews ("on clicking send evaluation this page should be closed").
- ADD A SLOT COMMITS TO THE CALENDAR — "Save Slot" carries `data-saveslot`; the handler pushes the chosen weekday + start time into `SLOTS` on the week the calendar is showing (`weekDaysOf(S.calAnchor)`), skips a duplicate day+time, closes the sheet, re-renders — the new block appears ("when I add a slot and click add I should see it in the calendar"). Every other sheet is still a prototype that just closes.
- EARNINGS "What's holding up your pay" shows AT MOST TWO rows (`owed.slice(0,2)`); each leads with the CANDIDATE'S PHOTO (`avatar`, not the warning icon); the row action is an ACCENT LINK "Evaluate {first} →" (`.acc-link`, no box, label + arrow `--accent-text`), not the black `Confirm recording` button.
- EARNINGS "Where you get paid" (`payoutDest`): the "Shown as Stripe describes it…" help paragraph and the green "Listed and bookable" line are REMOVED; the state TICK rides the top row next to the bank name (`.dest-tick`) and the sub-line drops its tick + state word, keeping only the date detail; the two rows sit 4px apart (`.dest-b`); "Update Payout Destination" is an ACCENT LINK with a trailing arrow (`.acc-link`).
- `.acc-link` is the agent-local no-box accent-link button (transparent, label + arrow `--accent-text`) — the Release-link pattern the admin keeps as `.acc-link`, added here for the Evaluate and Update-destination actions.

COHORT LEADER (hifi, Maryam 2 Oct 2026): the My Cohort black call card's static countdown default is **"In 02:40"** (HH:MM), matching the Sessions page — `lcalCard`'s `o.countdown || 'In 02:40'` (was "In 2hrs 40mins"). (lead.js)

TALENT AGENT PORTAL — EPIC 13 (stories 13.1–13.17), the agent-workspace stories, `tn-agent-portal.html` (Maryam, 30 Sep 2026). Built by TARGETED GAP-FILL within the EXISTING structure, not a rebuild — the portal already covers most surfaces, so keep the nav (Home / Interviews / Availability / Earnings / Standing + the four-tab Profile foot page: My Profile / Public Profile / Notifications / Privacy), and extend within it. Six gating answers Maryam gave up front: (1) **batched, all questions answered up front**; (2) **third parties stay screenshots** — Stripe payouts keep the shared `dsPayForm` picture, Auth0/LightspeedVT are simulated states; **NO Microsoft-calendar view** (a talent agent books nothing on their own end) — 13.9's whole Calendar-sync group + busy-block logic is DROPPED, only the custom calendar + open hours + slots stay; (3) **TALENT letters = 1–6 MULTI-select**, matching the admin levelling model, REVERSING the 22 Sep single-select TALENT-type on the agent eval; (4) **Agent Score = STARS OUT OF 5 everywhere** (rubric + candidate rating, the 13.15 labels), matching admin Epic 6 — treats 13.8's "82%/percentage" wording as superseded; (5) **US time zone**, adopting the stories' time-zone requirement (add a mandatory account time-zone field, label times with the US zone/offset e.g. "New York (UTC-04:00)") — REVERSES the 6 Sep "no timezone shown anywhere in the agent portal / times are bare" rule; (6) **Tal's evaluation read stays a SINGLE flowing paragraph** (not 13.13's four labelled parts), keeping the 21 Sep decision; **agent onboarding gate (13.1) is NOT built as a screen** — industries + intents are captured as editable fields on the Public Profile (13.16) and nowhere else. Per story:
  - **13.13 EVALUATION (built 30 Sep 2026).** The agent's post-call surface recommends a level + writes it up. TALENT letters are a **1–6 MULTI-select chip cloud** (`TALENT_LETTERS` keys `t1/a/l/e/n/t2`, display chars T·A·L·E·N·T from the six `TALENT_TYPES`; `lettersLabel` fixed-order, `talLettersOf` authored placeholder §74, `lettersPicker`, `data-ltrtoggle` toggle on `S.slotForm.evalLetters`, `.ltr-chip` round pills accent-when-on) — this REPLACES the single `dd('evalType')` picker. The 15-rung `iv-ladder` is the level picker (unchanged); Tal's suggested rung is marked "Tal recommends [level]" and Tal's letters "Tal recommends [letters]" beside each. DIVERGENCE (13.13): when the agent's rung ≠ Tal's rung a quiet `.eval-diverge` note names the distance + direction ("N rungs higher/lower … recorded for the reviewer") and a **"Why not [Tal's level]"** field appears (conditional, mandatory then); a letters-only difference is NOT a divergence. Fields, in order: level ladder → TALENT letters → Why this level (was "Overall", seeds Tal's read) → Why not [Tal's level] (conditional) → Strengths → Growth areas → Anything else (optional). Actions: **Save Draft** (`data-confirm="savedraft"`, an info `.conf` — a draft keeps it in Evaluations, payout blocked) + **Send Evaluation** (`sendrec`, copy reworded to 13.13: "…go to a reviewer, who has 24 hours to approve … you cannot change them after sending"). Tal's read stays ONE paragraph. The eval form is a documented non-persisting prototype, so the letters toggle re-renders like the dd fields. (tn-agent-portal.html, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
  - **13.15 VIEW MY STANDING (built 30 Sep 2026).** `V.standing` rebuilt onto the **Agent Score = stars out of 5** model (Epic 6 / gating 4), replacing the earlier Upwork "My stats" placeholder (a **percentage** ring computed off follow-up/depth/rating, a verbal band, an eight-week **profile-views trend** `STAND_TREND`, and a five-step views→signed funnel — all REMOVED, along with the "Also held" certification card, which is out of 13.15 and moved to My Profile per 13.16). Kept as the two-column card masonry Maryam chose; the cards changed, not the layout. New model on the agent record (`A` + `CFG.reviewed`, all §74 authored placeholder — no rubric/view/cancellation telemetry in the seed): `scoreApproved`/`startingScore`/`approvedScore`, `rubric`, `ratingCount`, `scoreAt`, `reviewNext`, `quarterEnd`, `ivsQ`, `signedPct`, `cancels`/`lateCancels`; `follow`/`depth` are the reviewer's PER-INTERVIEW marks (not score inputs). Cards: **Agent Score** — a gold `I.star` + the one-decimal `approvedScore` (no ring, no verbal label), "Last calculated [date]. Recalculated each quarter and approved by a reviewer.", components **Interview score N/5** + **Candidate rating N/5 · [count] ratings** (the rubric share is LABELLED "Interview score" to the agent, never "Rubric" — the rubric is the reviewer's internal instrument and the story's literal "Rubric" label is jargon; the data key stays `rubric`, matching admin Epic 6 — Maryam 30 Sep 2026), and the "TalentNext computes a proposed score from your interview scores and your candidate rating … a staff member approves or adjusts it" line; the **starting-score branch** (`scoreApproved===false`) reads "Starting score, set at invite. Your first calculated score is due [quarterEnd]." with components hidden. **Your review** — Reviewed by [name] with **NO role title** ("Not yet reviewed" before the first approval), cadence quarterly, last review = date + approved score, next review date, what it can move. **Cancellations** (13.12) — cancellations + late cancellations for the review period, "they do not change your Agent Score." **This quarter** — interviews held, position in browse n of m, reports signed on time % against the 24h report deadline; the quarter stated. **In browse** — n of m + "browse is ordered by your Agent Score … restated at the weekly recalculation and holds between." **Last five interviews** — follow-up/depth read **"Awaiting review"** (a colspan) until the quarter is reviewed (`revBool`, so the `working` stage shows Awaiting and `reviewed` shows the marks); the candidate rating always shows. Read-only. The `PAGESUM.standing` summary and the dashboard/standing `TALQ` chat answers were reworded off follow-up/depth onto the Agent Score. Every kv VALUE is RIGHT-ALIGNED to the edge (Maryam 30 Sep 2026: "the right side content should be right aligned, in all blocks") — a `kv` shadowed inside `V.standing` adds `text-align:right` on the `.v`, keeping the DS `.kv` grid so the row hairlines stay and the two-column grid holds on mobile (no stacking). **LAYOUT REVISED the same day (Maryam 30 Sep 2026, with a reference): the whole card masonry is REPLACED by an 8-CELL `.facts pf-facts` grid** (§105.1a pins it to a fixed 4 columns ≥900, so eight cells read as two attached rows of four, 2-up on a phone; `pfFact` cells, marks inked #111 per §29): **Your score** (`approvedScore` + gold star) · **Your rank** (`#rank`) · **Interview score** (rubric N/5, "—" when unapproved) · **Candidate rating** (N/5) · **Interviews held** (`ivsQ`) · **Position in browse** (n of m) · **Cancelled interviews** (`cancels`) · **Late cancellations** (`lateCancels`). Under the grid, one `.t-desc` line carries the two story-required statements (how the score is arrived at + browse is ordered by it, recalculated weekly; the starting-score copy when unapproved). **Your review is now a PLAIN section, NOT a card** — a `.sec-h` heading over the full-width right-aligned kv (reviewer name/cadence/last/next/what-it-moves). The **Last five interviews** table and the **"Your range decides who can book you"** note are REMOVED (`LAST_FIVE` deleted). NOTE: the story's **"Reports signed on time"** funnel % is no longer surfaced — the 8-cell list Maryam specified omits it (a known divergence from 13.15, flag; `signedPct` stays on the record). REFINED the same day (Maryam 30 Sep 2026): the explanatory `.t-desc` statement line under the grid and the **"What it can move"** review row are BOTH REMOVED (so the two story-required statements — how the score is computed, browse ordering — are no longer stated on the page either, a further divergence; the data supports them if re-added), and the **"Reviewed by" value carries the reviewer's PHOTO** — a round 28px `avatar('NR', AV.hana, 28)` disc + the name, right-aligned in the value cell. (tn-agent-portal.html, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)
  - **13.1 / 13.16 INDUSTRIES + INTENTS + TRAINING (built 30 Sep 2026).** Since the agent-onboarding gate is not a screen, the two mandatory onboarding signals (Industries + Intents) live on the **Profile** module: EDITABLE on My Profile as always-on chip clouds (`multiPicker` reusing the `.ltr-chip` skin, `data-multi="pubInd|pubInt:value"` toggling `S.pubInd`/`S.pubInt`, lazily seeded from `PUB.industries`/`PUB.intents`, min-one guard), and READ chip clouds on the Public Profile tab (candidate-facing). `INDUSTRIES` is the SAME list candidates choose from (the hifi `OB_Q` set); `INTENTS` is the three onboarding intents. Neither restricts anything — Tal's three match signals in priority order are level, then intent, then industry. My Profile also shows a read-only **Training module** status (13.16): "Complete on [date]. Completion does not expire." (green `checkOutline`) when certified, else "Not complete" + an Open-training LightspeedVT link (new tab) and a "listing stays blocked until … complete or a Super Admin overrides" note. Authored placeholder §74 (`PUB.industries`/`intents`/`trainingDate`). (tn-agent-portal.html, Maryam 30 Sep 2026)

Candidate portal — story-gap builds (Maryam, 15 Sep 2026, from the candidate user-stories gap analysis):
- **There is a `held` stage between `booked` and `assessed`** — interview held, report pending (story 17.1). Leaving the level interview call (`ai10 callLeave`, `kind==='iv'` from `booked`) advances to it, not back to the booked page. It carries `held:true`, `nav:'early'`, no level; the dashboard, Interviews and My Level all say the report is on its way and there is NO route to the agent. Needs `PAGESUM.dashboard.held`, `NOTIF.held`, a `journey()` case (interview step still `on`). Scoped to the first interview; the re-interview keeps its path to `promoted`. (data.js/ai10.js/ai6.js/views.js)
- **Cancel an interview with a reason** (17.4/16.4) — a "Cancel interview" ghost under the booked scheduled card opens `ivCancelModal()`: a `.conf` dialog with our `dd()` reason dropdown (Something came up / different agent / not ready / Other, note on Other), then confirm drops the booking and `setStage('new')` (back to the marketplace). Reason in `S.ddVal.ivcancel`, note in `S.ivCancelNote`, open flag `S.ivCancel`; `.dd-open` on the body so the menu is not clipped (§123.4). (views.js)
- **A receipt is a MODAL, never a route** (22.1) — the Payments row's "Receipt" carries `data-receipt="<i>"`; `receiptModal()` draws that row (payee/item, date, card, amount, Paid) over the page. `payRows(f)` is the one ledger source both the table and the modal read. A complimentary interview shows an empty card cell and amount "Complimentary". (views.js)
- **The legal notices are one shared `legalDoc()`** with four working tabs (Terms/Privacy/Data use/Cookies, `S.legalTab`, `data-legaltab`), drawn on the pre-auth `AUTH.terms` screen AND — for a signed-in candidate — in the **Privacy Settings tab** (`pfPrivacy`), which is where they live for signed-in users. The Data use notice promises ONLY controls that exist: the removed Profile controls (pause Tal, level review, download everything, self-serve delete-a-recording) are gone from the notice too, and the agent's level is stated as final. (views.js)
- **"What to talk about" is captured at booking** (16.3) — a textarea on `V.agent` (`data-ivtopic` → `S.ivTopic`, persisted by the input listener), echoed on the scheduled and held cards via `ivTopicBlock()`. (views.js)
- **`howItWorks()` is one helper on BOTH interview surfaces** (16.2) — the four-figure/four-step `.found` disclosure (`key='how'`) is drawn on `V.interviews` and `V.agents`. (views.js)
- **Notification settings state each toggle's timing plus an "Always on" block** (22.4) — under the three email toggles, a read-only list of the service messages that cannot be switched off (payments, level, booking, cohort) with the reason. (views.js `pfNotif`)
- **First interview is COMPLIMENTARY via a config default, not a hardcoded free branch** (16.3, client 15 Sep 2026: "keep the first interview complimentary for now ... charge on the re-interview ... keep the ability to charge for the first interview later"). `IV_FIRST_FREE` (default true) + `ivCharged(isRe)` / `ivFeeLabel(isRe, price)` in data.js; `isRe = !!cfg(S.stage).reinterview`. `talRec`, `V.agent`, `V.checkout` and `V.billing` all read it — first interview shows "Complimentary" and asks for no card; the re-interview charges the agent's fee. Flip the flag to charge first interviews again with no other edit. CONFIRMED by Maryam 15 Sep 2026 (this supersedes the earlier "no interview is free"): client feedback is the governing input over the user stories and the portal's current flow. (data.js/views.js)
- **THE CANDIDATE PORTAL CREDITS TALENTNEXT, NOT THE AGENT, WITH SETTING THE LEVEL** (client Talent-Agent V1 ruling, applied candidate-side; Maryam 15 Sep 2026: client feedback governs over the user stories, which say the agent sets and signs it). The agent INTERVIEWS the candidate; **TalentNext sets the level from that interview, after analysing it, and the decision is final** (no review, no appeal). Every candidate-facing level-attribution string is reframed off the agent: the Data use notice (short version + clause 3) and My Level's "Who decides"; the provenance line ("Confirmed 21 August, set by TalentNext" — was "signed by Priya Nair") in `lvlWing`; the journey rows ("set by TalentNext"); the `held`/assessed dashboards + `PAGESUM`; `signedSummary`'s header ("Interviewed by Priya Nair" — was "Assessed and signed by"); `howItWorks` step 3; the `SUMDROP` level lead and the `MEMO` growth-areas source. Priya stays named as the INTERVIEWER (booking, the call, "your interview with Priya"); only who SETS/SIGNS the level changed. Course-completion CERTIFICATES (LightSpeed-issued) keep their own signature and are out of this scope. (views.js/ai6.js/ai2.js)

Candidate portal — stories 15.1–15.6 (Maryam, 30 Sep 2026). Gap-fill within the hifi build, story by story; the existing dashboards, quick actions and the one-line achievement link (`achLine`) are KEPT (Maryam's call — the story's "no quick actions" and "stacking celebration banners" were NOT adopted). The top-bar track/level chip stays REMOVED (Maryam kept the 16 Sep removal — 15.1's TALENT-letter chip is not built, so no candidate `letters` field either).
- **THREE NEW STAGES, in the stage menu (data.js `STAGES`/`CFG`/`NAVSETS`/`NOTIF`, ai6 `PAGESUM.dashboard`, `journey()`).** `enrolPre` (Enrolled, cohort not started) — `enrolled:true preStart:true startIn:6`, nav `enrolPre` = Dashboard, My Level, Interviews, Payments, Cohort, Messages (no coursework yet — it opens on the start day); the dashboard is the pre-start block "Your cohort starts in n days" (`preStartBlock`, course/start/leader + Meet your cohort → roster). **Paying for a course now lands on `enrolPre`, not `week1`** (the `data-paid` handler + the `enrolSheet` gate); the reader picks `week1` from the menu. `cancelled` (Cohort cancelled → moved to a replacement) and `rebook` (interview could not take place, free rebooking) are MENU-ONLY (reached from nowhere in-app); `cancelled` reuses the pre-start block after the cancellation summary, `rebook` shows "Book another interview" + the credit/time-left + a marketplace CTA, `early` nav. `journey()` cases: enrolPre/cancelled = step 4 current (Step 4 of 4), rebook = interview step current (Step 2 of 4). (15.1/15.2/7.6/8.3)
- **`day90` nav drops Coursework** (`full90` = `full` minus Coursework; 15.1 — Coursework goes at day 90 under 19.1, Cohort/Messages/Progress/Achievements stay). **The cohort-closed nav (`next`) gains Messages** and its enrol item reads "Course Enrollment", not "Next course" (the same item for the life of the account, 15.1).
- **The account menu is My profile / Profile settings / role switches / Sign out** (`acctMenu`, 15.1). My profile → `account` tab `me`, Profile settings → `account` tab `priv` (both via `data-pftab`, read by the `[data-go]` branch). Sign out mirrors the rail's `stage:signup/login`.
- **Notifications — two kinds + dismissal (15.6, kept in the right slider).** A `kind:'cond'` item (interview confirmed, report pending, a cohort/consultant call coming up, rebooking credit active) is CONDITION-BASED: it carries NO dismiss cross and self-clears; everything else is INFORMATIONAL and carries a dismiss cross (`.nrow-x`, a `<span role=button>` inside the row button answered by `data-dismiss` BEFORE the row's `data-go`). `S.dismissed` holds dismissed info titles (they leave the panel and stop counting); the header gains **Dismiss all read** (`data-dismissread`, clears every read info item) beside Mark all read. `.nrow-x` is armed in `HOVER_KEEP`. `notifShown`/`unreadCount`/`notifReadInfo` all exclude dismissed. Crosses to the DS; only the candidate/leader panels emit `.nrow-x`. (§15/views.js/data.js)
- **Ask Tal — daily question limit + placeholder (15.5).** `TAL_LIMIT` = 25 (Settings 11.1); `S.talAsked` counts (a chip counts too), enforced at the ONE submit path `ask()` (`talAtLimit()` guard). At the limit the ask-page and `talPanel` composers disable, the chips give way to the limit line "You have asked Tal everything it can take today. Come back tomorrow.", and `ask()` refuses. Prototype-simulated (one session's count, no real midnight/timezone reset); onboarding (ob.js) is outside the limit. The composer placeholder is the fixed **"Ask Tal anything"**. (data.js/views.js/ai4.js, §51/§27)
- **Quiz Results is LEAN (15.3).** `V.result` shows only the TRACK and one line that the level is set at the interview — the score, taken-date, rose chart, strengths/growth and the "where the course picks this up" band are GONE; `PAGESUM.result` is scoreless too. Read-only (the one control is navigation). `quizRose`/`qzLow`/`qzChapter`/`qzTaken`/`QZ_STR`/`QZ_DEV` and the `.qz-*` chart layer are now orphaned, kept-not-deleted (one-page revert). Reached only from the dashboard's Open Quiz Results + Tal links, no nav entry.
- **The held result promise is 24 HOURS** (Maryam, 1 Oct 2026: "it should be 24 hours timer only", reversing the 30 Sep story-15.2 48h = report-deadline + reviewer-window sum). ONE number on every candidate held surface — the subtitle, the `held` summary body, the `held` notif and the black card's countdown all read 24h. `heldTill` = 24h; `heldOverdue()` swaps the card's countdown and `PAGESUM.dashboard.held` to "taking a little longer than usual" once passed (never in a session). The admin-side report/approval deadlines are their own constants, untouched. (views.js/ai6.js/data.js)

## 4. Component recipe index — before inventing a class, open the gallery

`design-system/gallery.html` renders every component with its markup. The CSS is half of each;
the other half is below.

| Component | Gallery | The other half |
|---|---|---|
| Head band, Tal summary, journey `.jrn` | `#signature` | `placeBand` / `placePageSummary` in `hifi/`; a hand page emits `.modhead` itself; copy is `PAGESUM` |
| Typing summary | `#signature` (replay) | `dsTypeSummary(p, key)` in `talentnext-ds.js` |
| `aiHead` | `#signature` | `aiHead({mark, title, desc, act, extra})` — `.aih-mk` is NOT `.ai-label` |
| Black card `.dark-card` (+ `.crow-dark`, `.rec-dark`) | `#signature` | `.dc-hd > .dc-hd-r > .dc-t` + ONE of `.dc-act` / `.dc-when`; §63 §6a inks by token |
| Call row `crow(kind, o)` | `#rows` | record fields `img`/`cover`/`v`/`xl`; `o.gate`; `data-call`; `joinLive` |
| `.stat` figure cell, `.qa-c` quick action | `#figures` | `.stats` takes exactly four; `--mk` inline per cell |
| `.stps` steps row / `.stp-all` popup | `#signature` | `.stp-all` INSIDE `.stp-top`; words in `.stp-t-l`; `stepIcon` table copied per portal |
| Disclosure `.found` | `#signature` | `foundHead(title, key)`; `S.disc[key]` + DOM class |
| Accordion `.acc`, course outline | `#rows` | `S.outl`; the `data-outl` branch runs BEFORE the generic `.acc-h` one |
| `.plate` + quiet state | `#signature` | `data-when` attribute; `dsPlateQuiet(el)`; `.plate-h`/`.plate-when` emitted by hand |
| `.ring` | `#figures` | two SVG circles; `--arc` is a dasharray length `163.36 × pct/100` |
| Agents table `.agt` | `#tables` | rows are `subgrid`; `--agt-act-w` reserves the hover |
| Confirmation dialog `.conf` | `#signature` | `.modal > .sheet.conf`; `.conf-ok` for success |
| Account menu `.acct-t` | `#nav` | trigger is `dsAcctTrigger(img, ini, name, role, open)` (face + name/role + chevron); `acctMenu()`; `data-swap` (repaint) vs `data-doc`/`data-portal` (load) |
| `.row-cta`, `.rec-tag`, `.tbl-flag` chip | `#rows`, `#chips` | `<span>` never `<button>`; hue written inline as `--mk` |
| Cohort cover `.gcard-art` | `#rows` | `gcard(..., {src, i})`; label is the `onerror` fallback |
| Interview scenes carousel `.scv-row` / `.scv` | `#scenes` | **`dsScenes(title, scenes, opts)`** in the DS — each scene `{img, i, name, at, title}`, `opts.role` (attribution label) + `opts.foot`; chevrons `.scv-nav > .scv-ch[data-scv]` scroll the row via **`dsScvScroll(this)`** (pure DOM, no render); card = `.scv-art` (img + `.scv-play` + `.scv-at`) → `.scv-b > .scv-h` → `.eo-lead` attribution. Candidate's `pfScenes()` is the caller. Layer §111 (in the DS) |
| Rail tree `.sn-subs` / `.sn-sub` | `#nav` | emitted only while `S.nav`; module row keeps the bar, sub-row keeps the accent ink |
| Sortable table `th[data-sort]`, action cell `td.tbl-act` | `#tables` | `table(cols, rows)` in the admin; `S.sort[page]` asc → desc → off; buttons sit nearer than `tr.clk` |
| Row action menu `.rowmenu` / `.rowmenu-list` (admin) | — | `rowActs(acts)` emits one kebab + a menu of `[icon, label, attr]` rows; `S.rowMenu` the open id; `placeRowMenu` lifts it into `.main` and positions it; the "Actions" column is `ACT_COL`; layer §125 (in the DS) |
| Row-select checkbox `.tbl-cb` (admin) | — | `selBtn(attr, on, ind, label)` — a `role="checkbox"` button, NOT a native input; `table()` prepends a `.tbl-sel` header + per-row cell; `S.sel[key]` holds the picks, `TBL_ROWKEYS[key]` the ordered keys the select-all reads; `data-sel`/`data-selall` matched before the row's `data-go`; CSS `#admin-tbl-sel`, admin-local |
| Bulk action bar `.actbar` (admin) | — | `bulkBar()` replaces `askDock()` while a `selectKind` table has a selection; `BULK[kind].single(id)`/`.multi(ids)` give the buttons; `bulkCtx()` finds the active table via `SEL_TABLES`; `bulkstatus`/`bulkdel` handlers confirm then apply to each; `data-selclearall` clears; content-width pill in `.askdock` with the dock shadow, admin-local |
| Table pagination `.pgn` (admin) | — | `table()` slices to `S.pg[key]` (page + size, key = `pageKey()#TBL_SEQ`); `pagination()` draws the bar; `data-pg`/`data-pgsize`; controls are `.btn`; layer §127 (in the DS) |
| Filter strip `.cs.lst-filt` under `.hd-srch` | `#tables` | counts in `.lf-n`; `filterStrip(name, def, opts)` |
| Record engine (admin) | — | `KINDS[kind]` = fields + create/update/remove; `rec:<kind>:<id>` sheet, `save:` / `askdel:` / `askstatus:` actions |
| Interview request card `.rqc` (agent) | — | `reqRow`; three zones (who / when+quote / decision) in a `.rqc-list`; desktop grid split by `--rule`, stacks below 900; the quote wears the Tal star + `--accent-text` ink (§63 §7), Decline is `.danger` red (border off), Accept is `.btn-p.ok` green with a tick; `.rqc-list` is a §10.15 label-column opt-out; layer §122 (in the DS) |
| Custom dropdown `.dd` / time range `.tm-row` (agent) | — | `dd(key, opts, val, {ic})`; `S.dd` is the open key, `S.slotForm` the values; `data-ddtoggle` / `data-ddset` (split on FIRST `:`), outside-click closes; layer §123 (in the DS) |
| Stripe "Add Payment Method" modal `.pay-modal` | — | `payForm(tab, open)` (views.js) → `dsPayForm` in the DS; embeds `PAY_ART` (Card/US-bank); `data-paytab` toggles, `data-payclose`+backdrop close; per-portal open flag |
| Saved-cards picker `cardPicker()` | — | `S.cards`+`BMK`, default via `payWithIdx()`, `data-paypick`→`S.payWith`, "Add a card"→the modal; on `V.payment` and `V.checkout` |
| Rulings Register (admin) | — | `SA_CASES` + `DECISIONS` (`sa`, `kind`, `blocks`); `decisionTile`, `homeRulings`, `SHEETS.sacase`; `saTag`. `ruling()` is a no-op — module pages no longer show inline notes (7 Sep) |
| View-as bar `.viewas` (admin) | — | `viewAsBar()` in `.view-col` before `.main`; `S.viewAs`; `.viewas-mk` 20px glyph, `.viewas-t` line (§63 §57), `.viewas-end`; layer §129 (in the DS) |
| Step-up confirmation | — | `askConfirm({stepup:true})` adds a `[data-f="code"]` field to `.conf`; the router refuses `data-do` without six digits while `SEC.stepup` |
| Sign-in scene (admin) | — | `authView()` → `.app > .auth-card > (brand, .auth-col > .shell + .main.form-page)`; `dsPh`, `.auth-id` panel; `S.auth` login → code |
| Voice recorder `.askfield.rec` / `.askrec` | — | ai4.js `askRecStart`; waveform from getUserMedia+AnalyserNode (synthetic when denied), transcript from Web Speech; `.rectx` transcribing; empty take → `askRecFallback()` suggested question; stop → `#askIn`, send → `ask()`, cancel discards; NOT_IN_DS |

Three that bite: `.ai-label` needs `.bare` outside the JS-assembled band; `.plate` takes
`data-when` as an attribute; a new page needs a host with `container-type:inline-size;
container-name:app` or no breakpoint fires.

## 5. Copy and voice

- A page head has two slots: `ph(title, sub)` is the factual spine as a `·` row (or `title` alone), `PAGESUM` is Tal's reading — two sentences, 18–28 words, real figures, `<b>` on the actionable phrase.
- The page-summary head band (`.ai-aura.talsum`) is labelled **"Summary by Tal"**, not "Tal" — platform-wide, all four portals. Only the head-of-page summary; every other Tal card (chat author, offer/advice cards, `V.interview`'s "Tal's draft") keeps the bare name "Tal". In `hifi/` `placePageSummary` sets it (`ai6.js`); the hand-written agent/admin portals write it into their `head()`/`detailHead()` `.talsum` band directly. (Maryam 6 Sep 2026)
- NO EM DASHES in anything Tal says — summaries, chat answers, drafts, all four portals: they read AI-generated.
